When the distance-growth parameter in the noise model vanishes, our guarantees recover the standard bounded-variance rates: $O(\varepsilon^{-4})$ for momentum, $O(\varepsilon^{-3})$ for variance reduction, and $O(\varepsilon^{-2})$ in the deterministic case. To our knowledge, these are the first convergence guarantees for normalized methods in non-convex stochastic optimization under $\mathsf{BG}$-0 noise without bounded domains, increasing batch sizes, or explicit anchoring, covering both standard and generalized smoothness regimes.

</details>


### [73] [Bounded-Rationality, Hedging, and Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15340)
*Pedro A. Ortega*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: A learner does not only fit data; it also determines how strongly the training sample may shape its output and how much distortion it can hedge. We study this relation as a bounded-rational decision problem whose primitive object is the induced channel from samples to outputs. The learner's response law determines which changes in this channel are cheap or costly, and therefore induces both a lower tradeoff curve between training loss and sample dependence and a matched upper certificate curve. When the response law is represented by an $f$-divergence regularizer, these curves live in the regularizer's native information geometry, with KL as the special case corresponding to Shannon mutual information. We show how the hedge and the two curves can be recovered from black-box behavior by observing responses to scaled losses and local loss perturbations. In learning, population loss is empirical loss plus the distortion induced by the particular training sample. The recovered hedge gives a practical certificate when it covers that distortion. Thus generalization is treated as a testable hedging property of the learner's own response law.

</details>


### [74] [LEAP: Trajectory-Level Evaluation of LLMs in Iterative Scientific Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15341)
*Marilyn Zhang,Tianfeng Chen,Fabián Barzuna,Ankita Rathod,Mark E. Whiting*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LEAPBench通过轨迹度量而非终点结果评估LLM在迭代科学设计中的学习效率，发现轨迹评分显著改变模型比较结论（53%任务最优模型易主），LLM整体不及贝叶斯优化基线，且反直觉地领域无关提示在生物设计任务中优于领域相关提示，同时AUC轨迹度量可作为有效训练目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前迭代科学设计基准仅在固定时间点评估最终结果，无法捕捉学习轨迹中的效率差异（如节省的迭代次数对应成本和时间节约）；LLM假设在自主实验室中能够利用领域先验和迭代反馈减少收敛所需迭代，但现有评估方式无法验证这一点。

Method: 提出了LEAPBench框架（55任务），采用"至今最佳面积曲线（best-so-far AUC）"轨迹度量替代固定终点快照评估；对比经典贝叶斯优化参考基线；基于已发表文献进行审计验证；对8个当代LLM进行评估实验，并探索将该指标作为离线强化学习奖励信号。

Result: 从终点结果切换到轨迹评分后，53%任务在匹配视域下的最优模型决策发生改变；LLM未能超越经典贝叶斯优化基线；在16个生物任务中，领域无关提示比领域相关提示在迭代30时更频繁匹配已发表最佳设计（约高10个百分点）；在6个文献典型配置与发表最佳配置存在分歧的任务中，领域无关提示在所有6个任务上都更常匹配发表最佳；AUC指标作为离线RL奖励可改善14/21保留任务的性能。

Conclusion: LLMs在迭代科学设计任务中无法超越经典贝叶斯优化基线；轨迹评分比最终结果评分更能揭示学习效率差异（53%任务改变最优模型决策）；在生物学任务中，领域无关提示反而优于领域相关提示（高出约10个百分点）；AUC轨迹度量可作为可处理的训练目标，离线强化学习利用该指标在21个保留任务中提升了14个的性能。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly deployed in autonomous laboratories, under the assumption that their domain priors and reasoning over iterative feedback let them converge on good designs in fewer iterations than feedback-only baselines. Current iterative scientific design benchmarks, however, score only outcome snapshots at fixed horizons. This leaves the learning trajectory unmeasured, even though the trajectory is what captures learning efficiency, where each iteration saved is a real saving in cost and time. Motivated by this, we examine three evaluation choices that change the conclusions one draws about LLM learning efficiency in iterative scientific design: what to measure, what baseline to compare against, and what to ground against. We introduce LEAPBench, Learning Efficiency in Adaptive Processes, a 55-task framework that pairs a best-so-far area under the curve (AUC) trajectory metric with a classical Bayesian-optimization reference and an audit grounded in published literature. Applied to eight contemporary LLMs, switching from final-outcome to trajectory scoring changes the best-model decision on 53% of tasks at matched horizons, and exposes efficiency gains overlooked by outcome-based scoring. LLMs do not outperform a classical Bayesian baseline. On 16 biology tasks where the oracle's reward signal is aligned with configurations from the published-best design, domain-aware prompting leads to LLM choices that match the published-best's approximately 10 percentage points less often than domain-agnostic prompting at iteration 30. The pattern is sharpest on 6 tasks where the literature-typical and published-best configurations diverge, and domain-agnostic prompting matches the published-best more often on all 6. The trajectory metric also doubles as a tractable training target. Offline reinforcement learning with the metric as a reward improves performance on 14 of 21 held-out tasks.

</details>


### [75] [PACER: Acyclic Causal Discovery from Large-Scale Interventional Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15353)
*Ramon Viñas Torné,Sílvia Fàbregas Salazar,Soyon Park,Ivo Alexander Ban,Artyom Gadetsky,Nikita Doikov,Maria Brbić*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Inferring the structure of directed acyclic graphs (DAGs) from data is a central challenge in causal discovery, particularly in modern high-dimensional settings where large-scale interventional data are increasingly available. While interventional data can improve identifiability, existing methods remain limited by soft acyclicity constraints, leading to optimization over invalid cyclic graphs, numerical instability, and reduced scalability. We introduce PACER (Perturbation-driven Acyclic Causal Edge Recovery), a scalable framework for causal discovery that guarantees acyclicity by construction. PACER parameterizes a distribution over DAGs through a joint model of variable permutations and edge probabilities, enabling direct optimization over valid causal structures without surrogate penalties. The framework supports a unified likelihood-based treatment of observational and interventional data, flexible conditional density models, and the incorporation of structural prior knowledge. For linear-Gaussian mechanisms, we derive closed-form expressions for the expected interventional log-likelihood and its gradients, yielding substantial computational gains. Empirically, PACER matches or exceeds state-of-the-art methods on protein signaling and large-scale genetic perturbation benchmarks, while scaling efficiently to networks with thousands of variables and achieving up to two orders of magnitude speedups over penalty-based differentiable approaches. These results demonstrate that exact and scalable causal discovery from high-dimensional perturbation data is achievable through principled search space design.

</details>


### [76] [Controllable Molecular Generative Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15354)
*Yihan Zhu,Yuhan Liu,Weijiang Li,Tengfei Luo,Meng Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CoMole是一个可控分子生成基础模型，融合motif-aware图扩散与强化学习，在保持高化学有效性的同时实现异构设计任务的可控生成，在多基准上取得最优性能并具备跨属性迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前分子图生成缺乏针对异构设计任务的统一框架和可靠可控性机制；强化学习虽适合任务特定优化，但直接应用于图生成模型面临原子级动作空间庞大和化学无效中间状态的瓶颈。

Method: 构建统一 motif-aware 图扩散管道，学习 motif-aware 图空间，将预训练的结构先验迁移至可控生成；在 motif-aware 动作空间上使用 RL 优化条件逆向策略，避免原子级大规模动作空间问题，保证化学有效性。

Result: 在三个异构基准测试（材料和药物发现）的九个可控性目标上均排名第一；MAE相对最强基线降低48.2%；有效性维持在0.94以上；可零样本迁移至未见属性，仅通过优化任务嵌入实现。

Conclusion: CoMole在三个异构基准测试中均排名第一，所有九个目标的可控性表现最优；相对最强基线MAE降低48.2%；有效性维持在0.94以上无需规则校正或后过滤；在生成器冻结情况下仅优化任务嵌入即可迁移可控性，性能与强任务特定基线相当。

Abstract: Despite the success of foundation models in language and vision, molecular graph generation still lacks a unified framework for heterogeneous design tasks with reliable controllability. While reinforcement learning (RL) offers a natural post-training mechanism for task-specific optimization, applying it to graph generative models is hindered by the vast atom-wise action spaces and chemically invalid intermediate states. We propose \textbf{Co}ntrollable \textbf{Mole}cular Generative Foundation Models (CoMole), built with a unified motif-aware graph diffusion pipeline. By learning a motif-aware graph space, CoMole transfers pretrained structural priors into controllable generation, where RL optimizes conditional reverse policies over chemically meaningful decisions. We theoretically characterize the bottleneck of atom-level RL and justify motif-aware policy optimization. Across three heterogeneous benchmarks spanning materials and drug discovery, CoMole ranks first in controllability on all nine targets, reduces MAE by up to 48.2% relative to the strongest baselines, and maintains validity above 0.94 without rule-based correction or post-hoc filtering. We further show that CoMole transfers controllability to unseen properties by optimizing only task embeddings with the generator frozen, achieving performance competitive with strong task-specific baselines.

</details>


### [77] [Is One Score Enough? Rethinking the Evaluation of Sequentially Evolving LLM Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15384)
*Songwei Dong,Zihan Chen,Chengshuai Shi,Peng Wang,Jundong Li,Cong Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SeqMem-Eval是一个用于评估大语言模型(LLM)顺序推理中记忆质量的诊断框架,解决了传统评估指标(最终准确率、累积性能)掩盖遗忘和负迁移等关键失败模式的问题。该框架从持续学习角度出发,关注外部的、提示介导的、可更新的记忆系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM记忆评估主要依赖聚合指标(如最终准确率或累积在线性能),这些指标可能掩盖关键失败模式,如遗忘(forgetting)和负迁移(negative transfer)。这导致无法真正评估不同记忆设计在适应性与稳定性之间的权衡,而这些权衡在标准评估指标下是不可见的。

Method: SeqMem-Eval框架在测试时设置下评估记忆,记忆是外部的、提示介导的,且更新时不修改模型参数。具体测量四个维度:在线效用(online utility)、留出泛化(hold-out generalization)、后向迁移(backward transfer)和遗忘(forgetting),从而提供更细粒度的记忆质量视图。实验涵盖多种任务和记忆方法。

Result: 实验表明,更高的最终准确率或累积准确率并不一定意味着更好的记忆质量:许多方法在获得强劲性能提升的同时,遭受着显著的遗忘或负迁移问题。

Conclusion: 不同记忆设计在适应性与稳定性之间展现出独特的权衡,而这些权衡在标准评估指标下是隐藏的。SeqMem-Eval能够揭示这些权衡,为理解和改进LLM记忆系统提供了更全面的诊断工具。

Abstract: Memory plays a central role in enabling large language models (LLMs) to operate over sequential tasks by accumulating and reusing experience over time. However, existing evaluations of LLM memory mostly rely on aggregate metrics such as final hold-out accuracy or cumulative online performance, which can obscure critical failure modes such as forgetting and negative transfer. In this paper, we introduce SeqMem-Eval, a diagnostic evaluation framework for sequentially evolving LLM memory. Drawing inspiration from continual learning, it targets a test-time setting in which memory is external, prompt-mediated, and updated without modifying model parameters. Rather than focusing only on final performance, SeqMem-Eval evaluates how memory states evolve, generalize, consolidate experience, and retain useful information during sequential inference. Specifically, it measures online utility, hold-out generalization, backward transfer, and forgetting, providing a finer-grained view of memory quality. Through extensive experiments across diverse tasks and memory methods, we show that higher final or cumulative accuracy does not necessarily imply better memory quality: many methods exhibit strong performance gains while suffering from substantial forgetting or negative transfer. Moreover, different memory designs exhibit distinct trade-offs between adaptability and stability that remain invisible under standard evaluation metrics.

</details>


### [78] [Unified High-Probability Analysis of Stochastic Variance-Reduced Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15388)
*Zhankun Luo,Antesh Upadhyay,M. Berk Sahin,Sang Bin Moon,Anuran Makur,Abolfazl Hashemi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文构建了一个统一的随机方差缩减估计框架，通过记忆保持、复位概率和修正项三组件的递归结构，整合了多种经典估计器并能生成新变体。基于新提出的向量值 Freedman 不等式，证明了维度无关的高概率误差界，适用于 Euclidean 和非 Euclidean（Banach 空间）设置。在应用层面，确立了镜像下降方法的最优置信水平依赖，并为期望约束随机优化带来首个 Õ(ε⁻³) 复杂度上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随机方差缩减估计器（如 momentum、SPIDER、STORM、PAGE）的分析高度依赖于具体估计器形式，仅提供期望值意义下的误差界，掩盖了决定估计可靠性的结构权衡，缺乏统一理论框架。

Method: 提出包含记忆保持、复位概率和迭代移动修正项三组件的递归框架；利用向量值 Freedman 不等式（随机向量鞅和）证明维度无关的高概率界；应用于 Banach 空间中的镜像下降方法。

Result: （1）统一框架可恢复多种经典估计器并激励新的二阶变体；（2）提供估计误差的偏差-方差分解；（3）获得镜像下降求解无约束优化问题在对数置信水平下的 Oracle 复杂度；（4）首次针对期望约束随机优化得到 Õ(ε⁻³) 的 Oracle 复杂度，较原有 Õ(ε⁻⁴) 有显著改善。

Conclusion: 建立了随机方差缩减估计的统一理论基础，证明了 Freedman 不等式在高概率分析中的适用性，并在镜像下降框架下实现了对数置信水平依赖，显著推进了约束随机优化的复杂度边界（从 Õ(ε⁻⁴) 降至 Õ(ε⁻³)）。

Abstract: Stochastic estimators are fundamental to large-scale optimization, where population quantities must be inferred from noisy oracle observations. Although influential methods such as momentum, SPIDER, STORM, and PAGE have been highly successful, their analyses are largely estimator-specific and expectation-based, obscuring the structural tradeoffs that determine reliability. In this paper, we develop a unified framework for stochastic variance-reduced estimation based on a recursion with three components: memory retention, reset probability, and a correction term for iterate movement. This framework recovers several classical estimators, motivates new second-order variants, and yields a bias-variance decomposition of estimation error. Our main result is a unified high-probability bound proved using a new dimension-free vector-valued Freedman inequality, valid for smooth normed spaces involving random sums of vector martingales. The result applies in both Euclidean and non-Euclidean settings, including the analysis of mirror-descent-based methods in Banach spaces. As applications, we obtain high-probability oracle complexities for unconstrained optimization with mirror descent, establishing the logarithmic dependence on the confidence level. We also derive the first $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\varepsilon^{-3})$ oracle-complexity bounds for stochastic optimization with expectation constraints, improving upon the existing $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\varepsilon^{-4})$ complexity by leveraging variance-reduced estimation for the first time in this setting.

</details>


### [79] [LPDS: Evaluating LLM Robustness Through Logic-Preserving Difficulty Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15393)
*Philipp Mondorf,Samuel J. Bell,Jesse Dodge,Dieuwke Hupkes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出逻辑保持难度扩展(LPDS)框架，系统性地识别大语言模型在逻辑保持的变体中最容易失败的场景。研究发现难度增加时模型性能下降，错误增多，且该框架找出的困难变体效果是随机采样的5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型在实体（如名称、数字等）变化但底层逻辑保持不变时容易失败。现有评估方法缺乏系统性识别最易导致失败的变体的手段，通常随机采样变体，可能高估模型的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出逻辑保持难度扩展(LPDS)框架：(i)量化问题变体的难度；(ii)在允许的变体空间中系统搜索最大化难度的变体。

Result: 随难度增加，性能下降且推理链错误更明显；LPDS比随机采样更高效地发现困难变体（性能下降效果提升5倍）；在更困难变体上微调能带来更一致的鲁棒性提升。

Conclusion: LPDS能有效识别LLM的薄弱环节，困难变体的训练价值更高。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed to perform tasks with minimal human oversight, it is crucial that these models operate robustly. In particular, a model that can solve a given problem should not fail simply because certain entities$\unicode{x2013}$such as names, numbers, or other contextual details$\unicode{x2013}$have changed while the underlying problem logic remains the same. Prior work suggests that current LLMs still struggle with this form of robustness: they often succeed on some variations of a problem but fail on others. However, existing evaluations often lack a systematic way to identify which logic-preserving variations are most likely to induce failure. Instead, they typically test a random subset of allowable variations, which can overstate robustness. To address this gap, we introduce logic-preserving difficulty scaling (LPDS), a framework that (i) quantifies the difficulty of a problem variation and (ii) systematically searches the space of allowable variations to find those that maximize difficulty and expose failures. We show that as difficulty increases, performance declines and errors in the models' reasoning chains become more pronounced. We further demonstrate that LPDS efficiently finds difficult problem variations for a model, resulting in performance drops up to 5 times larger compared to random sampling. Finally, we show that fine-tuning on more difficult variations leads to more consistent robustness gains than training on easier ones.

</details>


### [80] [Representation Without Reward: A JEPA Audit for LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15394)
*Biswa Sengupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究在 Llama-3.2-1B-Instruct 固定 LoRA 框架下，系统测试了 22 种 JEPA 相关辅助训练方法对自然语言到正则生成任务的影响，结果表明没有任何方法在家族误差校正后通过统计显著性检验，隐藏状态表示工程与解码任务准确率之间存在弱耦合，建议将 LLM 领域 JEPA 评估重新定位为"耦合问题"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA 原则要求模型通过预测潜在表示而非观测输出来学习更有用的抽象，但这一原则在自回归语言模型微调中的有效性尚未得到严格验证——隐藏状态几何结构不仅需要改善，还需要能够被语言模型头"解码"并提升下游任务指标。

Method: 在固定的 Llama-3.2-1B-Instruct LoRA 框架下，对二十二种训练时辅助方法进行系统评估，涵盖轨迹形状正则化、分布约束、预测器/目标不对称、Fisher 度量 Jacobi 残差以及位于交叉熵正锥内的 decoder-visible JEPA 目标函数；采用 Bonferroni 和 Holm-Bonferroni 校正进行家族误差控制，并进行全量微调复现实验（n=5 seeds）。

Result: 无任何辅助方法在家族误差校正后达到统计显著；T3-Local 在未校正时最强者（Δ=+2.53pp, p=0.003）；decoder-visible JEPA 首次产生正向辅助-交叉熵梯度余弦，但精确匹配仍在种子噪声内；全量微调复现确认双基准零结果（TURK: Δ=+0.04pp, p=0.96; SYNTH: Δ=+0.52pp, p=0.28）。

Conclusion: 在固定 LoRA 框架下的自然语言到正则表达式生成任务中，二十二种辅助训练方法均无法在 Bonferroni 或 Holm-Bonferroni 校正后通过统计显著性检验；即使 decoder-visible JEPA 目标函数产生了首个正向辅助-交叉熵梯度余弦，精确匹配性能仍落在种子噪声范围内；全量微调复现实验进一步确认了零结果的稳健性。作者由此将 LLM 领域 JEPA 评估重新定义为"耦合问题"，即在何种指标下有用的隐藏层几何结构能够转化为解码器可见的任务信号。

Abstract: Joint-embedding predictive architectures (JEPAs) propose that a model should learn more useful abstractions when trained to predict latent representations rather than observed outputs. For autoregressive language-model fine-tuning the principle entails a stricter requirement: the induced hidden-state geometry must reach the language-model head \emph{and} improve the decoded task metric. We test that requirement under a fixed Llama-3.2-1B-Instruct LoRA harness on natural-language-to-regex generation, comparing twenty-two training-time auxiliaries across trajectory-shape regularisation, distributional constraints, predictor/target asymmetry, Fisher-metric Jacobi residuals, and a decoder-visible JEPA objective constructed to lie in cross-entropy's positive cone. The empirical answer is a structured null: several auxiliaries clear single-cell paired $α= 0.10$ without correction (T3-Local at $Δ= +2.53$~pp, $p = 0.003$ being the strongest), but none survives Bonferroni or Holm--Bonferroni at the relevant family-wise threshold, even though many change curvature, anisotropy, variance, and gradient direction. Decoder-visible JEPA yields the first positive auxiliary--cross-entropy gradient cosine in the study, yet exact match remains inside seed noise; a full-fine-tuning replication of the same auxiliary at $n = 5$ seeds reproduces the null on both benchmarks (TURK: $Δ= +0.04$~pp, $p_{\text{paired}} = 0.96$; SYNTH: $Δ= +0.52$~pp, $p_{\text{paired}} = 0.28$), so the null is robust across LoRA and full fine-tuning for the decoder-visible construction. Hidden-state representation work and decoded-task accuracy are therefore weakly coupled in this regime; we accordingly reframe LLM-domain JEPA evaluation as a coupling problem, in which the operative question is under which metrics useful hidden geometry becomes decoder-visible task signal.

</details>


### [81] [Breakeven complexity: A new perspective on neural partial differential equation solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15399)
*Yijing Zhang,Nicholas Roberts,Tanya Marwah,Mikhail Khodak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了"breakeven complexity"指标来评估神经偏微分方程(PDE)求解器，通过计算学习求解器相对于传统求解器达到成本效益所需的前向求解次数来衡量其效率。研究发现神经PDE求解器在问题难度增加时(如成本、维度、 rollout长度、物理 regime如更高雷诺数)变得更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于精度的评估框架存在两个核心问题：(1) 神经求解器需要数据生成、训练和调优的前期成本；(2) 经典求解器也可生成足够低模拟成本的低保真解。现有评估未充分考虑端到端成本，亟需新的评估范式。

Method: 提出breakeven complexity指标——衡量学习求解器相对于误差等效的传统求解器达到成本效益所需的正向求解次数；应用scaling laws确定训练预算分配策略；实现平滑误差匹配的方法。

Result: 在APEBench的2D周期域三个PDE及PyFR代码生成的多障碍物流体benchmark上评估，神经PDE求解器在问题难度增加时变得更有效。

Conclusion: breakeven complexity可量化神经求解器相对于传统求解器的实际成本效益，为实际部署提供更全面的评估依据。

Abstract: Neural surrogate solvers of partial differential equations (PDEs) promise dramatic speedups over numerical methods, especially in scenarios requiring many solves. However, current accuracy-based evaluations do not fully consider two central issues: (1) neural solvers incur substantial up-front costs for data generation, training, and tuning; and (2) classical solvers can also generate low-fidelity solutions at a sufficiently low simulation cost. To explicitly account for these realities and fully incorporate end-to-end costs, we propose an evaluation framework centered on breakeven complexity, a metric that counts the forward solves before a learned solver is cost-effective relative to an error-equivalent traditional solver. To evaluate this measure, we apply scaling laws to determine how much training budget to allocate to data generation and discuss how to achieve smooth error-matching in diverse settings. We evaluate the breakeven complexity of multiple neural PDE solvers on three PDEs on 2D periodic domains from APEBench and a novel benchmark of flows past multiple obstacles generated by the GPU-native PyFR code. Among other findings, our results suggest that neural PDE solvers become more effective as problems get harder in terms of cost, dimension, rollout, physics regime (e.g. higher Reynolds number), etc.

</details>


### [82] [$φ$-Balancing for Mixture-of-Experts Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15403)
*Lizhang Chen,Jonathan Li,Qi Wang,Runlong Liao,Shuozhe Li,Chen Liang,Ni Lao,Qiang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: φ-balancing通过凸优化和镜像下降方法，直接优化总体层面的专家平衡，解决了现有方法依赖噪声小批量统计的问题，在大规模预训练和微调中表现优于Switch-style和loss-free基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前MoE模型中的负载均衡方法主要基于启发式策略，在噪声小批量分配统计上操作，导致与总体目标之间存在偏差，无法真正实现专家的平衡利用。

Method: 1) 将总体路由分布的期望表示为严格凸、对称、可微的势函数；2) 利用凸对偶推导等价的min-max优化形式；3) 通过镜像下降获得简单的在线算法；4) 实现基于EMA的路由调整，开销极小。

Result: 在大规模预训练和下游微调任务中，φ-balancing始终优于Switch-style和loss-free基线方法，展现出更稳定、更有效的专家利用效果。

Conclusion: 该框架为MoE负载均衡提供了一种原则性方法，通过直接优化总体目标而非依赖局部统计，实现了更稳定可靠的专家平衡，对预训练和微调阶段均有显著提升。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models rely on balanced expert utilization to fully realize their scalability. However, existing load-balancing methods are largely heuristic and operate on noisy mini-batch assignment statistics, introducing bias relative to population-level objectives. We propose $φ$-balancing, a principled framework that directly targets population-level expert balance by minimizing a strictly convex, symmetric, and differentiable potential of the expected routing distribution. Using convex duality, we derive an equivalent min-max formulation and obtain a simple online algorithm via mirror descent, yielding an efficient EMA-based routing adjustment with negligible overhead. Across large-scale pretraining and downstream fine-tuning, $φ$-balancing consistently outperforms prior Switch-style and loss-free baselines, demonstrating more stable and effective expert utilization.

</details>


### [83] [Transformer Scalability Crisis: The First Comprehensive Empirical Analysis of Performance Walls in Modern Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15413)
*Mahdi Naser Moghadasi,Faezeh Ghaderi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过对118个transformer模型的首次大规模系统评估，发现模型在处理长序列时存在严重的性能瓶颈：随着序列长度增加，成功率急剧下降，2048 tokens时全部失败。压缩模型反而比大型生成模型具有更高的参数效率，证实了O(n²)复杂度在实际部署中的真实约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管transformer架构在自然语言处理领域取得了显著成功，但其规模化限制仍缺乏系统性的实证分析理解。

Method: 系统化基准测试方法：对118个transformer模型进行跨7个架构类别的综合评估，分析加载时间、内存消耗和计算效率，序列长度范围从128到2048 tokens。

Result: 揭示了关键的规模化危机：88.1%的模型成功处理512 token序列，但1024 token时骤降至44.9%，2048 token时完全失败(0%)。压缩模型达到649.2 tokens/sec/M参数的优异参数效率，而大型生成模型仅为12.5 tokens/sec/M。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了新的transformer评估基准方法论，为实际部署决策提供了关键洞察。研究结果表明，理论上的O(n²)注意力复杂度确实转化为可测量的性能瓶颈，这挑战了当前关于模型规模化的普遍假设。

Abstract: Despite the remarkable success of transformer architectures in natural language processing, their scalability limitations remain poorly understood through systematic empirical analysis. This paper presents the first comprehensive large-scale evaluation of 118 transformer models across seven distinct architectural categories, revealing fundamental performance walls that manifest as hard deployment constraints. Our systematic benchmarking methodology uncovers a critical scalability crisis: while 88.1% of models successfully process sequences up to 512 tokens, this drops dramatically to 44.9% at 1024 tokens, with complete failure (0%) at 2048 tokens. Through rigorous analysis of loading times, memory consumption, and computational efficiency across sequence lengths from 128 to 2048 tokens, we demonstrate that compressed models achieve superior parameter efficiency (649.2 tokens/sec/M parameters) compared to large generative models (12.5 tokens/sec/M). Our findings challenge prevailing scaling assumptions and provide the first quantitative evidence that the theoretical O(n2) attention complexity translates into measurable performance walls. This work establishes new benchmarking methodologies for transformer evaluation and provides critical insights for practical deployment decisions in production environments.

</details>


### [84] [Margin-Adaptive Confidence Ranking for Reliable LLM Judgement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15416)
*Gaojie Jin,Yong Tao,Lijia Yu,Tianjin Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究针对大型语言模型（LLM）与人类判断一致性验证中的置信度估计问题，提出了一种基于间隔的排序学习方法，通过模拟标注者多样性训练专用置信度估计器，并提供泛化理论保证，在多个实验场景中验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有假设检验框架依赖模型估计置信度与人类分歧风险之间的单调性假设，但该假设在实际中可能不成立，且置信度估计器的泛化行为缺乏明确分析。

Method: 通过模拟标注者多样性和基于间隔（margin-based）的排序公式来学习置信度估计器，明确建模LLM区分"人类一致"与"人类不一致"案例的能力；推导出与间隔相关的泛化保证，指导自适应训练过程的设计。

Result: 学习到的置信度估计器在多个数据集和评判模型上展现出更高的排序准确率，强化了置信度与分歧风险之间的单调关系，提高了达成目标一致率的成功率。

Conclusion: 该研究通过学习专用置信度估计器解决了LLM与人判断一致性验证中的核心问题，为LLM输出质量控制提供了理论支撑和实用工具。

Abstract: Jung et al. (2025) introduce a hypothesis testing framework for guaranteeing agreement between large language models (LLMs) and human judgments, relying on the assumption that the model's estimated confidence is monotonic with respect to human-disagreement risk. In practice, however, this assumption may be violated, and the generalization behavior of the confidence estimator is not explicitly analyzed. We mitigate these issues by learning a dedicated confidence estimator instead of relying on heuristic confidence signals. Our approach leverages simulated annotator diversity and a margin-based ranking formulation to explicitly model how confidently an LLM distinguishes between human-agreement and human-disagreement cases. We further derive generalization guarantees for this estimator, revealing a margin-dependent trade-off that informs the design of an adaptive estimator training procedure. When integrated into fixed-sequence testing, the learned confidence estimator yields improved ranking accuracy and empirically strengthens the monotonic relationship between confidence and disagreement risk, leading to higher success rates in satisfying target agreement levels across multiple datasets and judge models.

</details>


### [85] [$f$-Trajectory Balance: A Loss Family for Tuning GFlowNets, Generative Models, and LLMs with Off- and On-Policy Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15417)
*Jake Fawkes,Jason Hartford*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: This paper extends the MSE-based surrogate loss in GFlowNets to the full family of f-divergences, demonstrating that off-policy evaluation preserves the global minimizer while on-policy gradients match the corresponding f-divergence. This enables flexible design of surrogate losses with controllable mode-covering/seeking properties, validated across molecule generation and LLM tuning tasks.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Standard MSE loss between log probabilities in GFlowNets and variational inference provides a low-variance surrogate for KL divergence. However, the researchers aim to extend this framework to the entire family of f-divergences, enabling more flexible control over the trade-off between mode-seeking and mode-covering behavior in generative models.

Method: The authors establish a one-to-one correspondence between translation invariant loss functions on target and model log probabilities and f-divergences. By evaluating the loss off-policy, the method retains the same global minimizer while maintaining validity, enabling application to data collected from different policies.

Result: The extended losses successfully apply to synthetic examples, SynFlowNets for molecule discovery, and asynchronous LLM tuning, retaining predicted on-policy and off-policy properties across diverse generative model architectures.

Conclusion: The proposed losses enable off-policy training while preserving the properties of corresponding f-divergences (e.g., mode coverage), and demonstrate effectiveness across diverse domains including synthetic tasks, molecule discovery, and asynchronous LLM tuning.

Abstract: In GFlowNets and variational inference, it has been shown that the mean square error between target and model log probabilities is an effective, low variance, surrogate loss for training generative models.
  This loss has the property that when evaluated \emph{on-policy} its gradients correspond to those of the KL divergence, while \emph{off-policy} it remains a valid loss with the same global minimizer. In this work, we demonstrate that this construction can be extended to the whole family of $f$-divergences, leading to a family of losses whose on-policy gradients are that of the corresponding $f$-divergence, but retain the same global minimizer off-policy. Specifically, we show that the on-policy gradients lead to a one to one correspondence between translation invariant loss functions on the target and model log probabilities, and $f$-divergences. This equivalence allows us to design new surrogate loss functions for tuning a wide class of generative models that inherit the properties of the corresponding $f$-divergence, such as being more mode covering, whilst being applicable to off-policy data. We apply our losses on a range of tasks, including classic synthetic examples, SynFlowNets for molecule discovery, and asynchronous large language model (LLM) tuning, demonstrating that our models retain their predicted properties on- and off-policy in a wide class of generative models.

</details>


### [86] [Lagrangian Flow Matching: A Least-Action Framework for Principled Path Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15419)
*Shukai Du,Junzhe Zhang,Yiming Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出拉格朗日流匹配框架，将流匹配与经典力学中的最小作用量原理结合。通过最小化一般拉格朗日量的作用量来确定概率路径和速度场，该动态问题可等价转化为静态最优传输问题。此框架统一了现有 OT 流匹配和方差保持扩散路径，并可通过选择不同拉格朗日量获得新的概率路径和动力学特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流匹配方法（包括纠正型和基于最优传输的路径）仅沿直线传输样本，仅覆盖极窄的动力学类别。作者观察到这对应于经典力学中最简 action 原理情形（动能拉格朗日量产生自由粒子直线轨迹），因此考虑推广到一般拉格朗日量以获得更丰富的概率路径和动力学。

Method: 提出拉格朗日流匹配框架，通过最小化一般拉格朗日量在连续性方程和指定端点约束下的作用量来确定概率路径和速度场。证明该动态问题存在等价的静态最优传输(OT)公式化，从而得到一族无模拟的训练目标。

Result: 推导出新的概率路径和速度场，其中 OT 流匹配作为动能特例被恢复，而三角方差保持扩散路径作为谐振子情形被恢复。更一般的拉格朗日量产生有意义的变化。

Conclusion: 该研究开辟了基于经典力学原理设计概率路径的新方向，通过推广到一般拉格朗日量可获得新的概率路径和速度场，且与现有条件流匹配模型具有相当的竞争力。

Abstract: Flow matching trains a neural velocity field by regression against a target velocity associated with a prescribed probability path connecting a simple initial distribution to the data distribution. A central design choice is the path itself. Existing constructions, including rectified and optimal-transport-based paths, transport samples along straight lines between coupled endpoints and thus cover only a narrow class of dynamics. We observe that this corresponds to the simplest case of the least-action principle in classical mechanics, in which the kinetic Lagrangian yields free-particle straight-line trajectories. Building on this observation, we propose Lagrangian flow matching, a physics-based framework in which the probability path and velocity field are determined by minimizing the action of a general Lagrangian subject to the continuity equation and the prescribed endpoints. We show that this dynamic problem admits an equivalent static optimal transport (OT) formulation, yielding a family of simulation-free training objectives that recover OT-based flow matching as the kinetic special case and the trigonometric variance-preserving diffusion path as the harmonic-oscillator case. More general Lagrangians give rise to new probability paths and velocity fields, and numerical experiments show that they induce meaningful changes in the learned dynamics while remaining competitive with existing conditional flow matching models.

</details>


### [87] [Spectral Priors vs. Attention: Investigating the Utility of Attention Mechanisms in EEG-Based Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15433)
*Tawsik Jawad,Gowtham Atluri,Vikram Ravindra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对脑电图(EEG)信号分类神经退行性疾病的挑战，提出基于频谱选择的特征构建方法来增强类别可分离性。通过提取主要脑波频段内的信号特征，将高维原始数据转化为高价值频谱特征。实验表明，传统机器学习模型配合频域/时频域特征即可达到或超越最先进的深度学习模型性能，且注意力机制无法有效提取健康神经活动的稳定特征签名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG时序信号具有显著噪声和粗糙的空间分辨率，增加了神经退行性疾病分类的难度。即使是最先进的深度学习架构也难以区分健康对照组与疾病组，或不同疾病类型之间的差异，原因在于组间相似性高。

Method: 采用频谱选择方法进行特征构建：隔离主要脑波频段（δ、θ、α、β、γ等）内的信号强度，将高维原始数据转换为高价值的频谱特征。方法涵盖频率域和时频域特征提取。

Result: 三个关键发现：a) 基于频域和时频域特征的传统机器学习模型可匹配或超越SOTA深度学习模型性能；b) 注意力机制无法提取健康神经活动中稳定的特征签名（在静息态和任务态EEG中均如此）；c) 注意力模型的局限性似乎是根本性的，提供频谱选择的时域输入也无法显著改善其性能。

Conclusion: 在三个开放获取的静息态EEG数据集和一个任务态EEG数据集上验证了方法的有效性，为所提主张提供了稳健的实证证据。

Abstract: Electroencephalograph (EEG) timeseries signals are characterized by significant noise and coarse spatial resolution, which complicates the classification of neurodegenerative diseases. Even SOTA deep learning architectures struggle to distinguish between healthy controls and diseased subjects, or between different disease types, due to high intergroup similarity. In this paper, we show that a spectrally selective approach to feature construction enhances class separability. By isolating signal strengths within the primary brainwave bands, we transform high dimensional raw data into high value spectral features. Our results demonstrate that a) features derived from frequency and time frequency domain allow traditional machine learning models to match or exceed the performance of SOTA deep learning models, b) Attention mechanism is unable to distill the stable feature signatures that characterize healthy neural activity in both resting and task EEGs, and c) the limitations of attention based models in finding relevant spectral features appear to be fundamental in that providing frequency selective time domain input do not appreciably improve their performance. We validate our methodology across three open source resting EEG datasets and one task EEG dataset, providing robust empirical evidence for our claims.

</details>


### [88] [Don't Stop Me Yet: Sampling Loss Minima via Dissipative Riemannian Mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15459)
*Albert Kjøller Jacobsen,Leo Uhre Jakobsen,Johanna Marie Gegenfurtner,Georgios Arvanitidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DiMS is a physics-inspired sampler using kinetic energy dynamics with configurable exploration to exactly sample reparameterization invariant minima in neural networks, showing promise for Bayesian uncertainty quantification.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Modern neural network loss functions have non-isolated minima forming connected components of reparameterization invariant solutions; existing sampling methods either spread over low-loss regions without exact sampling or are inherently local, limiting exploration across different minima valleys.

Method: Proposes DiMS, a dynamical system-based sampler using kinetic energy with gravitational pull and friction dissipation, designed to sample exactly from minimum level sets while controlling exploration via physically motivated hyperparameters.

Result: DiMS achieves exact sampling from minimum level sets with improved performance compared to previously proposed approaches in Bayesian uncertainty quantification.

Conclusion: DiMS provides an exact, physically-motivated sampling method that balances exploration and convergence, demonstrating improved performance in Bayesian uncertainty quantification tasks.

Abstract: The minima of modern neural network loss functions are typically not isolated, rather they form connected components of reparameterization invariant solutions on the training data. Analytically characterizing these solutions is a hard problem, but sampling approaches are feasible. By construction, existing methods either spread over low-loss regions, and thus do not sample reparameterization invariant solutions exactly, or are inherently local, which limits exploration of other minima valleys. We propose sampling such reparameterization invariant models using a dynamical system based on kinetic energy, subject to a gravitational pull and a friction term that dissipates energy from the system. Our proposed sampler, DiMS, is guaranteed to sample exactly from the minimum level sets and depends on physically motivated hyperparameters which allows control over the exploration capabilities of the sampler. We consider uncertainty quantification in Bayesian inference as the motivating problem and observe improved performance compared to previously proposed approaches.

</details>


### [89] [DrugSAGE:Self-evolving Agent Experience for Efficient State-of-the-Art Drug Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15461)
*Yikun Zhang,Xiwei Cheng,Tianyu Liu,Yuanqi Du,Wengong Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SEAE是一个跨任务记忆框架，通过积累和重用经验来高效构建药物发现领域的SOTA预测模型。在33个分子性质预测任务中排名第一，在跨任务评估中平均归一化得分达0.935，零测试时搜索模式下比基线高出10-30%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建药物发现SOTA预测模型需要昂贵的搜索过程。当前基于LLM的智能体虽能找到SOTA方案，但每次新任务都需从头搜索，无法利用累积经验，导致重复消耗计算资源。

Method: SEAE框架维护跨任务记忆库，包括：1) 验证过的技能；2) 有效策略的统计证据；3) 错误记录及修复方案。可直接复用已验证方案或进行测试时搜索。

Result: 单任务设置：33个分子性质预测任务中排名第一。跨任务评估：16个小任务记忆迁移至17个保留任务，平均归一化得分0.935。零测试时搜索模式下比所有基线高10-30%。

Conclusion: 跨任务记忆机制能显著提升药物发现中SOTA模型开发效率，证明经验复用是解决LLM智能体重复搜索问题的有效途径。

Abstract: Building state-of-the-art (SOTA) predictive models for drug discovery requires expensive search over tools, architectures, and training strategies. Current LLM-based agents can find SOTA solutions through extensive trial and error, but they do not retain the experience accumulated along the way and therefore pay the full search cost on every new task. We propose \method (Self-evolving Agent Experience), a framework that accumulates and reuses experience across tasks to build SOTA drug discovery models efficiently. \method maintains a cross-task memory of verified skills, statistical evidence about effective strategies, and a record of recurring errors and their fixes. In some cases, \method transfers a working solution directly without test-time search. In 33 molecular property prediction tasks, \method ranks first among nine SOTA agents in a single-task setting. With memory accumulated from 16 smaller tasks, \method achieves an averaged normalized score of 0.935 on 17 held-out tasks in a cross-task evaluation setting and outperforms all baseline agents by 10-30\% in a zero-test-time search regime. In summary, our work shows the advantage of cross-task memory for efficient SOTA model development in drug discovery.

</details>


### [90] [GRLO: Towards Generalizable Reinforcement Learning in Open-Ended Environments from Zero](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15464)
*Shangjian Yin,Yu Fu,Yue Dong,Zhouxing Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Post-training has become a crucial step for unlocking the capabilities of large language models, with reinforcement learning (RL) emerging as a critical paradigm. Recent RL-based post-training has increasingly split into two paradigms: reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF), which optimizes models using human preference signals in target domains, and reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR), which operates in verifier-backed environments. The latter has dominated recent reasoning-oriented post-training because it delivers stronger gains and higher efficiency on domain-specific tasks (e.g., reasoning). However, although in-domain RL training achieves promising performance, it still requires a substantial amount of GPU compute, which remains a major barrier to broad adoption. In this work, we study the generalization ability of RLHF learned from scratch from a small set of interactions in open-ended environments, and investigate whether the conversational abilities it explicitly acquires can implicitly transfer to downstream tasks such as mathematical reasoning and code generation, namely GRLO. Specifically, on Qwen3-4B-Base backbone, GRLO improves the average performance across all domains from 24.1 to 63.1 with only 5K prompts and 22.7 GPU hours, requiring about $46\times$ less data and $68\times$ less compute than a strong in-domain RLVR baseline. The resulting model is even competitive with Qwen's released post-trained models which required a much larger training cost. Notably, a subsequent in-domain RLVR stage brings only selective gains, mainly on harder competition-math benchmarks. We hope GRLO offers a simple and efficient recipe for building broadly capable post-trained models. Our code and data will be available at: \href{https://github.com/SJY8460/GRLO}{https://github.com/SJY8460/GRLO}.

</details>


### [91] [Toward World Modeling of Physiological Signals with Chaos-Theoretic Balancing and Latent Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15465)
*Yunfei Luo,Xi Chen,Yuliang Chen,Lanshuang Zhang,Md Mofijul Islam,Siwei Zhao,Peter Kotanko,Subhasis Dasgupta,Andrew Campbell,Rakesh Malhotra,Tauhidur Rahman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NormWear-2 is a world model for physiological signals that combines prior knowledge with adaptive state transitions for multi-scale forecasting, outperforming existing foundation models across diverse clinical and daily-life datasets.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing physiological signal modeling focuses on static tasks (classification, event forecasting, short-horizon next step prediction), leaving long-horizon signal-level forecasting and the predictive nature of physiological signals underexplored.

Method: NormWear-2 encodes multivariate physiological signals and clinical intervention variables into a shared latent space, modeling their joint temporal evolution as a dynamical system. The approach combines inference from pre-trained knowledge (intuition) with non-parametric latent state transition adaptation (insight). A chaos-theoretic balancing strategy of dynamical regime diversity is used during pretraining.

Result: NormWear-2 achieves best overall forecasting performance across time, frequency, and latent representation domains on 8,026 subjects spanning 3.2 hours to 2.3 years, with significant improvements over state-of-the-art time series foundation models.

Conclusion: NormWear-2 represents a step toward general-purpose world models for physiological signals, with competitive downstream representation quality alongside superior forecasting performance.

Abstract: Physiological time series signals reflect complex, multi-scale dynamical processes of the human body. Existing modeling studies focus on static tasks such as classification, event forecasting, or short-horizon next step prediction, while long-horizon signal-level forecasting and predictive nature of physiological signals remain underexplored. We introduce NormWear-2, a world model that encodes both multivariate physiological signals and clinical intervention variables into a shared latent space and models their joint temporal evolution as a dynamical system. Our approach combines inference from prior pre-trained knowledge (intuition) with instant non-parametric latent state transition adaptation (insight), enabling coherent forecasting across multiple temporal scales, conditioned on heterogeneous clinical interventions. During the pretraining phase, we find that chaos-theoretic balancing of dynamical regime diversity yields more robust representations, with a smaller balanced corpus outperforming one twice its size and capturing bifurcation regimes. We evaluate the world model performance across diverse real-world physiological datasets spanning heterogeneous temporal resolutions and intervention regimes, covering daily life, point-of-care, and clinical settings, including fitness planning, hemodialysis, diabetes management, and surgical monitoring. These evaluation datasets comprise records from 8,026 subjects, spanning study durations from 3.2 hours for high-resolution signal data to 2.3 years for longitudinal clinical biomarker tracking. NormWear-2 achieves the best overall forecasting performance across time, frequency, and latent representation domains, with significant improvements over state-of-the-art time series foundation models, while maintaining competitive downstream representation quality, providing a step toward general-purpose world models for physiological signals.

</details>


### [92] [Learning Normalized Energy Models for Linear Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15487)
*Nicolas Zilberstein,Santiago Segarra,Eero Simoncelli,Florentin Guth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于能量模型的扩散模型改进方法，通过协方差正则化训练实现可计算的归一化后验密度，解决了传统扩散模型隐式先验和采样偏差问题，并支持自适应采样、Metropolis-Hastings校正和盲估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型在图像逆问题中存在两大缺陷：(1) 先验密度以隐式方式表示，无法直接计算；(2) 依赖似然近似，引入采样偏差。这限制了模型的可解释性和精确性，阻碍了贝叶斯推理框架的直接应用。

Method: 提出一种用于去噪的能量模型，引入协方差正则化项以强制不同测量条件间的一致性。训练后的模型可直接计算归一化后验密度，支持多种线性逆问题而无需重新训练或微调。基于此框架实现了能量引导的自适应采样、Metropolis-Hastings校正和降质算子的盲估计。

Result: 在ImageNet、CelebA、AFHQ数据集上验证，图像修复和去模糊任务中达到或超越现有基线方法的性能。

Conclusion: 该方法在保留扩散模型采样能力的同时，提供了此前无法实现的功能（自适应采样、无偏校正、盲估计），为扩散模型在贝叶斯逆问题中的应用提供了更灵活、更精确的框架。

Abstract: Generative diffusion models can provide powerful prior probability models for inverse problems in imaging, but existing implementations suffer from two key limitations: $(i)$ the prior density is represented implicitly, and $(ii)$ they rely on likelihood approximations that introduce sampling biases. We address these challenges by introducing a new energy-based model trained for denoising with a covariance-based regularization term that enforces consistency across different measurement conditions. The trained model can compute normalized posterior densities for diverse linear inverse problems, without additional retraining or fine tuning. In addition to preserving the sampling capabilities of diffusion models, this enables previously unavailable capabilities: energy-guided adaptive sampling that adjusts schedules on-the-fly, unbiased Metropolis-Hastings correction steps, and blind estimation of the degradation operator via Bayes rule. We validate the method on multiple datasets (ImageNet, CelebA, AFHQ) and tasks (inpainting, deblurring), demonstrating competitive or superior performance to established baselines.

</details>


### [93] [SurvivalPFN: Amortizing Survival Prediction via In-Context Bayesian Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15488)
*Shi-ang Qi,Vahid Balazadeh,Michael Cooper,Russell Greiner,Rahul G. Krishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SurvivalPFN is a foundation model for survival analysis that leverages prior-data fitted networks and in-context learning to amortize Bayesian inference for censored time-to-event data. Pretrained on diverse synthetic data-generating processes, it adapts to any dataset in a single forward pass without task-specific training or hyperparameter tuning, while avoiding parametric assumptions and producing calibrated survival distributions. Extensive benchmarking demonstrates its strong predictive performance relative to 21 established methods across 61 datasets.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Selecting an appropriate survival analysis estimator from the many specialized approaches requires substantial methodological and domain expertise, presenting a barrier to practical application.

Method: SurvivalPFN is a prior-data fitted network (PFN) that amortizes Bayesian inference for censored observations through in-context learning. It is pretrained on a diverse family of synthetic, identifiable, and right-censored data-generating processes, enabling single forward-pass inference that adapts to the effective complexity of each dataset without task-specific training or hyperparameter tuning, while avoiding restrictive parametric assumptions and producing calibrated survival distributions.

Result: In a large-scale benchmark spanning 61 datasets, 21 methods, and 5 evaluation metrics, SurvivalPFN achieves strong predictive performance and often improves upon established survival models.

Conclusion: SurvivalPFN offers a principled and practical foundation model for survival analysis, with strong empirical evidence across large-scale benchmarks and potential applications in high-impact domains such as healthcare, finance, and engineering.

Abstract: Survival analysis provides a powerful statistical framework for modeling time-to-event outcomes in the presence of censoring. However, selecting an appropriate estimator from the many specialized survival approaches often requires substantial methodological and domain expertise. We introduce SurvivalPFN, a prior-data fitted network that amortizes Bayesian inference for censored observations through in-context learning. SurvivalPFN is pretrained on a diverse family of synthetic, identifiable, and right-censored data-generating processes, enabling it to amortize survival analysis in a single forward pass during inference. As a result, the model adapts to the effective complexity of each dataset without task-specific training or hyperparameter tuning, avoids restrictive parametric assumptions, and produces calibrated survival distributions. In a large-scale benchmark spanning 61 datasets, 21 methods, and 5 evaluation metrics, SurvivalPFN achieves strong predictive performance and often improves upon established survival models. These results suggest that SurvivalPFN offers a principled and practical foundation model for survival analysis, with potential applications in high-impact domains such as healthcare, finance, and engineering (https://github.com/rgklab/SurvivalPFN).

</details>


### [94] [Learning with Conflicts of Interest](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15504)
*Nischal Aryal,Arash Termehchy,Ali Vakilian,Marianne Winslett*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: We propose a game-theoretic framework to address the conflict of interest between ML systems and their users, using scalable algorithms with theoretical guarantees to maximize beneficial information/actions while minimizing biased and manipulative ones.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Financial, social, and political factors create misalignment between ML system owners and users. ML systems produce biased information that influences users to make suboptimal decisions. Existing solutions requiring bias mitigation protocols fail because system owners lack incentive to implement them.

Method: We propose a game-theoretic framework that models the interaction between ML systems and users with conflicting interests. We present scalable algorithms with theoretical guarantees.

Result: The proposed framework maximizes the amount of desired information and actions while minimizing biased and manipulative actions in user-ML system interactions.

Conclusion: The framework enables users to safely benefit from ML systems while being protected against biased and manipulative information through game-theoretic modeling of conflicts of interest.

Abstract: Financial, social, and political factors often prevent the interests of the owners of ML systems and services and their users from being perfectly aligned. ML systems often produce biased information that can influence users to make decisions that are not in their best interest. Current solution approaches require ML systems to implement protocols to mitigate their biases. However, ML system owners usually do not have any incentive to implement these protocols and often argue that it limits their freedom of expression or business. We believe that a successful solution to this problem must recognize the conflict of interest between the ML systems and their users, and use this information to protect users against information that adversely influences their decisions while allowing users to safely benefit from these systems. To this end, we propose a game-theoretic framework that models the interaction between ML systems and users with conflicts of interest. We present scalable algorithms with theoretical guarantees that maximize the amount of desired information and actions and minimize the amount of biased and manipulative actions in interaction with ML systems.

</details>


### [95] [STS: Efficient Sparse Attention with Speculative Token Sparsity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15508)
*Ceyu Xu,Jiangnan Yu,Yongji Wu,Yuan Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出STS稀疏注意力机制，无需模型重训练即可实现2.67倍加速，达到约90%稀疏度，在NarrativeQA上精度损失可忽略，在稀疏度-精度权衡上达到新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注意力机制的二次复杂度给LLM推理带来严重内存和计算瓶颈，在需要处理数百万token序列的智能体应用中尤为突出。

Method: STS利用关键洞察：小规模draft模型的注意力重要性判断与大模型具有高度相关性。通过集成到投机解码框架中，复用draft模型的注意力分数动态构建token级和head级的稀疏掩码，从而剪枝目标LLM中的昂贵注意力计算。

Result: 在NarrativeQA基准上实现2.67倍加速，稀疏度约90%，与密集注意力相比精度损失可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 在稀疏度-精度权衡上建立新SOTA，超越先前技术，能够在给定精度预算下实现更高稀疏度。

Abstract: The quadratic complexity of attention imposes severe memory and computational bottlenecks on Large Language Model (LLM) inference. This challenge is particularly acute for emerging agentic applications that require processing multi-million token sequences. We propose STS, a sparse attention mechanism that requires no model retraining. STS leverages the key insight that tokens identified as important by a smaller draft model are highly predictive of important tokens for a larger target model. By integrating into speculative decoding frameworks, STS repurposes the draft model's attention scores to dynamically construct a token-and-head-wise sparsity mask. This mask effectively prunes the expensive attention computation in the target LLM. Our evaluation shows that STS achieves a 2.67x speedup operating at approximately 90% sparsity on representative benchmark NarrativeQA, maintaining negligible accuracy degradation compared to dense attention. STS establishes a new state-of-the-art on the sparsity-accuracy trade-off, outperforming prior techniques by enabling higher sparsity levels for a given accuracy budget.

</details>


### [96] [parallelcbf: A composable safety-filter and auditability framework for tensor-parallel reinforcement learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15509)
*Yijun Lu,Zilei Yang,Yuyin Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ParallelCBF是首个统一张量并行UAV环境、硬门限CBF安全滤波器、分片BC-to-RL流程和第一性操作审计能力的端到端安全约束训练框架，通过四层可组合API实现了安全强化学习训练过程的全程可追溯性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仿真与强化学习工具(Isaac Lab、OmniSafe、safe-control-gym、CBFKit)虽各具优势，但缺乏统一框架实现端到端安全约束训练，导致安全验证与训练流程割裂，无法保证训练结果的可复现性和可审计性。

Method: ParallelCBF提出四层可组合API架构：(1)张量并行UAV环境；(2)硬门限CBF安全滤波器；(3)分片BC-to-RL流程；(4)第一性操作审计能力。通过预注册、监视狗注册表、故障取证和数据集审计等可组合API实现训练过程的全链路可审计性。技术实现上，采用CPU PyTorch实现双势垒(平方/线性预测)CBF，并设计属性驱动的安全不变性测试套件。

Result: 39测试用例的完整属性安全不变性测试在1.67秒内完成；31415个episode的行为克隆采集战役通过框架自身的ops原语实现课程混合、按桶产量和数据集SHA-256的可审计性；端到端pipeline演示中，审计层成功阻断未达到预注册收敛标准的下游训练阶段，防止退化检查点的静默传播。

Conclusion: ParallelCBF通过统一四大能力并嵌入审计层，实现了端到端安全约束训练的可追溯性，解决了现有工具链中安全验证与训练流程割裂的核心问题。实验证明其审计层可主动阻断不符合收敛标准的下游训练，防止退化检查点的静默传播。

Abstract: While Isaac Lab provides massive parallel UAV simulation, OmniSafe and safe-control-gym provide constrained-RL benchmarks, and CBFKit provides control-barrier-function synthesis tooling, no existing framework unifies these capabilities for end-to-end safety-constrained training. ParallelCBF is the first framework to unify (i)~tensor-parallel UAV environments, (ii)~hard-gate CBF safety filters, (iii)~sharded BC-to-RL pipelines, and (iv)~first-class operational auditability -- pre-registration, watchdog registries, failure forensics, and dataset audits as composable APIs rather than user-implemented scripts. We release ParallelCBF v0.1.0 under Apache~2.0 with a four-layer composable API, a CPU PyTorch reference implementation of a dual-barrier (squared / linear-predictive) CBF, property-based safety invariance tests across vectorized batch sizes that complete in 1.67~s for the full 39-test suite, and a 31{,}415-episode behavior-cloning collection campaign whose curriculum mix, per-bucket yields, and dataset SHA-256 are auditable through the framework's own \texttt{ops} primitives. We report a representative end-to-end pipeline execution in which the framework's auditability layer halted a downstream training stage that did not meet pre-registered convergence criteria, preventing silent propagation of a degraded checkpoint -- an architectural property we argue is necessary, not merely useful, for reproducible empirical robotics research. The framework is installable via \texttt{pip install parallelcbf}; source and release artifacts are available at https://github.com/xiaoyang-123-cell/ParallelCBF.

</details>


### [97] [Neural Point-Forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15524)
*Bruno Trentini,Jacob Hume,Vincenzo Antonio Isoldi,Philipp Misof,Ekaterina S. Ivshina,Kelly Maggs*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出神经点形式(Neural Point Forms, NPFs)，一种利用扩散几何和微分形式在点云上编码高阶切线信息的原则性可学习几何特征。通过拉普拉斯技术构建离散模型进行微分形式比较，并证明在标准采样条件下的长期一致性。该方法输出学习到的形式比较矩阵，具有紧凑、高效和排列不变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点云学习的现有方法主要依赖坐标、成对距离或学习的图邻域，但这些表示无法直接捕获底层几何对象的高阶切线信息。微分形式是编码此类信息的有效工具，但在离散点云上缺乏自然切结构。

Method: 1. 利用扩散几何中的拉普拉斯技术，在缺乏自然切结构的情况下构建离散的微分形式比较模型；2. 在连续统中，将共享特征空间的子流形表示为比较矩阵，其条目描述特征形式与外蕴切线信息的交互；3. 在标准采样、带宽、密度和流形假设下证明比较矩阵的长期一致性。

Result: NPFs是一个紧凑、高效且排列不变的神经常量层，输出学习到的形式比较矩阵。在合成数据和生物学相关实验表明，该方法具有竞争力和可解释性，尤其在标签依赖于采样密度、流形结构或响应相关群体几何时优势明显。

Conclusion: NPFs为点云学习提供了一种有竞争力的可解释表示方法，其最大优势在于能够捕获依赖采样密度、流形结构和响应相关几何的标签信息，为生物学等领域的点云分析提供了新工具。

Abstract: Point cloud learning often rests on the premise that observed samples are noisy traces of an underlying geometric object, such as a manifold embedded in a high-dimensional feature space. Yet much of this geometry is not captured directly by coordinates, pairwise distances, or learned graph neighborhoods alone. In the smooth setting, differential forms are devices to encode higher order tangency information. In this work, we introduce a new family of principled learnable geometric features for point clouds called neural point-forms (NPFs). In the absence of a natural tangency structure, we instead use Laplacian-based techniques from Diffusion Geometry to build a discrete model for comparing differential forms on point clouds via inner products. In the continuum, submanifolds of a shared ambient feature space are represented as comparison matrices, whose entries describe how pairs of feature forms interact with extrinsic tangency information. We make this intuition precise by proving the long-run consistency of comparison matrices under standard sampling, bandwidth, density, and manifold-hypothesis assumptions. This yields a compact, efficient and permutation-invariant neural layer whose output is a learned form-comparison matrix. Across synthetic and biologically relevant experiments, we show that NPFs provide a competitive, and interpretable representation, with the strongest benefits appearing when labels depend on sampling density, manifold-like structure, or response-relevant population geometry.

</details>


### [98] [DeltaPrompts: Escaping the Zero-Delta Trap in Multimodal Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15532)
*Jaehun Jung,Hyunwoo Kim,Brandon Cui,Ximing Lu,David Acuna,Prithviraj Ammanabrolu,Yejin Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文识别了VLM蒸馏中高达69%的训练提示是"零Delta"（师生模型答案分布完全一致），提供极小的学习信号。基于此，作者提出DeltaPrompts方法论，通过主动针对学生模型的失败模式合成高答案分歧的提示，最终生成200k高质量合成推理问题数据集，在多种蒸馏场景下实现最高15%的相对提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current VLM distillation relies on prompts chosen via simple heuristics or aggregated from off-the-shelf datasets, but up to 69% of these prompts are "zero-delta" — the teacher and student already agree on the answer distribution, providing minimal learning signal and causing student improvement to rapidly saturate regardless of data scale.

Method: A staged synthesis pipeline that repurposes existing datasets as seeds and actively targets student failure modes to produce prompts with high answer divergence ($Δ$). The key insight is that only prompts with non-zero divergence between teacher and student provide effective learning signals during distillation.

Result: Non-zero answer divergence is critical for effective scaling; DeltaPrompts drives substantial gains with up to 15% relative improvement even on top of highly-optimized reasoning models (e.g., Qwen3-VL-8B-Thinking), averaged over 10 benchmarks spanning chart, document, and perception-centric reasoning.

Conclusion: DeltaPrompts dataset (200k synthetic problems) achieves up to 15% relative improvement across three evaluation settings: on-policy distillation, cross-family transfer, and off-policy fine-tuning, consistently outperforming standard datasets on 10 benchmarks.

Abstract: Distillation enables compact Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to obtain strong reasoning capabilities, yet the prompts driving this process are typically chosen via simple heuristics or aggregated from off-the-shelf datasets. We reveal a critical inefficiency in this approach: up to 69% of the prompts in standard chart / document reasoning datasets are effectively zero-delta, meaning the teacher and student already induce the exact same answer distribution. Training on these prompts provides minimal learning signal, causing student improvement to rapidly saturate regardless of data scale. To escape the zero-delta trap, we return to first principles: distillation fundamentally minimizes distributional divergence, and thus a prompt is valuable only if it exposes a functional capability gap between the teacher and student. We quantify this gap through answer divergence ($Δ$), demonstrating that non-zero divergence is critical for effective scaling. Building on this insight, we propose a staged synthesis pipeline that repurposes existing datasets as seeds, actively targeting student failure modes to produce better prompts. The result is DeltaPrompts, a diverse dataset of 200k synthetic, high-divergence reasoning problems. We evaluate DeltaPrompts across three distinct settings: on-policy distillation with the target teacher-student pair, transfer to a novel model family without regenerating the data, and off-policy fine-tuning of a non-reasoning model. Across all scenarios, DeltaPrompts drives substantial gains, yielding up to 15% relative improvement even on top of a highly-optimized reasoning model (e.g., Qwen3-VL-8B-Thinking) -- averaged over 10 benchmarks spanning chart, document and perception-centric reasoning.

</details>


### [99] [CTF4Nuclear: Common Task Framework for Nuclear Fission and Fusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15549)
*Stefano Riva,Carolina Introini,Antonio Cammi,Dean Price,Alexey Yermakov,Yue Zhao,Philippe M. Wyder,Judah Goldfeder,Jan Williams,Amy Sara Rude,Matteo Tomasetto,Joe Germany,Joseph Bakarji,Georg Maierhofer,Miles Cranmer,J. Nathan Kutz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一个用于核工程中机器学习的通用任务框架（CTF），旨在为核能系统提供标准化的ML方法评估基准，解决当前核工程领域ML方法比较缺乏统一标准的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核能作为清洁能源的重要组成部分，其系统设计和运行极其复杂，涉及非线性多物理场相互作用。高保真模拟计算成本高昂，难以实时应用；传统基于模型的方法受限于简化假设导致与实际测量存在偏差。核工程作为安全关键领域，急需对不同ML方法进行公平比较和深入理解其优缺点。

Method: 提出核工程ML通用任务框架（CTF），包括：来自不同核及核相关系统的精选数据集；基于12个成熟指标的评估体系；以及仅从稀疏测量进行系统监测的新范式。使用标准ML基线模型进行基准测试。

Result: 通过对标准ML基线模型的基准测试，揭示了当前方法在核工程应用中的局限性。

Conclusion: 该框架旨在以隐藏测试集上的标准化评估取代随意比较，提升核工业科学机器学习的严谨性和可重复性标准。

Abstract: The demand for clean energy is ever increasing, with new nuclear technologies presenting a complementary solution to renewable energies. However, designing and operating these systems is exceptionally difficult, given the complexity of the physical phenomena that interact to form the system dynamics. While high-fidelity simulations help to understand the non-linear, multi-physics interactions within a reactor, they are computationally expensive and rarely suitable for real-time applications. Furthermore, model-based approaches are inherently sensitive to simplifying assumptions required to derive their governing equations and parameters, leading to inevitable discrepancies with real-world measurements. In contrast, Machine Learning (ML) methods have the potential to generate reliable surrogate models which may be able to quickly predict the system's behaviour. However, the number of data-driven methods that can potentially be used for this task is large and diverse. In a safety-critical setting such as nuclear engineering, a fair comparison of different ML methods, and a clear understanding of their advantages and limitations, is of paramount importance. To address this, we introduce a Common Task Framework (CTF) for ML in nuclear engineering, building upon previous efforts in dynamical systems and seismology. This CTF considers a curated set of datasets from different nuclear and nuclear-adjacent systems. The CTF evaluates the performance of a method on 12 established metrics, alongside a new paradigm focused on system monitoring from sparse measurements only. We illustrate the framework by benchmarking standard ML baselines against these datasets, revealing current method limitations. Our vision is to replace ad hoc comparisons with standardized evaluations on hidden test sets, raising the bar for rigour and reproducibility in scientific ML for the nuclear industry.

</details>


### [100] [Characterizing Learning in Deep Neural Networks using Tractable Algorithmic Complexity Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15551)
*Pedram Bakhtiarifard,Sophia N. Wilson,Mahmoud Afifi,Jonathan Wenshøj,Raghavendra Selvan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: This paper introduces QuBD (Quantized Block Decomposition), a method extending Kolmogorov-Chaitin-Solomonoff (KCS) complexity estimation to any k-ary object by quantizing neural network weights and aggregating per bit-plane CTM estimates. QuBD offers theoretically tighter bounds than prior binarization methods. Applied to DNN weight analysis, it reveals that algorithmic complexity decreases during training, scales with data, increases during overfitting, captures grokking dynamics, and correlates with generalization — while most information resides in most significant bit-planes.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Large-scale DNN training is resource-intensive, motivating model compression. The "learning as compression" hypothesis suggests training induces structure in weights enabling compression. Existing KCS complexity estimators (CTM, BDM) cannot scale to modern DNN weights — they are limited to small binary objects.

Method: QuBD extends KCS complexity estimation to k-ary objects through: (1) Weight quantization to finite alphabet, (2) Per bit-plane CTM estimation aggregated across planes. The authors establish theoretical bounds proving QuBD's estimation gap is strictly tighter than binarization-based methods. Experiments validate on multiple scales including grokking phenomena.

Result: QuBD enables measuring how neural network weight complexity evolves: it decreases during learning, scales with data budget, increases during overfitting, and captures the delayed generalization pattern in grokking. Higher algorithmic complexity correlates with worse generalization. The most significant bit-planes carry most algorithmic information.

Conclusion: QuBD provides the first scalable KCS complexity estimation method for large non-binary objects (DNNs). Key findings: (1) Algorithmic complexity decreases during learning, scales with data, increases during overfitting; (2) Captures delayed generalization in grokking; (3) Complexity correlates with generalization; (4) Most significant bit-planes contain most algorithmic information, offering a diagnostic for quantization decisions. The theoretical analysis guarantees strictly tighter estimation bounds than prior binarization approaches.

Abstract: Training large-scale deep neural networks (DNNs) is resource-intensive, making model compression a practical necessity. The widely accepted ''learning as compression'' hypothesis posits that training induces structure in network weights, which enables compression. Measuring this structure through Kolmogorov-Chaitin-Solomonoff (KCS) complexity is appealing, but existing estimators based on the Coding Theorem Method (CTM) and the Block Decomposition Method (BDM) are limited to small binary objects and do not scale to modern DNNs. We introduce the Quantized Block Decomposition method (QuBD), which extends algorithmic complexity estimation to any $k$-ary object. QuBD first quantizes the network weights to a finite alphabet, then estimates the KCS complexity by aggregating per bit-plane CTM estimates. We show theoretically that QuBD yields a strictly tighter estimation gap with respect to true KCS complexity than binarization-based methods. Using QuBD, we study how the algorithmic complexity of neural network weights evolves during training, showing that it decreases as models learn, scales with data budget, increases during overfitting, follows the delayed generalization observed during grokking, and correlates with generalization performance. We further show that algorithmic information resides predominantly in the most significant bit-planes, which can serve as a practical diagnostic for determining appropriate post-training quantization levels. This work offers novel insights into learning mechanisms in DNNs by providing the first scalable, tractable estimates of KCS complexity for large, non-binary objects such as DNN weights.

</details>


### [101] [CrystalBoltz: End-to-End Protein Structure Determination via Experiment-Guided Diffusion for X-Ray Crystallography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15564)
*Minseo Kim,Huanghao Mai,Jay Shenoy,Alec Follmer,Gordon Wetzstein,Frederic Poitevin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CrystalBoltz是一个晶体学精修的生成框架，将精修问题重新定义为贝叶斯推断，能够直接从结构因子振幅进行实验引导的原子结构采样和精修，在准确性和效率上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前在公共蛋白质结构数据库上训练的生成模型无法直接利用新的晶体学实验数据进行条件化。晶体学中测量的结构因子振幅本身无法确定电子密度图或原子结构，因为相关的相位不可观测。结构确定仍是一个逆问题，需要候选模型同时满足结构合理性和与衍射数据的一致性，通常需要人类专家的大量手动精修。

Method: CrystalBoltz框架将晶体学精修重新定义为原子结构的贝叶斯推断，直接在结构因子振幅上操作。该方法从无引导生成（基于预训练蛋白结构先验）过渡到实验引导的后验采样，随后进行原子坐标和B因子精修。

Result: 在多个蛋白质晶体学数据集上，CrystalBoltz实现了比基线方法更低的坐标RMSD和R因子，同时将运行时间相对现有实验引导精修方法减少了33倍。

Conclusion: CrystalBoltz demonstrates that directly incorporating crystallographic measurements into generative model workflows significantly improves structure determination. The method reduces runtime by 33x compared to existing approaches while achieving lower coordinate RMSD and R-factors, representing a substantial advance in automated crystallographic refinement.

Abstract: Generative models trained on public databases of protein structures, most of which have been determined by X-ray crystallography, now provide powerful priors for structure prediction. However, they are not readily conditioned on the measurements from a new crystallographic experiment, limiting their use for X-ray structure determination. In crystallography, the measured structure-factor amplitudes do not by themselves determine an electron density map or atomic structure because the associated phases are unobserved and must be inferred. Structure determination therefore remains an inverse problem in which candidate models must be both structurally plausible and consistent with measured diffraction data, often requiring substantial manual refinement by human experts. Emerging methods aim to incorporate experimental information more directly into predictive and refinement workflows. We present CrystalBoltz, a generative framework that casts crystallographic refinement as Bayesian inference over atomic structures and operates directly on structure-factor amplitudes. CrystalBoltz moves from unguided generation with a pre-trained prior over protein structures to experiment-guided posterior sampling, followed by atomic coordinate and B-factor refinement. Across multiple protein crystallography datasets, CrystalBoltz attains lower coordinate RMSD and lower R-factors than the strongest baselines considered, while reducing runtime by a factor of 33 relative to existing experimentally guided refinement.

</details>


### [102] [AstraFlow: Dataflow-Oriented Reinforcement Learning for Agentic LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15565)
*Haizhong Zheng,Yizhuo Di,Jiahui Wang,Shuowei Jin,Xueshen Liu,Yongji Wu,Z. Morley Mao,Ion Stoica,Jiawei Zhao,Beidi Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AstraFlow是一个数据流导向的强化学习系统，通过将训练器中心控制架构替换为原则性组件抽象，实现 rollout 服务、数据流管理和训练的解耦，从而高效支持多策略智能体RL工作负载及异构跨区域计算资源利用，在保持精度的同时实现2.7倍训练加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习用于提升大语言模型的推理、编码和工具使用能力，但智能体RL成本高昂。扩展RL到智能体LLM需要支持多策略协作训练和弹性异构跨区域计算资源高效利用。现有LLM RL系统存在局限：每次扩展都需专门的系统工程，根源在于训练器中心控制架构和缺乏RL系统组件的原则性抽象。

Method: 提出AstraFlow，一个数据流导向的RL系统。核心创新：用原则性组件抽象替代传统训练器中心控制。将rollout服务、数据流管理和训练解耦为自主组件，实现复杂多策略智能体RL工作负载的原生支持，高效利用多样计算资源。

Result: 在数学、代码、搜索和AgentBench工作负载上评估，证明同一系统支持多策略训练、弹性扩展、异构跨区域执行和可组合数据算法（无需系统级代码修改）。多策略协作训练中，达到与现有RL系统相当或更好的精度，训练时间加速2.7倍。

Conclusion: AstraFlow通过数据流导向的架构设计，成功解决了智能体RL系统的可扩展性和效率问题，为复杂RL工作负载提供了统一、高效的系统支持框架。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) is increasingly used to improve the reasoning, coding, and tool-use capabilities of large language models, but agentic RL remains prohibitively expensive. Scaling RL to agentic LLMs requires supporting complex workloads, including multi-policy collaborative training, while efficiently using elastic, heterogeneous, and cross-region compute resources. Existing LLM RL systems support some of these capabilities, but each new extension often requires dedicated system engineering. This burden arises from trainer-centered control architectures and the lack of principled abstractions for RL system components. To address these limitations, we propose AstraFlow, a dataflow-oriented RL system that replaces conventional trainer-centered control with principled component abstractions. In AstraFlow, rollout services, dataflow management, and training are decoupled into autonomous components, enabling the system to natively support complex multi-policy agentic RL workloads and efficiently exploit diverse compute resources. We evaluate AstraFlow across math, code, search, and AgentBench workloads, showing that the same system supports multi-policy training, elastic scaling, heterogeneous cross-region execution, and composable data algorithms without system-level code changes. In multi-policy collaborative training, AstraFlow achieves comparable or better accuracy than existing RL systems while speeding up training time by 2.7x.

</details>


### [103] [VSPO: Vector-Steered Policy Optimization for Behavioral Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15604)
*Xuechen Zhang,Zijian Huang,Kai Yang,Weijia Zhang,Jiasi Chen,Samet Oymak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VSPO是一种解决语言模型多目标优化中稀疏奖励问题的新方法，通过在GRPO中引入可变steering强度来上采样稀有行为，并在理论上证明了其相比reward shaping的优越性，实验验证其在多个推理基准上能有效控制目标行为同时保持精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代语言模型需要同时优化主要精度目标和次要行为偏好（如verbosity、agreeableness、专业水平），但基模型中目标行为可能非常罕见甚至不存在，导致稀疏奖励瓶颈问题。

Method: VSPO修改GRPO，通过使用与目标行为相关的steering vector来控制生成rollouts的行为强度，通过变化steering强度对稀有行为进行上采样并丰富rollouts多样性。这一过程可解释为一种on-policy latent self-distillation程序。

Result: 理论方面：在bandit抽象下，当steering诱导的分布与目标行为充分对齐时，VSPO比reward-shaped GRPO获得更好的迭代复杂度。实验方面：在MATH和MMLU-Pro基准上针对四种目标行为（解释专业性、信心表达、抗误导鲁棒性、响应冗长度）的评估显示，VSPO一致性地改善了目标行为控制，同时相比reward shaping、teacher-trace distillation和guidance-based基线保持了或提升了任务精度。

Conclusion: VSPO provides an effective framework for multi-objective LLM optimization, achieving better control over target behaviors while maintaining or improving task accuracy across multiple reasoning benchmarks.

Abstract: Modern language models often need to optimize a primary accuracy objective while also accommodating secondary behavioral preferences, such as verbosity, agreeableness, or the level of technical expertise in its response. In practice, a base model may exhibit a desired behavior very rarely or not at all. Thus, endowing the model with a target behavior creates a sparse behavioral reward bottleneck. To address such multi-objective problems, we introduce Vector-Steered Policy Optimization (VSPO) which employs a steering vector associated with the target behavior to control the behavior intensity of the generated rollouts. VSPO is obtained by modifying GRPO to sample rollouts with varying steering intensities. This process can be interpreted as an on-policy latent self-distillation procedure where the model internalizes its steering vector. By varying steering intensities, VSPO upsamples rare behaviors and enriches rollout diversity, which alleviates the sparse reward issue and provably accelerates the policy optimization. Through comprehensive theory and experiments, we establish that VSPO has favorable properties compared to vanilla reward shaping and other alternative approaches. Specifically, under a bandit abstraction, VSPO provably achieves better iteration complexity than reward-shaped GRPO when the steering-induced distributions are sufficiently aligned with the target behavior. We evaluate VSPO across multiple reasoning benchmarks, including MATH and MMLU-Pro, for four target behaviors: explanation expertise, confidence expression, robustness to misleading context, and response verbosity. Our results show that VSPO consistently improves the control along target behavior while maintaining or improving task accuracy compared with reward shaping, teacher-trace distillation, and guidance-based baselines.

</details>


### [104] [Embracing Biased Transition Matrices for Complementary-Label Learning with Many Classes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15586)
*Tan-Ha Mai,Chao-Kai Chiang,Han-Hwa Shih,Gang Niu,Masashi Sugiyama,Hsuan-Tien Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对互补标签学习（CLL）在多类别场景下的性能瓶颈，提出通过非均匀生成互补标签来突破限制。核心创新是BICL框架，它将互补标签限制在类别子集中以增强学习信号，首次使CLL在大规模标签空间（100-200类）上实现有效学习，精度提升超过七倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CLL方法假设互补标签均匀生成，在大规模标签空间下会严重稀释学习信号，导致性能急剧下降，难以扩展到大规模分类任务。

Method: 提出Bias-Induced Constrained Labeling (BICL)框架，通过精心设计非均匀的互补标签生成过程，将互补标签限制在部分类别子集中，从而增强学习信号。该框架贯穿数据收集到训练的全流程。

Result: 在CIFAR-100（100类）和TinyImageNet-200（200类）上相比传统CLL方法实现了超过七倍的精度提升。

Conclusion: BICL框架能够有效突破传统CLL方法在多类别场景下的性能瓶颈，在CIFAR-100和TinyImageNet-200上实现了超过七倍的精度提升，为将CLL扩展到大规模标签空间的实际应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: Complementary-label learning (CLL) is a weakly supervised paradigm where instances are labeled with classes they do not belong to. Despite a decade of research, CLL methods remain competitive mainly on 10-class classification, with scaling to large label spaces continuing to be an enduring bottleneck. This limitation stems from the common assumption of uniform label generation in traditional methods, which fatally dilutes the learning signal in many-class settings. In this paper, we demonstrate that this long-standing barrier can be overcome by deliberately designing a biased (non-uniform) generation process that restricts complementary labels to a subset of classes.
