The authors propose that calibrating the source to data-specific characteristics can improve performance. Additionally, unconditional field generation is difficult for high-resolution PDE solutions.

Method: MFFM calibrates the source distribution to empirical low-to-high-fidelity residual scale using local Gaussian-blur correlation, conditions velocity networks on low-fidelity solutions to simplify the residual refinement problem, applies multi-resolution cascades between adjacent fidelities, and performs level-wise pretraining followed by end-to-end fine-tuning with deterministic one-step rollout.

Result: The method achieves efficient cascade refinement that reaches the finest grid in L deterministic network evaluations (one per level), demonstrating strong performance across eight benchmarks including super-resolution and spatiotemporal forecasting tasks.

Conclusion: MFFM provides a learned multigrid analog that reaches the finest grid in L deterministic network evaluations per query (one per cascade level), validated on 8 benchmarks including super-resolution and spatiotemporal forecasting tasks from PDEBench, The Well, and FNO Navier-Stokes dataset.

Abstract: The source distribution in conditional flow matching is a design parameter that can be calibrated to data, not a default isotropic prior. We exploit this in Multi-Fidelity Flow Matching (MFFM), a cascade refinement framework for parametric PDE solutions: the source is calibrated to the empirical low-to-high-fidelity residual scale with local Gaussian-blur correlation, and the velocity network is conditioned on the low-fidelity solution. Conditioning makes the residual refinement problem substantially easier than unconditional field generation, while residual-calibrated source noise improves the flow-matching training geometry. A multi-resolution cascade applies the same construction independently between adjacent fidelities. After level-wise flow-matching pretraining, we fine-tune the composed cascade end-to-end with a deterministic one-step rollout, which makes one velocity evaluation per cascade level the optimized operating point at inference. The result is a learned analog of multigrid refinement that reaches the finest grid in $L$ deterministic network evaluations per query. We validate MFFM on eight benchmarks: two super-resolution problems and six spatiotemporal forecasting tasks from PDEBench, The Well, and the FNO Navier--Stokes dataset.

</details>


### [140] [Entropy Across the Bridge: Conditional-Marginal Discretization for Flow and Schrödinger Samplers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16126)
*Bruno Trentini,Dejan Stancevic,Michael M. Bronstein,Alexander Tong,Luca Ambrogioni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种基于条件边际熵率（conditional-marginal entropy-rate）的目标函数，用于在低NFE（函数评估次数）预算下优化flow matching和Schrödinger bridge模型的推理时间调度。该方法无需训练，通过推导熵率的解析形式（针对高斯布朗桥）来构建非均匀时间网格，使采样资源更多地分配给边界区域。在CIFAR-10和蛋白质生成任务上验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有flow matching和Schrödinger bridge模型的推理网格通常沿用扩散模型中端点驱动的启发式方法，未充分考虑bridge的几何结构。在低推理预算下，采样质量高度依赖于如何分配有限的函数评估次数。现有方法缺乏对bridge几何与边际流演化之间关系的系统性理论指导。

Method: 1) 推导条件边际熵率目标函数，将bridge几何与边际演化分离；2) 对于高斯布朗桥，熵率具有闭合形式，呈现U形分布；3) 基于此设计训练无关的熵感知推理调度器，采用边界密集的非均匀时间网格；4) 在2D bridge/flow模型、CIFAR-10（EDM）和AlphaFlow蛋白质生成任务上验证。

Result: 1) 2D实验中，熵率轮廓成功恢复U形预测，10步ODE-Heun的MMD提升18.1%，SDE-Heun提升22.7%；2) CIFAR-10上，5步采样的FID达到186.3±4.0（线性调度200.5±2.9，余弦调度238.0±5.3）；3) AlphaFlow在CAMEO22和ATLAS基准上，低NFE条件下条件边际调度展现出优势。

Conclusion: 熵率调度可作为高维bridge和flow采样器在低推理预算下的有效资源分配信号。该方法无需额外训练，具有良好的实用性和通用性。

Abstract: For a fixed flow-based generative model under a small inference budget, sample quality can depend strongly on where the sampler spends its few function evaluations. Flow matching and Schrödinger bridges define probability paths, yet their inference grids are usually heuristic or inherited from one-endpoint diffusion. We derive a conditional-marginal entropy-rate objective for bridge-aware discretization, separating endpoint-conditioned bridge geometry from marginal flow evolution, and use it to build a training-free entropic inference-time scheduler from first principles. For Gaussian Brownian bridges this rate is closed-form and U-shaped, motivating boundary-heavy nonuniform grids. On trained two-dimensional bridge/flow models, the estimated profile recovers the predicted shape and improves 10-step ODE-Heun MMD over linear by 18.1%, with a paired 22.7% SDE-Heun improvement in the same low-NFE sweep. On EDM/CIFAR-10, the entropic time-discretization gives the best tested five-step FID (186.3 \pm 4.0 versus 200.5 \pm 2.9 for linear and 238.0 \pm 5.3 for cosine). On AlphaFlow protein generation, entropic conditional-marginal (cond-marg) scheduling shows advantage in low-NFE regimes on both CAMEO22 and ATLAS benchmarks. These results support entropy-rate scheduling as a practical low-budget allocation signal for high-dimensional bridge and flow samplers.

</details>


### [141] [Navigating Potholes with Geometry-Aware Sharpness Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16134)
*Simon Dufort-Labbé,Mehrab Hamidi,Razvan Pascanu,Ioannis Mitliagkas,Damien Scieur,Aristide Baratin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LLQR+SAM将SAM的尖锐度校正与LLQR学习预条件器的慢速几何感知相结合，通过双时间尺度机制在"坑洞"方向放大逃逸信号，同时保持宽平坦盆地的稳定性，实现性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAM通过对所有参数方向统一扰动来寻找平坦最小值，但忽视了底层损失几何结构。

Method: 将SAM与LLQR框架衍生的学习预条件器结合。预条件器以稀疏方式更新并维护为慢速指数移动平均，捕获损失景观几何的平滑低分辨率图像；SAM扰动在此基础上以更快时间尺度探测曲率。

Result: 在标准视觉和序列建模基准上，LLQR+SAM相比SAM和LLQR单独使用均获得一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: LLQR+SAM通过双时间尺度机制实现互补：慢速学习的几何结构引导SAM在平坦区域保持稳定，同时在局部尖锐的"坑洞"处放大逃逸信号。该方法在视觉和序列建模基准上均超越单独使用SAM或LLQR。

Abstract: Sharpness-aware minimization (SAM) encourages flat minima by perturbing parameters along directions of high loss curvature, but treats all parameter directions uniformly, ignoring the underlying loss geometry. We introduce LLQR+SAM, which combines SAM with a learned preconditioner obtained from the recently proposed LLQR framework, a second-order method that recasts steepest descent as a layerwise linear-quadratic regulator problem. The preconditioner is updated sparsely and maintained as a slow exponential moving average, so it captures a smoothed, low-resolution picture of the loss landscape geometry. The SAM perturbation then operates on top of this learned geometry, probing curvature at a faster timescale. We show that this two-timescale structure is not merely a computational convenience: theoretically, the preconditioner amplifies the SAM escape signal in directions that are flat under the average geometry but locally sharp (potholes). Wide, flat basins, by contrast, remain stable. Empirically, LLQR+SAM gives consistent gains over both SAM and LLQR alone across standard vision and sequence modeling benchmarks, supporting the view that slow learned geometry and fast sharpness correction are genuinely complementary.

</details>


### [142] [Surrogate Neural Architecture Codesign Package (SNAC-Pack)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16138)
*Jason Weitz,Dmitri Demler,Benjamin Hawks,Aaron Wang,Nhan Tran,Javier Duarte*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SNAC-Pack is an open-source AutoML framework for hardware-aware neural architecture search targeting FPGA deployment. It uses multi-objective optimization (Optuna/NSGA-II) with a hardware surrogate model to avoid synthesis costs, followed by a combined QAT and pruning compression stage, with final deployment via hls4ml. Demonstrated on particle physics and quantum computing applications, achieving comparable/better accuracy with reduced hardware resource usage and drastically shorter design cycles.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current NAS methods either optimize only for accuracy or use proxy metrics (e.g., BOPs) that poorly correlate with FPGA hardware costs. FPGA cost is multi-dimensional (LUTs, DSPs, FFs, BRAM, latency), creating a large gap between proxy-based optimization and actual hardware constraints.

Method: SNAC-Pack employs a two-stage approach: (1) Multi-objective global search with Optuna/NSGA-II using a hardware surrogate model for fast resource/latency estimation, enabling parallel distributed search; (2) Local search stage combining quantization-aware training (QAT) with iterative magnitude pruning in a unified compression loop. Final models are synthesized via hls4ml.

Result: On jet classification (LHC) and superconducting qubit readout tasks, SNAC-Pack discovered architectures matching or exceeding strong baselines on task metrics while reducing FPGA resource utilization. Notably, qubit readout design exploration was reduced from months of manual tuning to hours of automated search.

Conclusion: SNAC-Pack provides an end-to-end solution for hardware-aware NAS on FPGAs, reducing exploration time from months to hours while maintaining or improving task performance and reducing resource utilization.

Abstract: Neural architecture search (NAS) is a powerful approach for automating model design, but existing methods often optimize for accuracy alone or rely on proxy metrics such as bit operations (BOPs) that correlate poorly with hardware cost. This gap is particularly large for FPGA deployment, where cost is dominated by a multi-dimensional budget of lookup tables, DSPs, flip-flops, BRAM, and latency. We present the Surrogate Neural Architecture Codesign Package (SNAC-Pack), an open-source AutoML framework for hardware-aware neural architecture codesign and end-to-end FPGA deployment. SNAC-Pack runs a multi-objective global search with Optuna and NSGA-II, loading trials to a shared SQLite store that enables parallel workers across compute nodes. A hardware surrogate model outputs per-trial resource and latency estimates, avoiding the synthesis cost that would otherwise dominate the search loop. A local search stage then applies quantization-aware training (QAT) together with iterative magnitude pruning in a combined compression loop, after which the final model is synthesized to FPGA firmware via the hls4ml Python library. A YAML configuration and an optional agentic frontend let users run the pipeline on new datasets without modifying the framework. We demonstrate SNAC-Pack on jet classification at the Large Hadron Collider and superconducting qubit readout, discovering compact architectures that match or exceed strong baselines on the task metric while reducing FPGA resource utilization and, in the qubit readout case, reducing the design space exploration process from months of manual fine-tuning to hours of automated search.

</details>


### [143] [Entropic Auto-Encoding via Implicit Free-Energy Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16164)
*Hazhir Aliahmadi,Irina Babayan,Greg van Anders*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了熵自编码器（EAEs）框架，通过仅使用重建损失作为显式目标，并利用自由能最小化的编码器集合隐式生成潜在变量的先验分布，从而缓解变分自编码器（VAEs）中的后验崩溃问题。该方法引导学习过程朝向高容量近优解区域，同时保持潜在表示的信息性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分自编码器（VAEs）虽然应用广泛，但本质上存在后验崩溃问题——潜在变量被完全忽略。这种失败源于显式先验 imposition 将优化推向对应于无信息潜在表示的损失景观区域。

Method: 熵自编码器（EAEs）框架的核心创新在于：仅将重建损失作为唯一显式目标；通过自由能最小化的编码器集合隐式生成潜在变量的先验分布；该集合偏向于引导学习朝向高容量近优解区域，而解码器更新则将搜索轨迹导向信息丰富的潜在表示。

Result: EAEs 通过学习非高斯、多模态的潜在分布来缓解后验崩溃，生成多样化且数据一致的样本，并保留数据中的不同底层结构。具体表现为：在反应扩散过程中捕获低维动态的超位置；识别 MNIST 潜在表示中的隐式类别差异；在 CelebA 数据集上展示面部结构的层级理解（从"全人类"面部到个体依赖特征）。

Conclusion: EAEs 成功解决了 VAEs 中的后验崩溃问题，同时保持了生成多样性。该框架为理解和改进生成模型提供了一条有前景的新途径。

Abstract: Despite their ubiquity, variational autoencoders (VAEs) inherently suffer from posterior collapse, a failure mode in which latent variables are effectively ignored. This failure arises because explicit prior imposition drives optimization toward loss landscape regions corresponding to uninformative latent representations. Here, we introduce Entropic Autoencoders (EAEs), a framework in which reconstruction loss is the only explicit objective, and entropy generates the latent variables' prior implicitly through a free energy-minimizing ensemble of encoders. This ensemble biases learning toward high-volume regions of near-optimal solutions, while decoder updates direct the search trajectories toward informative latent representations. We demonstrate that EAEs mitigate posterior collapse by learning non-Gaussian, multimodal latent distributions that yield diverse, data-consistent generations and preserve different forms of underlying structure in the data. As a proof-of-concept, we show that an EAE captures a superposition of the known low-dimensional dynamics of a reaction-diffusion process. Then, we show that an EAE identifies implicit categorical distinctions in MNIST latent representations, and displays a hierarchical understanding of facial structure on the CelebA dataset, from an "all-human" face to individual-dependent features.

</details>


### [144] [BAPR: Bayesian amnesic piecewise-robust reinforcement learning for non-stationary continuous control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16170)
*Yifan Zhang,Liang Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BAPR通过融合贝叶斯在线变点检测与鲁棒集成RL，在分段平稳控制系统中实现自适应保守性——切换后最大化保守，随置信度增长平滑放松。所有理论结果经Lean 4完全形式化验证（无sorry），为实际部署提供严格保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决分段平稳实际控制系统中的鲁棒RL两难困境：全局保守策略浪费稳态性能，局部自适应策略在 regime 切换时面临灾难性失败风险。

Method: 提出BAPR算子（模式条件Bellman算子的凸组合）实现γ-收缩；设计基于RMDM损失的上下文条件模块提供模式感知表示；采用BOCD驱动自适应保守机制（检测延迟O(log(1/δ))）。

Result: 严格证明了收缩边界：当信念依赖Q函数时收缩因子为γ+λΔ，收缩失败条件为γ+λΔ≥1；推导出支撑切换后恢复的逐分量形式误差界。

Conclusion: BAPR通过冻结信念分布设计确保理论继承，实现无需部署标签的模式感知策略自适应；所有结果已通过Lean 4完全形式化验证（1,145行代码，22个定理）。

Abstract: Real-world control systems frequently operate under \emph{piecewise stationary} conditions, where dynamics remain stable for extended periods before undergoing abrupt regime changes. Standard robust RL methods face a fundamental dilemma: a globally conservative policy wastes performance during stable periods, while a locally adaptive policy risks catastrophic failure when the regime changes undetected.
  We propose \textbf{BAPR} (Bayesian Amnesic Piecewise-Robust SAC), which unifies Bayesian Online Change Detection (BOCD) with robust ensemble RL. The BAPR operator -- a convex combination of mode-conditional Bellman operators weighted by a frozen belief distribution -- is a $γ$-contraction. A complementary counterexample, machine-verified in Lean~4, establishes a \emph{sharp boundary}: when beliefs depend on the Q-function, the contraction factor becomes $γ+ λΔ$ (where $Δ$ is the mode reward gap), and contraction fails exactly when $γ+ λΔ\geq 1$. We derive a \emph{component-wise} formal error budget for the abstract operator -- every component machine-verified -- bounding post-switch recovery; the budget applies to the abstract mode-mixture operator and inherits to the implemented shared-critic algorithm only through the frozen-parameter design intuition. All results are formally verified with no \texttt{sorry} (1,145 lines across 3 Lean~4 files, 22 machine-verified theorems). BOCD drives an adaptive conservatism mechanism: the policy becomes maximally conservative after detected change-points and smoothly relaxes as confidence grows, with detection delay $O(\log(1/δ))$. A context-conditioning module trained via RMDM loss provides mode-aware representations from simulator-provided mode IDs at training time and requires no mode labels at deployment.

</details>


### [145] [Imitation learning for clinical decision support in pediatric ECMO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16175)
*Fateme Golivand,Michael Skinner,Saurabh Mathur,Ameet Soni,Phillip Reeder,Kristian Kersting,Lakshmi Raman,Sriraam Natarajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对儿科ECMO（体外膜肺氧合）治疗中的临床决策支持问题，提出将模仿学习框架应用于观测数据（动作未直接观察的场景），并评估了TabPFN（基于Transformer的表格数据方法）与传统基线模型（XGBoost、MLP）的性能差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 儿科重症监护中的ECMO治疗具有高复杂度、数据稀缺的挑战，且临床决策涉及持续监测和治疗调整。传统的监督学习方法因动作标签缺失和标注数据有限而难以直接应用。

Method: 采用模仿学习框架，将临床决策建模为从轨迹数据中学习动作模型，核心特点是动作未直接观察。实验对比了TabPFN（新兴的Transformer表格数据方法）与XGBoost、MLP在真实儿科ECMO数据上的表现。

Result: TabPFN在所有对比指标上均一致优于XGBoost和MLP基线模型。

Conclusion: TabPFN可作为儿科ECMO决策支持的强临床行为基线方法，验证了基于Transformer的表格数据方法在此高风险、低资源医疗场景中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Pediatric critical care is a dynamic, high-stakes process involving constant monitoring and adjustments in life-saving treatments. Modeling these interventions is crucial for effective decision support. To address the challenges of high complexity and data scarcity in pediatric Extracorporeal Membrane Oxygenation (ECMO), we frame clinical decision-making as learning to act from trajectories, i.e., imitation learning that learns action models from observational data, with a key feature that actions are not directly observed. We consider TabPFN, a recent transformer-based approach for tabular data, and traditional baselines including XGBoost and Multi-Layer Perceptrons(MLPs) on real-world pediatric ECMO data to learn the action models. We find that the TabPFN-based approach consistently outperforms these classical baselines, supporting its use as a strong clinician-behavior baseline for pediatric ECMO decision support.

</details>


### [146] [Hypothesis-driven construction of mesoscopic dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16211)
*Zhuoyuan Li,Aiqing Zhu,Qianxiao Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Traditional scientific modeling typically begins with fixed, instance-wise effective equations and then carries out equation-specific analysis and computation, a procedure that becomes exceptionally challenging in complex applications such as multiscale systems. We propose an alternative paradigm by learning mesoscopic dynamics within a mathematically constrained hypothesis class. Building upon a generalized Onsager principle, we introduce a unified framework encompassing both dissipative and conservative mesoscopic dynamics. We establish uniform and a priori theoretical guarantees, including global well-posedness, asymptotic stability, unique factorization identifiability, and discrete energy dissipation, applicable to all spatio-temporal evolution equations within this hypothesis class prior to all learning stages. Data from each problem instance is then used to guide the identification of members within our hypothesis class, giving rise to accurate, robust and interpretable dynamical models. We empirically validate this framework on both data from continuum PDE models as a check, and on data arising from microscopic chain models for which exact meso-scale models are unknown. The proposed approach not only acts as an effective dynamics learner, but also offers vital interpretable diagnostics of the underlying physics.

</details>


### [147] [The Privacy Price of Tail-Risk Learning: Effective Tail Sample Size in Differentially Private CVaR Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16219)
*El Mustapha Mansouri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了差分隐私下CVaR学习的完整统计速率理论。研究发现隐私有效样本量并非n，而是εnτ（纯DP）或更小的值；私人CVaR风险分解为尾部统计误差和隐私代价两部分。对于标量估计和有限类获得了精确速率（Θ(B·min{...})），对凸Lipschitz学习类通过模块化约简证明了1/(εnτ)项的必然尺度。最终将问题归约为Θ(nτ)尾部记录上的标准私有学习，确定了核心困难子问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条件风险价值(CVaR)是风险度量中的关键指标，但在差分隐私框架下，其尾部风险估计面临特殊挑战——隐私机制对尾部样本的影响更为显著。研究的核心动机是精确刻画隐私保护如何改变CVaR学习的样本复杂度，以及识别真正的困难所在。

Method: 研究采用三重方法：首先是信息论下界推导（针对标量估计和有限类），其次是对凸Lipschitz学习类的模块化上下界约简，最后建立纯DP下界对近似DP的拓展性质。研究特别关注τ（尾部质量）和隐私预算ε对有效样本量的共同影响。

Result: 研究获得了三组完整（tight）速率结果：（1）标量估计：Θ(B min{1, (nτ)^(-1/2) + (εnτ)^(-1)})；（2）有限类（大小M）：Θ(B min{1, √(log(2M)/(nτ)) + log(2M)/(εnτ)})；（3）凸Lipschitz学习：隐私相关项必然按1/(εnτ)尺度缩放，且维度依赖继承自私有随机凸优化。研究证明这些下界在近似DP的小δ regime下依然成立。

Conclusion: 研究将私人CVaR学习问题归约为标准的私有学习问题，其中有效样本量为Θ(nτ)，从而确定了该领域的核心困难子问题。这为后续研究提供了理论基准，即在评估任何私有CVaR估计算法时，应以其在约nτ个尾部记录上的私有学习性能作为参照标准。

Abstract: Differential privacy changes the effective sample size governing CVaR learning. For tail mass $τ$, the privacy-relevant sample size is not $n$, but $nτ$; equivalently, the effective private tail sample size is $εnτ$. Private CVaR excess risk decomposes into ordinary tail-risk statistical error and a privacy price. This decomposition is complete for scalar estimation and finite classes: scalar estimation has rate $Θ(B \min\{1,(nτ)^{-1/2}+(εnτ)^{-1}\})$, and finite classes of size $M$ have rate $Θ(B \min\{1,\sqrt{\log(2M)/(nτ)}+\log(2M)/(εnτ)\})$. These complete rates hold under pure DP, and their lower bounds extend to approximate DP in the stated small-$δ$ regimes. For convex Lipschitz learning, modular upper and lower reductions show that the CVaR-specific privacy term necessarily scales as $1/(εnτ)$, with dimension dependence inherited from private stochastic convex optimization. Together, these results identify ordinary private learning on $Θ(nτ)$ informative tail records as the canonical hard subproblem inside private CVaR learning.

</details>


### [148] [Dynamics-Level Watermarking of Flow Matching Models with Random Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16239)
*Shuchan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于动力学层面的生成模型水印方法，将水印直接嵌入到流匹配模型的速度场中，通过密钥依赖的扰动实现水印嵌入与提取，在保证生成分布和质量的前提下，实现安全可靠的水印检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生成模型水印方法通常将水印嵌入到模型权重或输出中，存在被检测或移除的风险。为了提高水印的隐蔽性和鲁棒性，需要一种更底层的水印嵌入策略。

Method: 采用随机编码方法，通过连续通道进行水印嵌入。在训练时添加密钥依赖的扰动，嵌入水印到流匹配模型的速度场中。该扰动被设计为不改变生成分布。

Result: 在MNIST和CIFAR-10数据集上，针对不同架构的实验表明，该方法能够可靠地恢复水印信息，保持生成质量，且在没有密钥的情况下解码准确率接近随机猜测水平。

Conclusion: 通过在推理时进行黑盒查询，可以从学习到的连续动态中提取水印信息，无需访问模型内部权重。实验验证了方法的可靠性和安全性。

Abstract: We introduce a dynamics-level approach to watermarking generative models. Rather than embedding signals into model weights or outputs, we embed the watermark directly into the learned continuous dynamics -- the velocity field of a flow matching model. We formulate this as random coding over a continuous channel: a key-dependent perturbation is added during training, and the message is recovered at detection time from black-box queries. The perturbation is designed to leave the generated distribution unchanged. Experiments on MNIST and CIFAR-10 across different architectures confirm reliable message recovery, preserved generation quality, and chance-level decoding accuracy without the secret key.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [149] [DeepSlide: From Artifacts to Presentation Delivery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15202)
*Ming Yang,Zhiwei Zhang,Jiahang Li,Haoseng Liu,Yuzheng Cai,Weiguo Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DeepSlide是一个人在环多智能体系统，专注于优化学术报告的全流程交付，而不仅仅是幻灯片视觉质量。系统通过逻辑链规划器、时间预算分配、内容检索、顺序渲染和安全执行等模块，实现了从需求获取到排练支持的完整流程。在20个领域和多样化受众中，该系统在保持视觉质量的同时，显著提升了叙事流畅性、节奏精确性和幻灯片-讲稿协同性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI幻灯片生成器过度优化视觉成品（美观性），而忽视交付过程（节奏、叙事、演示准备）的优化。这导致生成的演示文稿虽然视觉上吸引人，但在实际演讲表现上欠佳。

Method: 提出DeepSlide，一个人在环多智能体系统，包含四个核心组件：(1)可控逻辑链规划器，带每节点时间预算；(2)轻量级内容树检索器用于内容锚定；(3)带样式继承的马尔可夫式顺序渲染；(4)带最小修复的沙箱执行确保可渲染性。同时提出双计分牌基准测试，将静态成品质量与动态交付表现分离评估。

Result: 在20个领域和多样化受众配置下，DeepSlide在成品质量上与强基线相当，同时在交付指标上获得更大提升，表现为更清晰的叙事流程、更精确的时间节奏、更强的幻灯片-讲稿协同性以及更明确的注意力引导。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了将AI生成从"视觉优化"扩展到"交付优化"的价值。DeepSlide通过整合规划、检索、渲染和执行模块，为学术演示提供了更全面的AI支持框架。

Abstract: Presentations are a primary medium for scholarly communication, yet most AI slide generators optimize the artifact (a visually plausible deck) while under-optimizing the delivery process (pacing, narrative, and presentation preparation). We present DeepSlide, a human-in-the-loop multi-agent system that supports preparing the full presentation process, from requirement elicitation and time-budgeted narrative planning, to evidence-grounded slide--script generation, attention augmentation, and rehearsal support. DeepSlide integrates (i) a controllable logical-chain planner with per-node time budgets, (ii) a lightweight content-tree retriever for grounding, (iii) Markov-style sequential rendering with style inheritance, and (iv) sandboxed execution with minimal repair to ensure renderability. We further introduce a dual-scoreboard benchmark that cleanly separates static artifact quality from dynamic delivery excellence. Across 20 domains and diverse audience profiles, DeepSlide matches strong baselines on artifact quality while consistently achieving larger gains on delivery metrics, improving narrative flow, pacing precision, and slide--script synergy with clearer attention guidance.

</details>


### [150] [Does Theory of Mind Improvement Really Benefit Human-AI Interactions? Empirical Findings from Interactive Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15205)
*Nanxu Gong,Zixin Chen,Haotian Li,Zishu Zhao,Jianxun Lian,Huamin Qu,Yanjie Fu,Xing Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文指出当前ToM评估方法的局限性，提出交互式ToM评估新范式，通过系统研究证明静态基准测试的提升不能直接转化为真实人机交互场景中的性能改善，强调需要基于交互的评估方法来指导下一代社交感知LLM的开发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试通过故事阅读和第三人称视角的多选题来衡量ToM能力，忽略了人机交互（HAI）中第一人称、动态和开放性的特点，无法直接检验ToM改进技术对HAI交互的实际效果。

Method: 提出交互式ToM评估新范式（包含视角和指标的双重转变）；使用四个真实数据集和用户研究进行系统评估；覆盖目标导向任务（编程、数学）和体验导向任务（咨询）。

Result: 静态基准测试的改进并不能总是转化为动态人机交互中更好的表现。

Conclusion: 静态基准测试的改进不能直接转化为动态人机交互中的更好表现；需要基于交互的评估范式来开发下一代具有社会意识的大语言模型；该研究为下一代HAI共融系统中社交感知LLM的开发提供了关键见解。

Abstract: Improving the Theory of Mind (ToM) capability of Large Language Models (LLMs) is crucial for effective social interactions between these AI models and humans. However, the existing benchmarks often measure ToM capability improvement through story-reading, multiple-choice questions from a third-person perspective, while ignoring the first-person, dynamic, and open-ended nature of human-AI (HAI) interactions. To directly examine how ToM improvement techniques benefit HAI interactions, we first proposed the new paradigm of interactive ToM evaluation with both perspective and metric shifts. Next, following the paradigm, we conducted a systematic study of four representative ToM enhancement techniques using both four real-world datasets and a user study, covering both goal-oriented tasks (e.g., coding, math) and experience-oriented tasks (e.g., counseling). Our findings reveal that improvements on static benchmarks do not always translate to better performance in dynamic HAI interactions. This paper offers critical insights into ToM evaluation, showing the necessity of interaction-based assessments in developing next-generation, socially aware LLMs for HAI symbiosis.

</details>


### [151] [Fair outputs, Biased Internals: Causal Potency and Asymmetry of Latent Bias in LLMs for High-Stakes Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15217)
*Jagdish Tripathy,Marcus Buckmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现语言模型在抵押贷款审批中输出层面无偏见，但内部表征保留了种族信息且可被激活转向技术操控，产生不对称偏见效应，表明仅依赖输出审计不足以保障AI公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指令微调语言模型在输出层面表现出公平性，但在内部表征中保留并放大了人口统计信息，且这种隐性偏差在不同人口群体间呈现不对称性。

Method: 使用仅在种族相关姓名上有差异的匹配抵押贷款申请，对开源权重模型进行测试；通过激活转向（activation steering）和跨层干预技术实验。

Result: 模型输出无明显偏见，但在各层保留并放大了人口统计表征；关键层重新注入隐藏表征信息可产生近乎完全的决策逆转；隐性偏见呈现不对称性——转向干预只影响单一方向的决策；且易受对抗性提示工程和参数高效微调影响。

Conclusion: 行为审计不足以检测AI公平性问题，需要结合输出评估与表征分析的"双层测试框架"用于AI治理。

Abstract: Instruction-tuned language models exhibit behavioural fairness in high-stakes decisions while retaining biased associations in their internal representations. However, whether these suppressed representations can affect model outputs - and whether such causal potency is symmetric across demographic groups - remains unknown. We investigate the use of open-weight models for mortgage underwriting using matched applications that differ only in racially-associated names and reveal a critical disconnect: models show no output-level bias, yet retain and amplify demographic representations across model layers. Through activation steering and novel cross-layer interventions, we demonstrate that this suppressed information is decision-relevant: when reinjected at critical layers, it produces near-complete decision reversals. Critically, this latent bias is asymmetric - steering interventions affect decisions in one demographic direction, while producing minimal effects in reverse - and susceptible to adversarial prompt engineering and parameter-efficient fine-tuning. These findings demonstrate that behavioural audits focused on outputs are insufficient: fair outputs can mask exploitable internal biases. They also motivate dual-layer testing frameworks combining output evaluation with representational analysis for AI governance in high-stakes decisions.

</details>


### [152] [CAX-Agent: A Lightweight Agent Harness for Reliable APDL Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15218)
*Chenying Lin,Yichen Hai,Yi He,Ran Wang,Haiyan Qiang,Liang Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出用于MAPDL自动化的CAX-Agent智能体框架，重点评估其恢复策略组件。实验表明基于模型的恢复策略表现最佳，任务完成率达92.67%，显著优于规则驱动和无恢复策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在MAPDL有限元仿真中面临可靠性问题：缺乏结构化执行控制、工具封装和故障恢复机制，导致输出不一致和任务失败率较高。

Method: 提出CAX-Agent三层架构（LLM服务层、Agent工具层、求解器后端层），设计四级恢复阶梯（确定性规则修补→模型驱动重生成→上下文增强→人工介入）；在50个标准结构基准测试上评估三种恢复策略，每种策略重复3次运行；采用双盲独立评分，验证评分者一致性（κ=0.84）。

Result: model_only策略在所有指标上优于其他策略：任务完成率92.67%、任务评分3.59/4、总评分9.16/10、零干预率84%；相比rule_only和no_recovery具有显著优势（Cliff's delta效应量0.81-0.87）。

Conclusion: CAX-Agent框架采用分层架构实现MAPDL自动化中的故障恢复，实验验证了model_only策略的有效性。研究局限性在于基准测试使用简化几何体，结论的普适性需进一步验证，未来工作应扩展到更复杂的工程场景。

Abstract: Large language models deployed for MAPDL finite-element simulation face practical reliability challenges: without structured execution control, tool encapsulation, and fault recovery, outputs may be inconsistent and task failures are common. The Agent Harness paradigm addresses this by inserting domain-specific orchestration middleware that manages tool lifecycles, workflow state, and recovery escalation. This paper presents the architecture of CAX-Agent, a lightweight agent harness purpose-built for MAPDL automation, and empirically evaluates one of its core components -- the recovery policy.CAX-Agent organizes execution into three layers -- LLM service, agent harness, and solver backend -- with a recovery ladder that escalates from deterministic rule patching through model-driven regeneration to context enrichment and human intervention. We evaluate three recovery strategies (no_recovery, rule_only, and model_only) on 50 standard structural benchmarks with three repeated runs per strategy (450 case-runs total). Two independent human raters score task completion under blind conditions; inter-rater agreement is strong (quadratic weighted Cohen's kappa = 0.84, 96 percent of score pairs within one point). Model_only achieves the best completion rate (0.9267), task score (3.59/4), total score (9.16/10), and zero-intervention rate (0.84), outperforming rule_only (0.7733, 3.17/4, 7.03/10, 0.00) and no_recovery (0.6933, 2.74/4, 5.60/10, 0.00) with large effect sizes (Cliff's delta = 0.81-0.87). The benchmark uses deliberately simple geometries to isolate recovery-policy effects; we discuss the scope of these findings and directions for broader validation.

</details>


### [153] [NOVA: Fundamental Limits of Knowledge Discovery Through AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15219)
*Salman Avestimehr,Ken Duffy,Muriel Médard*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NOVA框架通过将迭代自我改进建模为知识空间上的自适应采样，系统分析了AI发现新知识的条件与代价。研究发现了污染陷阱现象——当易发现知识耗尽时，小误报率即可导致知识库退化；并证明了在Zipf定律假设下发现成本随D^α扩展的扩展定律，揭示了探索前沿推进时收益递减的数学本质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索AI系统通过迭代自我改进能否发现真正的新知识，以及发现知识的代价是什么。

Method: NOVA框架将"生成-验证-积累-重训练"循环建模为知识空间上的自适应采样过程；识别有限域覆盖的充分条件及违反时的四种失败模式；分析不完美验证下的污染陷阱机制；在Zipf尾等价假设下推导扩展律。

Result: （1）识别了导致知识最终覆盖有限域的充分条件，以及违反条件时的四种失败模式：污染、遗忘、探索失败和接受失败；（2）发现污染陷阱：易发现知识耗尽后，即使小误报率也会使无效产物进入知识库的速度超过真发现；（3）澄清Good-Turing估计是局部批量多样性诊断量，而非历史未发现有效质量的估计量；（4）证明在Zipf定律（α>1）假设下，累积成本满足R_cum(D)=Θ(c_gen D^α)的扩展定律，量化了探索前沿推进时的渐近收益递减；（5）形式化人类在引导、生成和验证中的放大作用。

Conclusion: 在有限域上，满足充分条件时，累积的真知识最终会覆盖整个域；但违反条件时会产生四种失败模式。文章揭示了污染陷阱现象：当易发现知识耗尽时，即使较低的误报率也会导致无效产物进入知识库的速度超过真发现的速度。研究澄清了Good-Turing估计的局限性，并在Zipf定律（α>1）假设下证明了累积成本满足R_cum(D)=Θ(c_gen D^α)的扩展定律。人机协作中，专家输入在自主探索障碍附近最有价值。

Abstract: Can AI systems discover genuinely new knowledge through iterative self improvement, and if so, at what cost? We introduce the NOVA framework, which models the common ``generate, verify, accumulate, retrain'' loop as an adaptive sampling process over a knowledge space. We identify sufficient conditions under which accumulated genuine knowledge eventually covers a finite domain, and show how their violations produce distinct failure modes: contamination, forgetting, exploration failure, and acceptance failure. We then analyze imperfect verification and identify a contamination trap: as easy-to-find knowledge is exhausted, the model mass assigned to new valid artifacts shrinks, so even small false-positive rates can cause invalid artifacts to enter the knowledge base faster than genuine discoveries. We clarify that Good--Turing estimation is a local batch-diversity diagnostic, not an estimator of the historically undiscovered valid mass that governs long-term discovery. Under a separate tail-equivalence assumption relating the model's effective discovery distribution to a Zipf law with exponent $α>1$, we prove that the cumulative generation cost required to obtain $D$ distinct genuine discoveries satisfies $R_{\mathrm{cum}}(D)=Θ(c_{\mathrm{gen}}D^α)$, where $c_{\mathrm{gen}}$ is the per-candidate generation cost. This scaling law quantifies asymptotic diminishing returns as the discovery frontier advances. Finally, we formalize human amplification through guidance, generation, and verification, explaining why expert input is most valuable near autonomous exploration barriers.

</details>


### [154] [Belief Engine: Configurable and Inspectable Stance Dynamics in Multi-Agent LLM Deliberation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15343)
*Joshua C. Yang,Maurice Flechtner,Damian Dailisan,Michiel A. Bakker*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"信念引擎"(BE)，一个可审计的信念更新框架，将信念视为命题上的证据状态并以标量立场呈现。该系统通过结构化记忆提取论点，使用对数赔率规则更新立场，可控参数包括证据吸收率和先验锚定。在DEBATE数据集上的实验表明，该方法能可靠地塑造立场动态，并为研究证据驱动型 deliberation 提供可配置的基础设施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current LLM-based agents simulating deliberative interactions produce transcripts that fail to explain stance changes. Observed movement may result from various factors (evidence uptake, anchoring, role drift, echoing, or changed context), making it difficult to understand deliberation dynamics.

Method: The Belief Engine (BE) uses structured memory to extract arguments, with stance updated via a log-odds rule controlled by two key parameters: evidence uptake (u) and prior anchoring (a). This provides an auditable, evidence-level update trail across multiple base LLMs.

Result: Parameter sweeps demonstrate that the u and a controls reliably shape stance dynamics across multiple LLMs while preserving an evidence-level update trail. On DEBATE dataset, BE best reconstructs participants whose final stance follows extracted evidence, while stable and evidence-opposed cases indicate anchoring or external factors.

Conclusion: BE provides configurable infrastructure for studying evidence-grounded deliberation, enabling explicit analysis of openness, commitment, convergence, and disagreement rather than relying on hidden prompt effects. It successfully separates evidence-driven updates from anchoring effects and external factors.

Abstract: LLM-based agents are increasingly used to simulate deliberative interactions such as negotiation, conflict resolution, and multi-turn opinion exchange. Yet generated transcripts often do not reveal why an agent's stance changes: movement may reflect evidence uptake, anchoring, role drift, echoing, or changed prompt and retrieval context. We introduce the Belief Engine (BE), an auditable belief-update layer that treats "belief" as an evidential state over a proposition and exposes it as scalar stance. BE extracts arguments into structured memory and updates stance with a log-odds rule controlled by evidence uptake u and prior anchoring a. Across multiple base LLMs, parameter sweeps show that these controls reliably shape stance dynamics while preserving an evidence-level update trail. On DEBATE, a human deliberation dataset with pre/post opinions, BE best reconstructs participants whose final stance follows extracted evidence; stable and evidence-opposed cases instead point to anchoring or factors outside the extracted evidence stream. BE provides configurable infrastructure for studying evidence-grounded deliberation, where openness, commitment, convergence, and disagreement can be tied to explicit update assumptions rather than hidden prompt effects.

</details>


### [155] [X-SYNTH: Beyond Retrieval -- Enterprise Context Synthesis from Observed Human Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15505)
*Guruprasad Raghavan,George Nychis,Rohan Narayana Murthy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: X-SYNCH是一个企业上下文合成框架，通过人类注意力（数字化可观察的交互特征）而非传统检索方法来解决AI agent的上下文获取问题。框架为每个工作者建立Digital Twin Signature (DTS)，动态选择七种注意力过滤器之一，在销售线索识别任务上将TLR从9.5%提升至61.9%（6.5倍），FLR从90.5%降至18.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前企业AI agent面临的核心问题：上下文分散在多个系统中，现有检索方法仅通过匹配请求内容与存储信息进行检索，对复杂agentic任务效果较差。关键痛点包括：(1) 真正线索率(True Lead Rate, TLR)低；(2) 假线索率(False Lead Rate, FLR)高；(3) 模型缺乏改进机制。根本原因在于：合成质量取决于"应该暴露什么以及如何解释"——这属于组织、团队和个人层面的特定知识，存在于行为模式中，无法被任何检索索引捕获。

Method: X-SYNTH框架基于人类注意力，利用每个工作者的数字化可观察交互特征进行建模。核心创新包括：(1) Digital Twin Signature (DTS)：为每个个体建模行为基线；(2) 七种注意力过滤器（Proportional, Inverse, Differential, Recurrent, Comparative, Sequential, Collective）：根据个体和查询动态选择；(3) 四阶段pipeline：组装基于行为模式排序的上下文，而非基于查询嵌入。

Result: 在销售线索识别任务上测试效果显著： frontier模型单独使用时TLR仅为9.5%，FLR高达90.5%。使用X-SYNTH增强后，TLR提升至61.9%（提升6.5倍），FLR降至18.8%。无外部标签情况下，与正向结果相关的行为轨迹可与负向结果区分。

Conclusion: 企业上下文合成不是检索问题，而是相关性（relevance）问题，人类注意力是其最可靠的ground truth。X-SYNTH通过行为模式而非查询嵌入来组装上下文，为复杂agentic任务提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: In enterprise operations, the context required for an AI agent task is scattered across systems of record, static information stores, and communication channels. What is stored is system state, a lossy representation of the work that actually happened [2, 52]. The prevailing approach [17, 31, 34, 36] retrieves by matching request content to what is stored; for narrow requests this works well. But synthesis quality depends on knowing what to surface and how to interpret it: knowledge specific to each organization, team, and individual [5, 57, 61], present in behavioral patterns, absent from any retrieval index. For complex agentic tasks it breaks down: True Lead Rate is low, False Lead Rate is high, and the model has no mechanism to improve. We present X-SYNTH, a framework for enterprise context synthesis grounded in human attention, the digitally observable interaction signatures of each worker, encoding not just what they did but the sequence in which they did it, along with implicit reward signals. Behavioral traces preceding positive outcomes are distinguishable from those that did not, without external labeling. X-SYNTH models each individual's behavioral baseline as a Digital Twin Signature (DTS) and selects among seven qualitatively distinct attention filters: Proportional, Inverse, Differential, Recurrent, Comparative, Sequential, and Collective, per individual and per query, to identify causally relevant activity signatures. A four-stage pipeline assembles ranked context grounded in behavioral patterns rather than query embeddings. On a sales lead identification task, a frontier model unaided achieves 9.5% True Lead Rate (TLR) with 90.5% False Lead Rate (FLR). Augmented with X-SYNTH, TLR rises to 61.9% (6.5x) while FLR falls to 18.8%. Enterprise context synthesis is not a retrieval problem. It is a relevance problem, and human attention is its most reliable ground truth.

</details>


### [156] [Formal Methods Meet LLMs: Auditing, Monitoring, and Intervention for Compliance of Advanced AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16198)
*Parand A. Alamdari,Toryn Q. Klassen,Sheila A. McIlraith*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究AI治理中的一个关键维度：如何在AI开发生命周期（从预部署测试到后部署审计）中监控和审计AI系统。研究提出结合形式化方法与机器学习的技术，实现对黑盒AI系统（特别是LLM）行为约束的离线审计和运行时监控。实验表明，基于LTL的方法优于LLM基线方法，且小型标注器可匹配或超越前沿LLM评判器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统（尤其是LLM）在关键领域的广泛应用，如何有效监控和审计AI行为成为重要挑战。现有方法难以处理时序扩展的行为约束（如安全约束、规范、规则和法规），且对黑盒系统的审计能力有限。

Method: 1. 利用LTL的形式化语法和语义进行行为约束建模；2. 设计离线审计技术用于预部署测试；3. 开发在线（运行时）监控技术，包括预测性监控（基于采样的方法）和干预性监控（运行时预防和缓解违规）；4. 对黑盒LLM进行行为验证。

Result: 1. 基于LTL的审计和监控技术在检测时序行为约束违规方面显著优于LLM基线方法；2. 小型模型标注器可匹配或超越前沿LLM评判器；3. 预测性和干预性监控显著降低LLM智能体的违规率，同时基本保持任务性能；4. LLM时序推理能力随事件距离、约束数量和命题数量增加而明显下降。

Conclusion: 形式化方法与机器学习的结合为AI治理提供了有效的技术路径。LTL能够精确表达时序行为约束，结合轻量级监控技术可实现对复杂AI系统的可靠审计与实时监控。研究揭示了当前LLM在时序推理方面的局限性，提示需在架构层面改进时序建模能力。

Abstract: We examine one particular dimension of AI governance: how to monitor and audit AI-enabled products and services throughout the AI development lifecycle, from pre-deployment testing to post-deployment auditing. Combining principles from formal methods with SoTA machine learning, we propose techniques that enable AI-enabled product and service developers, as well as third party AI developers and evaluators, to perform offline auditing and online (runtime) monitoring of product-specific (temporally extended) behavioral constraints such as safety constraints, norms, rules and regulations with respect to black-box advanced AI systems, notably LLMs. We further provide practical techniques for predictive monitoring, such as sampling-based methods, and we introduce intervening monitors that act at runtime to preempt and potentially mitigate predicted violations. Experimental results show that by exploiting the formal syntax and semantics of Linear Temporal Logic (LTL), our proposed auditing and monitoring techniques are superior to LLM baseline methods in detecting violations of temporally extended behavioral constraints; with our approach, even small-model labelers match or exceed frontier LLM judges. Our predictive and intervening monitors significantly reduce the violation rates of LLM-based agents while largely preserving task performance. We further show through controlled experiments that LLMs' temporal reasoning shows a pronounced degradation in accuracy with increasing event distance, number of constraints, and number of propositions.

</details>


### [157] [Context Pruning for Coding Agents via Multi-Rubric Latent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15315)
*Jingjing Wang,Xiwen Chen,Wenhui Zhu,Huayu Li,Zhengxiao He,Feiyang Cai,Ana S. Carreon-Rascon,Xuanzhao Dong,Feng Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LaMR is a structured context pruning framework for LLM coding agents that decomposes code relevance into two interpretable dimensions (semantic evidence and dependency support), each with dedicated CRF transition dynamics and query-conditioned MoE gating, achieving superior performance while reducing token usage.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing learned pruners use a single-objective sequence labeler that collapses all facets of code relevance into one score, creating a modeling bottleneck where a single CRF transition prior cannot handle heterogeneous retention patterns (contiguous semantic spans and sparse structural support lines).

Method: LaMR decomposes code relevance into two quality dimensions (semantic evidence and dependency support), each with a dedicated CRF for dimension-specific transition dynamics. A MoE gating network weights per-rubric emissions conditioned on the query, with supervision derived via AST-based program analysis without additional annotation cost.

Result: LaMR frequently matches or outperforms unpruned full-context baselines. Achieves 12/16 head-to-head wins on multi-turn comparisons, saves up to 31% more tokens on multi-turn tasks, and improves Exact Match by up to +3.5 on single-turn tasks.

Conclusion: LaMR outperforms baselines in 12/16 head-to-head comparisons, saves up to 31% more tokens, and improves Exact Match by up to +3.5, while denoising the context frequently enhances performance.

Abstract: LLM-powered coding agents spend the majority of their token budget reading repository files, yet much of the retrieved code is irrelevant to the task at hand. Existing learned pruners compress this context with a single-objective sequence labeler, collapsing all facets of code relevance into one score and one transition matrix. We show that this formulation creates a modeling bottleneck: a single CRF transition prior must serve heterogeneous retention patterns, including contiguous semantic spans and sparse structural support lines. We propose LaMR (Latent Multi-Rubric), a structured pruning framework that decomposes code relevance into two interpretable quality dimensions, semantic evidence and dependency support, each modeled by a dedicated CRF with dimension-specific transition dynamics. A mixture-of-experts gating network dynamically weights the per-rubric emissions conditioned on the query, and a final CRF layer on the fused emissions produces the aggregate keep-or-prune decision. To supervise each dimension without additional annotation cost, we derive multi-rubric labels from the existing training corpus via AST-based program analysis, simultaneously denoising the teacher's binary labels. By effectively filtering distracting noise, LaMR frequently matches or even outperforms unpruned full-context baselines. Experiments on four benchmarks (SWE-Bench Verified, SWE-QA, LCC, LongCodeQA) show that LaMR wins 12 of 16 head-to-head multi-turn comparisons. It saves up to 31% more tokens on multi-turn agent tasks and improves Exact Match by up to +3.5 on single-turn tasks, while performance is frequently enhanced by denoising the context, and any remaining drops are marginal.

</details>


### [158] [Zero-Shot Goal Recognition with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15333)
*Kin Max Piamolini Gusmão,Nathan Gavenski,Nir Oren,Felipe Meneguzzi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地对前沿LLM在经典PDDL基准上进行零样本目标识别评估。研究发现LLM在目标识别上的能力不均衡：部分模型能够随证据积累而提升表现，另一部分则始终受限于世界知识先验。定性分析表明这种差异源于证据整合能力的根本不同，而非领域熟悉度差异。因此，目标识别可作为评估LLM基础规划知识的有效基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在经典规划任务上的能力可能依赖于世界知识利用而非真正的符号推理；目标识别作为一种补全式推理任务，评估与世界知识的一致性而非生成新动作序列，可能更适合LLM的优势

Method: 对前沿LLM进行零样本评估，在经典PDDL基准上测试其作为目标识别器的表现

Result: 不同模型表现差异显著：部分模型能随证据量增加而提升性能，在完整观测下接近基于地标的准确率；其他模型则始终受限于世界知识先验，无法有效整合额外证据

Conclusion: 目标识别被定位为评估LLM基础规划知识的 principled benchmark；不同模型在证据整合能力上存在根本性差异，这，而非领域熟悉度，决定了目标识别的表现。

Abstract: Large language models have recently reached near-parity with classical planners on well-known planning domains, yet this competence relies on world-knowledge exploitation rather than genuine symbolic reasoning. Goal recognition is a complementary abductive task structurally better suited to LLM strengths: it consists of evaluating consistency with world knowledge rather than generating novel action sequences. This paper provides the first systematic zero-shot evaluation of frontier LLMs as goal recognisers on key classical PDDL benchmarks. Our results show that LLM competence on goal recognition is uneven: some models scale with evidence and approach landmark-based accuracy at full observations, while others remain anchored to world-knowledge priors regardless of how much evidence accumulates. Qualitative analysis of model reasoning traces reveals that this divergence reflects a fundamental difference in evidence integration rather than domain familiarity. These findings position goal recognition as a principled benchmark for the foundational planning knowledge of LLMs.

</details>


### [159] [From LLM-Generated Conjectures to Lean Formalizations: Automated Polynomial Inequality Proving via Sum-of-Squares Certificates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15445)
*Ruobing Zuo,Hanrui Zhao,Gaolei He,Zhengfeng Yang,Jianlin Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出NSPI框架，通过结合LLM的启发式猜想能力与符号计算的精确推导能力，以及Lean的形式化验证，实现多项式不等式证明的端到端流水线。该方法在保持强证明保证的同时，解决了纯符号方法的可扩展性问题，能够处理最多10个变量的大规模多项式不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯符号方法虽提供强保证但随着变量数或度数增加可扩展性差（代价昂贵的代数运算和快速膨胀的中间表达式）；LLM引导方法在少量变量的问题上表现良好，但需解决可扩展性问题。

Method: 提出NSPI神经符号框架：首先利用LLM生成近似多项式SOS分解猜想，再通过符号计算将其精化为精确的多项式SOS表示以直接证明目标不等式，最后在Lean中形式化验证证明。

Result: 实验表明该方法在最多达10个变量的多项式基准测试上有效且可扩展。

Conclusion: NSPI框架在包含最多10个变量的多项式基准测试上展现了高效性和可扩展性，实现了从启发式发现到机器验证证明的端到端流水线，为大规模多项式不等式证明提供了新思路。

Abstract: Automated proving of polynomial inequalities is a fundamental challenge in automated mathematical reasoning, where rich algebraic structure and a rapidly growing certificate search space hinder scalability. Purely symbolic approaches provide strong guarantees but often scale poorly as the number of variables or the degree increases, due to expensive algebraic manipulations and rapidly growing intermediate expressions. In parallel, LLM-guided methods have made notable progress, particularly on competition-style inequalities with a small number of variables. To address the remaining scalability challenges, we propose NSPI, a neuro-symbolic framework that combines the complementary strengths of LLMs and symbolic computation for polynomial-inequality proving. Concretely, an LLM proposes a conjecture in the form of an approximate polynomial Sum-Of-Squares (SOS) decomposition; we refine it via symbolic computation to obtain an exact polynomial SOS representation, which directly proves the target inequality, and we further certify the proof in Lean, yielding an end-to-end pipeline from heuristic discovery to machine-checked proof. Experiments on challenging benchmarks involving polynomials with up to 10 variables demonstrate the effectiveness and scalability of the proposed method.

</details>


### [160] [CAPS: Cascaded Adaptive Pairwise Selection for Efficient Parallel Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15513)
*Fangzhou Lin,Shuo Xing,Peiran Li,Siyuan Yang,Qianwen Ge,Kazunori Yamada,Ziming Zhang,Haichong Zhang,Zhengzhong Tu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CAPS proposes a cascaded adaptive pairwise selection framework that reduces verifier compute by ~50% per candidate by non-uniformly allocating evidence viewing and comparison distribution across a four-stage cascade.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Pairwise self-verification is costly: each judgment reads two complete solutions, and existing methods perform fixed number of comparisons regardless of informativeness. This inefficiency limits scalability of test-time compute allocation.

Method: Four-stage cascade with evidence axis (adaptive partial evidence) and distribution axis (adaptive comparison spreading), plus optional rescue subroutine. Adopts closed-form verifier-token cost analysis.

Result: Outperforms leading pairwise verifier on 14/20 benchmark suites, uses 25.4% of verifier-token budget, outperforms pointwise self-verification on all 20 suites.

Conclusion: CAPS achieves comparable or superior performance to leading pairwise verifiers while using only 25.4% of the verifier-token budget, with interpretable diagnostics for cascade suitability pre-deployment.

Abstract: Parallel reasoning, where a generator samples many candidate solutions and an aggregator selects the best, is one of the most effective forms of test-time scaling in large language models, and pairwise self-verification has become its strongest aggregation primitive. Yet pairwise verification carries a heavy cost: each judgment reads two complete solutions in full, and existing methods perform tens of such judgments per problem regardless of whether the comparison is informative. We introduce CAPS (Cascaded Adaptive Pairwise Selection), an inference-only framework that allocates verifier compute non-uniformly along two orthogonal axes: an evidence axis that adapts how much of each candidate the judge sees, and a distribution axis that adapts how comparisons are spread across the pool. CAPS instantiates these into a four-stage cascade with an optional rescue subroutine, and admits a closed-form verifier-token cost in which the per-candidate marginal cost is roughly halved relative to uniform full-evidence schedules. On four self-verifying models (Qwen3-14B, GPT-OSS-20B, Qwen3-4B-Instruct/Thinking) and five reasoning benchmarks spanning code (LiveCodeBench-v5/v6, CodeContests) and math (AIME 2025, HMMT 2025), CAPS outperforms the leading pairwise verifier on 14 of 20 suites while using 25.4% of its verifier-token budget on code, and outperforms pointwise self-verification on all 20. The trade-off suites admit an interpretable diagnostic in terms of the verifier's accuracy at partial versus full evidence, providing a concrete pre-deployment check for cascade suitability.

</details>


### [161] [RTL-BenchMT: Dynamic Maintenance of RTL Generation Benchmark Through Agent-Assisted Analysis and Revision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15537)
*Jing Wang,Shang Liu,Hangan Zhou,Zhiyao Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RTL-BenchMT，一个用于动态维护RTL生成基准测试的智能体框架，旨在解决现有基准测试中的缺陷案例和过度拟合两大挑战，通过自动化方式降低人工维护成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前RTL基准测试面临两个关键挑战：基准测试中存在缺陷案例，以及模型对基准测试的过度拟合。这两个问题难以通过纯人工工程方式解决。

Method: 提出RTL-BenchMT智能体框架，包含两个核心功能模块：缺陷案例识别与修订、过度拟合案例检测与更新。

Result: 对有缺陷和过度拟合的案例进行了全面深入分析，生成了精炼的基准测试套件。

Conclusion: 通过RTL-BenchMT框架自动化维护RTL生成基准测试，显著降低人工成本，生成高质量基准套件并计划开源。

Abstract: This paper introduces RTL-BenchMT, an agentic framework for dynamically maintaining RTL generation benchmarks. Large Language Models (LLMs) assisted automated RTL generation is one of the most important directions in EDA research. However, current RTL benchmarks face two critical challenges: (1) flawed cases in the benchmarks and (2) overfitting to the benchmarks. Both challenges are difficult to resolve purely by manual engineering effort. To address these issues and systematically reduce human maintenance costs, we propose an automated agentic framework, RTL-BenchMT. RTL-BenchMT focuses on two key applications: (1) automatically identifying and revising flawed benchmark cases and (2) automatically detecting and updating overfitting cases. With the assistance of RTL-BenchMT, we conduct a thorough, in-depth analysis of flawed and overfitting cases and produce a refined benchmark suite that will be open-sourced to the community.

</details>


### [162] [DRS-GUI: Dynamic Region Search for Training-Free GUI Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15542)
*Yichao Liu,Huawen Shen,Liu Yu,Shiyu Liu,Zeyu Chen,Yu Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: GUI agents powered by Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated impressive capability in understanding and executing user instructions. However, accurately grounding instruction-relevant elements from high-resolution screenshots cluttered with irrelevant UI components remains challenging for existing approaches. Inspired by how humans dynamically adjust their perceptual scope to locate task-related regions on complex screens, we propose DRS-GUI, a training-free dynamic region search framework for GUI grounding that can be seamlessly integrated into existing MLLMs. DRS-GUI introduces a lightweight UI Perceptor that performs three human-like perceptual actions (Focus, Shift, and Scatter) to progressively explore the interface and generate region proposals. To dynamically schedule these actions, we further design an Action Planner based on Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS). A region quality reward is employed to evaluate and select the highly instruction-relevant region, efficiently pruning redundant UI elements. Experiments demonstrate that DRS-GUI yields a 14\% improvement on ScreenSpot-Pro for general and GUI-specific MLLMs (Qwen2.5-VL-7B and UGround-V1-7B), significantly enhancing grounding performance and generalization.

</details>


### [163] [Position: Artificial Intelligence Needs Meta Intelligence -- the Case for Metacognitive AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15567)
*Sergei Chuprov,Richard D. Lange,Leon Reznik,Paulo Shakarian,Raman Zatsarenko,Dmitrii Korobeinikov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文主张将"元认知"作为AI设计的通用原则，使系统能够自我监控状态并根据问题难度智能分配资源。通过结合资源理性AI和认知科学策略，识别了嵌入这些策略的具体挑战，并以联邦学习为案例验证了其实用价值，同时开发了配套软件框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI系统在准确性、安全性和效率方面仍面临挑战，需要一种通用的设计原则来系统性解决这些问题，使AI系统能够智能地自我调节资源分配。

Method: 整合资源理性AI和认知科学中的元认知策略，提出通用设计原则；通过联邦学习（FL）案例研究验证概念；开发专门的软件框架以支持元认知赋能AI应用的设计、部署和实验。

Result: 在联邦学习场景中验证了元认知原则能够提升学习效率、效果和安全性；开发了支持元认知AI应用开发的软件框架。

Conclusion: 文章提出将元认知作为AI设计的通用原则，通过FL案例验证了其实用价值，并开发了专门的软件框架供社区使用，为后续研究提供了工具支持。

Abstract: This position paper argues for metacognition as a general design principle for creating more accurate, secure, and efficient AI. The metacognitive solution involves systems monitoring their own states and judiciously allocating resources depending on each problem instance's difficulty or cost of mistakes. Drawing inspiration both from past work on resource-rational AI and from well-documented metacognitive strategies in psychology and cognitive science, we identify specific challenges in embedding these strategies into AI design and highlight open theoretical and implementation problems. We showcase these principles through a tangible example of improved learning efficiency, effectiveness, and security in a Federated Learning (FL) case study. We show how these principles can be translated into practice with a novel software framework developed specifically to allow the community to design, deploy, and experiment with metacognition-enabled AI applications.

</details>


### [164] [STAR: A Stage-attributed Triage and Repair framework for RCA Agents in Microservices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15581)
*Junle Wang,Xingchuang Liao,Wenjun Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: STAR是一种基于阶段归因的RCA（根因分析）智能体错误修复框架，通过将RCA工作流分解为四个结构化阶段（证据包、假设集、分析结构、决策报告），实现阶段级错误定位与针对性修复，而非将错误视为端到端问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-based RCA agents在微服务AIOps中的可靠性脆弱，早期阶段的错误（证据收集、假设生成或因果分析）会沿推理链传播并最终污染最终诊断结果。

Method: STAR基于LangGraph构建，包含四个核心机制：(1)四阶段结构化分解：EP(Evidence Package)、HS(Hypothesis Set)、AS(Analysis Structure)、DR(Decision Report)；(2)阶段级审计机制；(3)预算感知的Fast/Slow Routing策略；(4)基于反事实候选评估的决定性阶段定位；(5)阶段特定的patch-and-replay修复。

Result: 在公开大规模基准测试和真实生产数据集上，使用2种RCA agent工作流和3种基础模型进行评估。STAR在根因定位和故障类型分类上均优于强基线；能高精度识别决定性故障阶段；大多数初始错误轨迹在1-2轮重放中即可修复；Fast/Slow Routing和反事实阶段评估均带来显著收益。

Conclusion: 显式建模RCA agent"在哪里"失败是构建可靠、可调试、自修复的agentic RCA系统的有效途径。

Abstract: LLM-based root cause analysis (RCA) agents have recently emerged as a promising paradigm for incident diagnosis in microservice AIOps. However, their reliability remains fragile: an error in early evidence collection, hypothesis formulation, or causal analysis can propagate through the reasoning trace and eventually corrupt the final diagnosis. In this paper, we present \textbf{STAR}, a \emph{Stage-attributed Triage and Repair} framework for repairing erroneous RCA traces. STAR explicitly decomposes an RCA workflow into four structured stages, namely \emph{Evidence Package} (EP), \emph{Hypothesis Set} (HS), \emph{Analysis Structure} (AS), and \emph{Decision Report} (DR), and treats agent failure as a stage-localizable reasoning bug rather than a monolithic end-to-end error. Built on top of LangGraph, STAR performs stage-wise auditing, budget-aware \emph{Fast/Slow Routing}, \emph{decisive stage localization via counterfactual candidate evaluation}, and stage-specific patch-and-replay repair.
  We evaluate STAR on a public large-scale benchmark and a real-world production dataset, using two RCA agent workflows and three foundation models. Experimental results show that STAR consistently improves both root cause localization and fault type classification over strong baselines. Moreover, STAR identifies the decisive faulty stage with high accuracy, repairs most initially incorrect traces within one or two replay rounds, and benefits substantially from both Fast/Slow Routing and counterfactual stage evaluation. These results suggest that explicitly modeling \emph{where} an RCA agent fails is an effective path toward reliable, debuggable, and self-repairing agentic RCA systems.

</details>


### [165] [See Before You Code: Learning Visual Priors for Spatially Aware Educational Animation Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15585)
*Yuejia Li,Ke He,Junheng Li,Shutong Chen,Jingkang Xia,Zhiyue Su,Junchi Zhang,Mang Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出OmniManim框架，解决大语言模型生成教育动画代码时的视觉缺陷检测与修复问题。通过引入Vision Agent进行视觉规划和后渲染诊断，实现高质量教育动画的自动生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型生成的教育动画代码在执行后常出现元素重叠、对齐错误和动画连续性断裂等问题，这些缺陷仅从代码层面无法可靠检测，必须在渲染后才能发现。

Method: OmniManim采用共享场景状态、显式视觉规划、结构化后渲染诊断和局部修复机制。Vision Agent作为任务特定的视觉规划模块，通过粗到细的边界框去噪预测稀疏关键帧布局，并优化插值感知目标以减少下游动画插值导致的中间帧失败。

Result: 在EduRequire-500数据集上，OmniManim相比单模型基线和现有多智能体框架在渲染质量上均有提升。消融实验证实显式视觉规划（尤其是粗空间先验、边界框细化和插值感知优化）是性能提升的核心。

Conclusion: 通过结合视觉规划与渲染反馈机制，OmniManim有效解决了教育动画生成中的视觉质量问题，为可执行教育内容的自动生成提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Large language models can generate executable code for educational animations, but the resulting renders often exhibit visual defects, including element overlap, misalignment, and broken animation continuity. These defects cannot be reliably detected from the code alone and become apparent only after execution. We formalize this problem as render-feedback-aware constrained code generation: given a natural language specification, the model must generate executable code whose rendered output satisfies structured quality criteria that can be evaluated only after rendering. To address this problem, we introduce OmniManim, a render-feedback-aware educational animation generation framework built around a shared scene state, explicit visual planning, structured post-render diagnostics, and localized repair. Within OmniManim, the Vision Agent is a task-specific visual planning module: it predicts sparse keyframe layouts with coarse-to-fine bounding-box denoising and optimizes an interpolation-aware objective to reduce intermediate-frame failures induced by downstream animation interpolation. We further construct two datasets, ManimLayout-1K and EduRequire-500, and provide a reproducible evaluation protocol covering executability, instructional quality, visual quality, and efficiency. On EduRequire-500, OmniManim improves measured render quality over both single-model baselines and existing multi-agent frameworks. Systematic ablation studies further verify that explicit visual planning, especially its coarse spatial prior, bounding-box refinement, and interpolation-aware optimization, is central to these gains.

</details>


### [166] [TopoEvo: A Topology-Aware Self-Evolving Multi-Agent Framework for Root Cause Analysis in Microservices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15611)
*Junle Wang,Xingchuang Liao,Wenjun Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TopoEvo is a topology-aware multi-agent framework for root cause analysis in microservices that addresses symptom-amplification bias through MOMA-based multimodal alignment, VQ-based symptom tokenization, and a Hypothesis-Evidence-Test workflow with self-evolving mechanisms for drift robustness.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Root cause analysis in microservices faces three key challenges: noisy heterogeneous multimodal observability (metrics, logs, traces), cascading failure propagation amplifying downstream symptoms, and non-stationary topology drift from autoscaling/updates. Existing LLM-based RCA agents are topology-agnostic and suffer from symptom-amplification bias, misattributing root causes to downstream victims.

Method: The framework employs three core mechanisms: (1) MOMA for modality alignment and stable node representation; (2) VQ-based symptom tokenization with a symptom lexicon for auditable retrieval; (3) Multi-agent HET workflow for propagation-consistent hypothesis verification. A self-evolving mechanism provides hierarchical incident memory and test-time adaptation.

Result: The framework achieves stable node representations through contrastive multimodal alignment, enables reliable retrieval via discretized symptom tokens, explicitly separates initiating anomalies from amplified downstream symptoms, and maintains robustness under topology drift through conservative test-time adaptation.

Conclusion: TopoEvo proposes an integrated solution combining graph representation learning with structured reasoning for robust RCA in dynamic microservice environments. The framework aims to mitigate symptom-amplification bias through explicit topology-constrained verification and maintains robustness under drift via self-evolving mechanisms with pseudo-label adaptation.

Abstract: Root cause analysis (RCA) in microservices is challenging due to (i) noisy and heterogeneous multimodal observability (metrics, logs, traces), (ii) cascading failure propagation that amplifies downstream symptoms, and (iii) non-stationary topology drift induced by autoscaling and rolling updates. Recent LLM-based RCA agents can generate tool-grounded explanations, yet they often remain topology-agnostic and suffer from \emph{symptom-amplification bias}, misattributing the root cause to salient downstream victims. We propose \textbf{TopoEvo}, a topology-aware self-evolving multi-agent framework that couples graph representation learning with structured, topology-constrained reasoning. TopoEvo first introduces \emph{Metric-orthogonal Multimodal Alignment} (MOMA), which decomposes metric embeddings into complementary subspaces and contrastively aligns logs and traces to reduce modality redundancy and sparsity, yielding stable node representations for graph encoding. It then applies \emph{Vector Quantization} (VQ) to discretize topology-enhanced states into auditable \emph{symptom tokens} with a symptom lexicon, enabling reliable retrieval and token-level evidence grounding. On top of these discrete topology cues, TopoEvo performs a multi-agent \emph{Hypothesis--Evidence--Test} (HET) workflow to explicitly verify propagation-consistent explanations and separate initiating anomalies from amplified downstream symptoms. Finally, a \emph{Self-Evolving Mechanism} refreshes hierarchical incident memory and performs conservative test-time adaptation with high-confidence pseudo-labels to maintain robustness under drift.

</details>


### [167] [ColPackAgent: Agent-Skill-Guided Hard-Particle Monte Carlo Workflows for Colloidal Packing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15625)
*Lijie Ding,Changwoo Do*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ColPackAgent是一个基于Model Context Protocol (MCP)的智能体框架，用于自主执行胶体堆积蒙特卡洛模拟。该框架通过将HOOMD-blue硬粒子蒙特卡洛模拟器封装为colpack Python包，并结合四阶段工作流协议，使LLM智能体能够可靠地执行而非仅描述复杂科学模拟流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: General-purpose LLM agents tend to merely describe colloidal packing simulation workflows rather than reliably executing them. Without dedicated simulation tools and workflow instructions, autonomous execution of these complex scientific simulations remains challenging.

Method: ColPackAgent framework using Model Context Protocol (MCP) tool server and agent skill. The MCP server exposes a colpack Python package wrapping HOOMD-blue hard-particle Monte Carlo simulator, while the agent skill encodes a four-stage workflow contract. The framework supports three operational modes: interactive with human feedback, autonomous end-to-end execution, and autoresearch from a program file. Evaluation conducted using 17 stage-specific prompts across multiple LLMs.

Result: Demonstrated successfully across multiple scenarios: cube particles in 3D, binary system of disks and capsules in 2D, and 2D hard-disk freezing transition via autoresearch. Benchmark results provide stage-level assessment of different LLMs' reliability in following setup, planning, and analysis workflows.

Conclusion: Pairing a domain-specific Python package with MCP tools and a portable agent skill provides a practical route for converting simulation toolkits into agent-assisted research workflows. This approach enables reliable execution of complex simulations rather than mere descriptions of workflows.

Abstract: We introduce ColPackAgent, an agent framework that autonomously runs Monte Carlo simulations of colloidal packing through a Model Context Protocol (MCP) tool server and an agent skill, whether as a standalone agent or inside an existing agent system. By harnessing the MCP server and agent skill, ColPackAgent executes a structured workflow for colloidal packing simulations, which are central to studies of phase behavior, self-assembly, and materials design. Without dedicated simulation tools and workflow instructions, general-purpose Large Language Model (LLM) agents tend to describe such workflows rather than execute them reliably. The MCP server exposes a custom-built colpack Python package that wraps HOOMD-blue hard-particle Monte Carlo, and the skill encodes a four-stage workflow contract. ColPackAgent can carry out the workflow interactively with human feedback, autonomously from an end-to-end prompt, or as autoresearch following a provided program file. We demonstrate the system in different modes with several colloidal packing simulation examples such as cube particles in 3D, a binary system of disks and capsules in 2D, and the 2D hard-disk freezing transition using autoresearch. We also compare model performance on this workflow across a panel of LLMs with 17 stage-specific prompts. This benchmark provides a stage-level check of how reliably different models follow the setup, planning, and analysis workflow. Together, these results show that pairing a domain Python package with MCP tools and a portable agent skill provides a practical route for turning a simulation toolkit into an agent-assisted research workflow.

</details>


### [168] [Nudging Beyond the Comfort Zone: Efficient Strategy-Guided Exploration for RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15726)
*Chanuk Lee,Sangwoo Park,Minki Kang,Sung Ju Hwang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NudgeRL提出一种在可验证奖励强化学习(RLVR)中进行结构化多样性探索的框架，通过策略级上下文条件化 rollout 和统一目标函数（分解为上下文间/内组件并包含蒸馏目标）来克服探索受限问题，在数学推理任务上以更少计算成本超越大规模rollout和oracle引导方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前RLVR范式的有效性受限于探索能力——策略只能从已采样的轨迹中改进。增加rollout数量虽能缓解但计算代价高昂，而现有修改优化目标的方法对探索控制有限。核心问题：如何高效引导策略探索多样化推理轨迹？

Method: 1) 策略引导(Strategy Nudging)：使用轻量级策略级上下文(conditioning)来诱导多样化推理轨迹，无需昂贵的神谕监督。2) 统一目标函数：将奖励信号分解为上下文间(inter-context)和上下文内(intra-context)组件，并加入蒸馏目标将发现的行为迁移回基础策略。

Result: NudgeRL在5个挑战性数学基准上：1) 以远少于标准GRPO的rollout预算(约1/8)达到同等或更优性能；2) 平均性能超越使用oracle引导的RL基线方法。

Conclusion: 结构化的、上下文驱动的探索可作为高效可扩展的替代方案，同时优于"暴力扩展rollout"和"基于特权信息的可行性导向"两类方法，展示了策略级条件化在引导多样性探索中的有效性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has emerged as a scalable paradigm for improving the reasoning capabilities of large language models. However, its effectiveness is fundamentally limited by exploration: the policy can only improve on trajectories it has already sampled. While increasing the number of rollouts alleviates this issue, such brute-force scaling is computationally expensive, and existing approaches that modify the optimization objective provide limited control over what is explored. In this work, we propose NudgeRL, a framework for structured and diversity-driven exploration in RLVR. Our approach introduces Strategy Nudging, which conditions each rollout on lightweight, strategy-level contexts to induce diverse reasoning trajectories without relying on expensive oracle supervision. To effectively learn from such structured exploration, we further propose a unified objective, which decomposes the reward signal into inter- and intra-context components and incorporates a distillation objective to transfer discovered behaviors back to the base policy. Empirically, NudgeRL outperforms standard GRPO with up to 8 times larger rollout budgets, while outperforming oracle-guided RL baseline on average across five challenging math benchmarks. These results demonstrate that structured, context-driven exploration can serve as an efficient and scalable alternative to both brute-force rollout scaling and feasibility-oriented methods based on privileged information. Our code is available at https://github.com/tally0818/NudgeRL.

</details>


### [169] [Can We Trust AI-Inferred User States. A Psychometric Framework for Validating the Reliability of Users States Classification by LLMs in Operational Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15734)
*Izabella Krzeminska,Michal Butkiewicz,Ewa Komkowska*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究验证了大语言模型评估用户状态时指标可靠性的基本假设，发现大多数AI评估指标在个体层面缺乏稳定性，仅适用于聚合分析而非实时适应系统；但可作为事后分析工具识别交互模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型用于评估对话式自适应系统中的用户状态时，假设所用指标在个体评分层面是稳定且可解释的，但这一假设尚未得到实证验证

Method: 采用复制评估程序，在三个多模态大语言模型（GPT-4o audio、Gemini 2.0 Flash、Gemini 2.5 Flash）上评估一组广泛指标的重复性；同时分析个体评分可靠性和聚合可靠性，以区分实时适应场景与仅在聚合分析中有价值的指标

Result: 仅31/213个指标满足可靠性标准；个体评分层面的不稳定性排除了将此类分数作为实时自适应系统中用户状态指标的可能性；个体层面不稳定的指标在事后研究中仍可识别交互规则及其与用户体验参数（满意度、信任度、参与度）的关系

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一套可复现的评估框架来衡量指标的适用性，推动了自适应系统中更负责任的AI设计；仅31/213个指标满足可靠性标准，说明AI评估指标的稳定性问题普遍存在，需要在解释结果时明确验证可靠性并持续监控

Abstract: The use of large language models to assess user states in conversational and adaptive systems is based on the assumption that the metrics used for such assessment are stable and interpretable at the level of individual scores. This paper empirically tests this assumption, focusing on the psychometric reliability of artificial intelligence (AI) measures of user states.
  This study employed replication evaluation procedures to assess the repeatability of a broad set of metrics across three different bimodal large language models (GPT-4o audio, Gemini 2.0 Flash, Gemini 2.5 Flash). Analyses include both individual score reliability and aggregated reliability, allowing us to distinguish metrics potentially useful for real-time adaptation from those that retain their value only in aggregated analyses.
  The results demonstrate that metric reliability cannot be considered a default property in interpretive domains. The lack of stability at the level of individual scores precludes the interpretation of such scores as indicators of user state in real-time adaptive systems, even if these metrics demonstrate stability after aggregation. At the same time, the study indicates that individually unstable metrics can retain analytical utility in post-hoc studies, identifying rules governing interactions and their relationships with user experience parameters such as satisfaction, trust, and engagement.
  The main contribution of this work, besides quantifying the severity of the problem (only 31 of 213 metrics met the criteria), is the proposal of a replicable evaluation framework, enabling measurable evaluations of metric applicability. This approach supports more responsible AI design of adaptive systems, in which the interpretation of results requires explicit validation of reliability and monitoring for violations over time.

</details>


### [170] [SaaS-Bench: Can Computer-Use Agents Leverage Real-World SaaS to Solve Professional Workflows?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15777)
*Kean Shi,Zihang Li,Tianyi Ma,Zengji Tu,Jialong Wu,Xinbo Xu,Qingyao Yang,Ruoyu Wu,Weichu Xie,Ming Wu,Jason Zeng,Michael Heinrich,Elvis Zhang,Liang Chen,Kuan Li,Baobao Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SaaS-Bench是一个基于23个SaaS系统的专业工作流基准测试，涵盖6个领域106项任务，用于评估计算机使用智能体。实验表明当前最强LLM模型仅能完成不到4%的端到端任务，揭示了现有智能体在复杂专业环境中的重大能力缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Web和GUI智能体基准测试通常依赖简化设置、孤立任务或短时域交互，难以评估智能体在现实专业工作流程中的能力。SaaS环境是评估CUA的理想选择，因其承载了大量现代数字化工作，自然涉及动态系统状态、跨应用协调、领域特定知识和长时域依赖。

Method: 构建了SaaS-Bench基准，包含23个可部署的SaaS系统跨越6个专业领域，共106个任务。这些任务要求长时域执行，涵盖纯文本和多模态设置，采用加权验证检查点评估任务完成度和部分进展。

Result: 代表性LLM智能体在SaaS-Bench上表现挣扎，即使是能力最强的模型也仅能端到端完成不到4%的任务，暴露出在规划、状态跟踪、跨应用上下文维护和错误恢复方面的不足。

Conclusion: SaaS-Bench成功揭示了当前CUA系统在复杂专业工作流程中的显著能力差距。即使是表现最强的模型也仅能完成不到4%的端到端任务，暴露了规划、状态跟踪、跨应用上下文维护和错误恢复等方面的局限性。

Abstract: Computer-Using Agents (CUAs) are rapidly extending large language models (LLMs) beyond text-based reasoning toward action execution in more complex environments, such as web browsers and graphical user interfaces (GUIs). However, existing web and GUI agent benchmarks often rely on simplified settings, isolated tasks, or short-horizon interactions, making it difficult to assess capabilities of agents in realistic professional workflows. Software-as-a-Service (SaaS) environments are a natural choice for CUA evaluation, as they host a large share of modern digital work and naturally involve dynamic system states, cross-application coordination, domain-specific knowledge, and long-horizon dependencies. To this end, we introduce SaaS-Bench, a benchmark built on 23 deployable SaaS systems across six professional domains, containing 106 tasks grounded in realistic work scenarios. These tasks require long-horizon execution, cover both text-only and multimodal settings, and are evaluated with weighted verification checkpoints that measure strict task completion and partial progress. Experiments show that representative LLM-based agents struggle on SaaS-Bench, with even the strongest model completing fewer than 4% of tasks end-to-end, exposing limitations in planning, state tracking, cross-application context maintenance, and error recovery. Code are available at https://github.com/UniPat-AI/SaaS-Bench for reproduction.

</details>


### [171] [Imperfect World Models are Exploitable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15960)
*Logan Mondal Bhamidipaty,Esmeralda S. Whitammer,David Abel,Mykel J. Kochenderfer,Subramanian Ramamoorthy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出模型exploitation的形式化定义并建立与reward hacking的统一定义，发现大策略集上exploitation不可避免，有限策略集的unhackability条件不适用于exploitation，最终通过relaxed exploitation概念和safe horizon为安全规划提供边界条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始reward hacking的不可能性证明无法直接迁移到exploitation问题，且现有有限策略集的unhackability条件是否也适用于exploitation尚不清楚，需要建立统一理论来理解世界模型中规划安全性问题。

Method: 提出模型exploitation的形式化定义（世界模型与真实环境对策略偏好产生矛盾），构建了reward hacking和model exploitation的统一理论框架，开发了超越原有不可能性证明的广义分析方法，并引入relaxed exploitation概念与安全 horizon 界限。

Result: 证明了在大策略集上exploitation本质不可避免，reward hacking是该框架的特例；有限策略集下unhackability条件无法对应防止exploitation的条件；提出relaxed exploitation概念并推导出可避免的安全 horizon。

Conclusion: 研究建立了reward hacking与model exploitation之间的形式化桥梁，揭示了在大策略集上 exploitation 本质上不可避免，而在有限策略集中防止exploitation的条件不存在。这些发现明确了世界模型安全规划的边界，为该领域后续研究提供了理论基座。

Abstract: We propose a novel definition of model exploitation in reinforcement learning. Informally, a world model is exploitable if it implies that one policy should be strictly preferred over another while the environment's true transition model implies the reverse. We analogize our definition with a prior characterization of reward hacking but show that the associated proof of inevitability does not transfer to exploitation. To overcome this obstruction, we develop a general theory of reward hacking and model exploitation that proves that exploitation is essentially unavoidable on large policy sets and yields the corresponding claim for hacking as a special case. Unfortunately, we also find that the conditions that guarantee unhackability in finite policy sets have no counterpart that precludes exploitation. Consequently, we introduce a relaxed notion of exploitation and derive a safe horizon within which it can be avoided. Taken together, our results establish a formal bridge between reward hacking and model exploitation and elucidate the limits of safe planning in world models.

</details>


### [172] [Deterministic Event-Graph Substrates as World Models for Counterfactual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15967)
*Fabio Rovai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Event-graph substrates formalize a class of world models using append-only RDF triple logs for counterfactual reasoning. The approach achieves strong symbolic performance on CLEVRER and a new Smallville counterfactual benchmark, though falls short of parametric methods on predictive and counterfactual tasks.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
