Motivation: Current counterfactual reasoning methods often lack inspectability, exact counterfactual support, and transferability across domains. This work aims to create a unified framework that addresses these limitations through event-graph substrates.

Method: Represent agent state as append-only logs of typed RDF triples and answer counterfactual queries by forking the log under a structured intervention vocabulary. The method reduces explanatory and counterfactual queries to the same causal-ancestor traversal through a formal duality proof.

Result: On CLEVRER (n=75,618), substrate exceeds NS-DR symbolic oracle on all four question categories (by 9.89, 20.26, 17.65, 0.80 pp). Against parametric baselines, it exceeds ALOE on descriptive/explanatory but lags on predictive/counterfactual. On twin-EventLog benchmark, exceeds Llama-3.1-8B by 18.80 points joint accuracy.

Conclusion: Event-graph substrates provide a promising symbolic approach to counterfactual reasoning that achieves strong results on visual reasoning benchmarks and new small-scale counterfactual tasks, though performance gaps remain against parametric methods on predictive and counterfactual tasks.

Abstract: We study event-graph substrates: a class of world models that represent agent state as an append-only log of typed RDF triples and answer counterfactual queries by forking the log under a structured intervention vocabulary. Substrates are inspectable at the triple level, support exact counterfactuals, and transfer across domains without learned components. We formalize the class, prove a duality between explanatory and counterfactual queries that reduces both to the same causal-ancestor traversal, and evaluate a 1,400-line CLEVRER-DSL interpreter atop a domain-agnostic substrate runtime at full CLEVRER validation scale (n=75,618). The substrate exceeds the NS-DR symbolic oracle on all four per-question categories (by 9.89, 20.26, 17.65, and 0.80 percentage points), and exceeds the parametric ALOE baseline on descriptive and explanatory while lagging on predictive and counterfactual. We also introduce twin-EventLog, a 500-specification Park-canonical Smallville counterfactual benchmark on which the substrate exceeds Llama-3.1-8B with full context by 18.80 points joint accuracy.

</details>


### [173] [Learning Bilevel Policies over Symbolic World Models for Long-Horizon Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15975)
*Dillon Z. Chen,Till Hofmann,Toryn Q. Klassen,Sheila A. McIlraith*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BISON提出双层策略框架，结合低层模仿学习与高层符号抽象，在长时域规划任务上展现出优异的泛化能力和效率优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前具身AI智能体面临长时域规划难题。模仿学习虽擅长低层精细操作与控制，但难以生成长时域规划；而高层符号抽象虽利于长时域规划但缺乏底层控制能力。

Method: 本文提出双层策略(π^hl, π^ll)，由从低层演示学习的神经策略π^ll和由低层演示的符号抽象与归纳泛化构建的高层符号策略π^ll组成，在BISON系统中实现。

Result: 在MetaWorld基准测试中，BISON在长时域和更多物体数量的任务上泛化能力优于VLA及端到端方法，训练和推理的时间与内存效率均更高。忽略低层执行时，高层策略可在1分钟内解决含10,000个相关对象的问题。

Conclusion: BISON通过结合低层模仿学习与高层符号抽象，有效解决了长时域规划问题。该方法在泛化能力、训练和推理效率方面均优于VLA和端到端方法，高层策略能够在1分钟内解决包含10,000个相关对象的问题。

Abstract: We tackle the challenge of building embodied AI agents that can reliably solve long-horizon planning problems. Imitation learning from demonstrations has shown itself to be effective in training robots to solve a diversity of complex tasks requiring fine motor control and manipulation over low-level (LL), continuous environments. Yet, it remains a difficult endeavour to generate long-horizon plans from imitation learning alone. In contrast, high-level (HL), symbolic abstractions facilitate efficient and interpretable long-horizon planning. We propose to combine the strengths of LL imitation learning for manipulation and control, and HL symbolic abstractions for long-horizon planning. We realise this idea via \emph{bilevel policies} of the form $(π^{\mathrm{hl}}, π^{\mathrm{ll}})$, consisting of a neural policy $π^{\mathrm{ll}}$ learned from LL demonstrations, and an HL symbolic policy $π^{\mathrm{hl}}$ that is constructed from symbolic abstractions of the LL demonstrations combined with inductive generalisation. We implement these ideas in the BISON system. Experiments on extended MetaWorld benchmarks demonstrate that BISON generalises to long horizons and problems with greater numbers of objects than those solved by VLA and end-to-end methods, and is more time and memory efficient in training and inference. Notably, when ignoring LL execution, BISON's HL policies can solve HL problems with 10,000 relevant objects in under a minute. Project page: https://dillonzchen.github.io/bison

</details>


### [174] [Petri Net Induced Heuristic Search for Resource Constrained Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15983)
*Ido Lublin,Dor Atzmon,Izack Cohen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将RCPSP建模为基于资源的时间转移Petri网的可达图最优搜索问题，使用A*算法结合关键路径和资源下界启发式求解，在PSPLIB基准测试中优于SCIP和CBC等强MILP基线方法，且发现启发式搜索和MILP沿不同轴退化（资源紧度 vs 公式规模）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The Resource-Constrained Project Scheduling Problem (RCPSP) is a fundamental optimization problem in operations research, and improving exact solution methods for this NP-hard problem is of significant practical importance.

Method: Formulated RCPSP as optimal search over reachability graph of Timed Transition Petri Net with Resources using relative-delay tokens, solved with A* guided by heuristic combining Critical Path and resource-based lower bounds, proved consistency under token-based time semantics.

Result: Outperforms strong exact MIP baselines (SCIP, CBC) on PSPLIB benchmarks in both success rate and solve time.

Conclusion: Heuristic search (A*) and MIP degrade along independent axes: resource tightness for A* and formulation size for MIP, with resource strength mediating which solver benefits from scale.

Abstract: We formulate the Resource-Constrained Project Scheduling Problem (RCPSP) as optimal search over the reachability graph of a Timed Transition Petri Net with Resources, using relative-delay tokens so that scheduling decisions correspond to transition firings in the induced state space. We solve the resulting problem with $A^*$ guided by a heuristic that combines Critical Path and resource-based lower bounds, and prove that it is consistent under our token-based time semantics. Experiments on the PSPLIB benchmarks show that the approach outperforms strong exact Mixed-Integer Linear Programming (MIP) baselines (SCIP, CBC) in both success rate and solve time. Per-instance analysis shows that heuristic search and MIP degrade along independent axes, resource tightness for $A^*$ and formulation size for MIP, with resource strength mediating which solver benefits from scale.

</details>


### [175] [ScreenSearch: Uncertainty-Aware OS Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16024)
*Michael Solodko,Justin Wagle*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ScreenSearch是一个用于大规模桌面探索的系统，通过结构化屏幕检索、去重和歧义感知PUCT图bandit，在11个应用上收集100万+截图和3万+去重状态，证明状态识别、提议质量、歧义感知搜索三者缺一不可


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 桌面GUI智能体在部分可观测环境下操作：视觉相似的屏幕可能对应不同的工作流状态，局部合理的动作可能导致截然不同的结果，这本质上是一个计算机/操作系统状态探索问题

Method: 将UIA树转换为位置感知结构特征，通过稀疏token搜索和元数据过滤器索引相关屏幕，在VM workers间维护共享去重状态图；定义基于匹配动作结果分散性的可扩展歧义信号，结合前沿奖励驱动PUCT图bandit进行大规模探索和重放启动策略评估

Result: 在11个桌面应用上收集超过100万张截图和3万多个去重状态；发现在固定的replay-start切片上存在明显的novelty-ambiguity权衡；更强的提议先验可显著提升独特状态发现

Conclusion: 状态识别、提议质量和歧义感知搜索在决定何时探索和何时提交时都很重要；强先验可提升独特状态发现；歧义减少本身不是充分的探索目标

Abstract: Desktop GUI agents operate under partial observability: visually similar screens can correspond to different underlying workflow states, so locally plausible actions can lead to sharply different outcomes. We frame this as a problem of computer/OS state exploration, where effective behavior requires both expanding the reachable frontier and reducing ambiguity before committing. We present ScreenSearch, a system that combines structural screen retrieval and deduplication with an ambiguity-aware PUCT graph-bandit for large-scale desktop exploration. The retrieval layer converts UIA trees into location-aware structural features, indexes related screens through sparse token search and metadata filters, and maintains a shared deduplicated state graph across VM workers. On top of this graph, we define a scalable ambiguity signal based on matched-action outcome dispersion. If similar screens produce different next states under the same action signature, the state should be probed further rather than treated as resolved. We use this signal together with frontier rewards to drive large-scale exploration and replay-start policy evaluation over the shared graph. Across 11 desktop applications, ScreenSearch collects over 1M screenshots and over 30K deduplicated states, yielding large exploration corpora with substantial cross-application and within-application diversity. On a fixed replay-start slice, we observe a clear novelty--ambiguity trade-off: some policies reduce ambiguity quickly while discovering little frontier. Ambiguity reduction alone is therefore not a sufficient exploration objective. Appendix ablations show that stronger proposal priors can materially improve unique-state discovery during corpus building. These results suggest that state identity, proposal quality, and ambiguity-aware search all matter when deciding when to probe and when to commit.

</details>


### [176] [Reasoners or Translators? Contamination-aware Evaluation and Neuro-Symbolic Robustness in Tax Law](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16052)
*Parisa Kordjamshidi,Samer Aslan,Madhavan Seshadri,Leslie Barrett,Enrico Santus*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究评估了LLM在税法推理中的表现，发现传统LLM的性能可能被数据污染高估。通过对比单体LLM与神经符号混合系统，发现后者在法律推理的可靠性和泛化能力上表现更优，证实了法律推理的组合性特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在自动化法律推理方面取得了显著进展，但尚不清楚其表现是真正的法律推理能力还是数据污染的产物。

Method: 实现了一套污染检测协议以严格评估LLM可靠性；构建了新型测试套件，通过案例和规则变化测试对未见文档的泛化能力；对比了单体LLM与将成文法条翻译为形式化表示并委托给符号求解器的混合系统。

Result: 研究表明污染可以夸大模型性能；法律推理本质上是组合性的；神经符号框架更加可靠和稳健。

Conclusion: Neuro-symbolic frameworks为法律人工智能提供了更可靠、更稳健的基础，并在未见情况中展现出更好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have significantly enhanced automated legal reasoning. Yet, it remains unclear whether their performance reflects genuine legal reasoning ability or artifacts of data contamination. We present a comprehensive empirical study of tax law reasoning approaches and implement a contamination detection protocol to rigorously assess LLM reliability. We show that performance can be inflated by contamination. Building on this analysis, we conduct a systematic evaluation, comparing monolithic LLMs with hybrid systems that translate statutory text into formal representations and delegate inference to symbolic solvers. We build a novel test suite designed to probe generalization to unseen documents via case and rule variations. Our findings indicate that legal reasoning is inherently compositional and that neuro-symbolic frameworks offer a more reliable and robust foundation for legal AI, as well as improved generalization to unobserved situations.

</details>


### [177] [Sign-Separated Finite-Time Error Analysis of Q-Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16103)
*Donghwan Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: This paper provides a sign-separated finite-time error analysis for constant step-size Q-learning by decomposing errors into positive and negative components, identifying a structural asymmetry caused by the max operator that leads to overestimation, and establishing separate convergence bounds for both components.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Standard Q-learning analysis often treats positive and negative errors symmetrically, but the maximum operator in the Bellman equation creates inherent asymmetry in error dynamics that is not well-characterized in existing finite-time bounds.

Method: The methodology decomposes the Q-learning error into componentwise negative and positive parts using a switching-system representation, where the negative part is analyzed via a lower comparison LTI system associated with the optimal policy, and the positive part is controlled by a linear switching system.

Result: The bounds demonstrate that the negative-side LTI certificate converges no slower than the positive-side switching certificate and can produce a faster exponential envelope, revealing that positive action-wise errors are preferentially selected and propagated by the Bellman maximum.

Conclusion: The max-induced asymmetry in error dynamics reveals a structural advantage in Q-learning's positive-side error propagation, and finite-time bounds for both deterministic and stochastic constant-step-size recursions are established.

Abstract: This paper develops a sign-separated finite-time error analysis for constant step-size Q-learning. Starting from the switching-system representation, the error is decomposed into its componentwise negative and positive parts. The negative part is dominated by a lower comparison linear time-invariant (LTI) system associated with a fixed optimal policy, whereas the positive part is controlled by a linear switching system. The resulting bounds show that the negative-side LTI certificate is no slower than the positive-side switching certificate and may produce a faster exponential envelope. The analysis identifies a max-induced asymmetry in Q-learning error dynamics. This asymmetry is connected to overestimation: positive action-wise errors can be selected and propagated by the Bellman maximum, whereas negative errors admit an optimal-policy lower comparison. Finite-time bounds are provided for both deterministic and stochastic constant-step-size recursions.

</details>


### [178] [ShopGym: An Integrated Framework for Realistic Simulation and Scalable Benchmarking of E-Commerce Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16116)
*Chinmay Savadikar,Mingyu Zhao,Yuanzheng Zhu,Han Li,Shuang Xie,Alberto Castelo,Tianfu Wu,Lingyun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ShopGym框架，用于电子商务网页智能体的现实模拟和可扩展基准测试，通过ShopArena模拟层和ShopGuru任务合成器解决现有评估方法的现实性与可控性之间的矛盾，并在224个任务上验证了合成商店与真实商店的结构相似性和评估信号一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前电子商务网页智能体评估面临两难选择：真实网店提供现实性但不可控、难以检验且不可复现；手工构建的沙盒基准测试可控但覆盖范围狭窄。核心瓶颈在于缺乏一种可扩展的方法来构建同时具备真实性、多样性、可控性、可检验性和可复现性的评估环境。

Method: ShopGym框架包含两个核心组件：1) ShopArena模拟层，通过匿名化商店规范和阶段性验证生成流程，将真实种子网店转换为自包含的沙盒商店；2) ShopGuru任务合成器，跨七个技能类别合成基准测试任务，基于商店目录、导航结构、政策和交互能力进行任务锚定。采用图结构分析和智能体行为评估进行验证。

Result: 在6个沙盒商店（3个合成数据、3个真实数据）上生成224个任务进行评估，结果表明合成商店保留了真实网店的关键结构属性，且智能体在合成商店上的表现与在真实商店上的表现呈正相关。

Conclusion: 实验验证了框架有效性，合成商店保留了真实商店的关键结构属性，且智能体在合成商店与真实商店上的表现呈正相关。

Abstract: Developing and evaluating e-commerce web agents requires environments that preserve meaningful task structure while enabling controllable, reproducible, and scalable scientific comparison. Existing methodologies force a tradeoff: live storefronts provide realism but are non-stationary, difficult to inspect, and irreproducible, while hand-built sandbox benchmarks provide control but cover only a narrow range of layouts, catalogs, policies, and interaction patterns. We argue that the core bottleneck is methodological: the field lacks a scalable way to construct evaluation settings that are simultaneously realistic, diverse, controllable, inspectable, and reproducible. We introduce ShopGym, an integrated framework for realistic simulation and scalable benchmarking of e-commerce web agents. ShopGym is a framework for constructing e-commerce simulation environments and grounded benchmark tasks. Its simulation layer, ShopArena, converts live seed storefronts into self-contained sandbox shops through anonymized shop specifications and a staged, validated generation process. On top of these simulated storefronts, ShopGuru synthesizes benchmark tasks across seven skill categories, grounding each task in the shop's catalog, navigation structure, policies, and interaction affordances. Together, ShopArena and ShopGuru produce self-contained, resettable, inspectable, and stable evaluation artifacts that preserve structural properties and agent-evaluation signals relevant to shopping tasks. We validate the framework through graph-based structural analysis and agent-based behavioral evaluation with 224 generated tasks across six sandbox shops: three constructed with synthetic data and three with real data. Our results show that the synthetic shops preserve key structural properties of live storefronts, with agent performance on synthetic shops positively correlated with performance on live storefronts.

</details>


### [179] [Property-Guided LLM Program Synthesis for Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16142)
*Augusto B. Corrêa,André G. Pereira,Jendrik Seipp*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出属性引导的LLM程序综合方法，通过形式化属性检查替代简单的数值评分，在属性违反时提供具体反例指导LLM修复。在PDDL规划领域合成启发式函数的实验中，相比最佳基线方法，生成程序数量减少七倍，计算评估成本降低数个数量级，且能解决更多无需搜索的任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM程序综合依赖简单数值评分（如解决方案价值或通过测试数量）来指示程序质量，但评分无法提供程序失败原因的指导，导致系统需要生成和评估大量候选程序，造成高昂的LLM推理和评估成本。

Method: 属性引导的LLM程序综合方法：在程序评估后检查其是否满足形式化定义的属性；违反属性时提前停止评估并提供具体反例。通过反例引导的修复循环，在训练集上检查属性并返回第一个违反属性的案例。应用于PDDL规划领域，合成直接启发式函数。

Result: 在十个规划领域和分布外测试集上的评估表明：合成的启发式函数在几乎所有测试任务上都是有效的直接启发式；相比最佳先前生成方法，平均每个领域生成的程序数量减少七倍；能在更多任务上无需搜索直接求解；评估候选程序的计算成本降低数个数量级。

Conclusion: 只要问题存在可验证的属性，属性引导的LLM综合方法就能降低成本并提高程序质量。该方法通过形式化属性检查和反例反馈，能有效指导LLM生成更强的程序。

Abstract: LLMs have shown impressive success in program synthesis, discovering programs that surpass prior solutions. However, these approaches rely on simple numeric scores to signal program quality, such as the value of the solution or the number of passed tests. Because a score offers no guidance on why a program failed, the system must generate and evaluate many candidates hoping some succeed, increasing LLM inference and evaluation costs. We study a different approach: property-guided LLM program synthesis. Instead of scoring programs after evaluation, we check whether a candidate satisfies a formally defined property. When the property is violated, we stop the evaluation early and provide the LLM with a concrete counterexample showing exactly how the program failed. This feedback drastically reduces both the number of program generations and the evaluation cost, and can guide the LLM to generate stronger programs. We evaluate this approach on PDDL planning domains, asking the LLM to synthesize direct heuristic functions: every state reachable by strictly improving transitions has a strictly improving successor. A heuristic with this property leads hill-climbing algorithm directly to a goal state. A counterexample-guided repair loop generates one candidate program, checks the property over a training set, and returns the first case that violates the property. We evaluate our approach on ten planning domains with an out-of-distribution test set. The synthesized heuristics are effectively direct on virtually all test tasks, and compared to the best prior generation method our approach generates seven times fewer programs per domain on average, solves more tasks without using search, and requires several orders of magnitude less computation to evaluate candidates. Whenever a problem admits a verifiable property, property-guided LLM synthesis can reduce cost and improve program quality.

</details>


### [180] [Look Before You Leap: Autonomous Exploration for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16143)
*Ziang Ye,Wentao Shi,Yuxin Liu,Yu Wang,Zhengzhou Cai,Yaorui Shi,Qi Gu,Xunliang Cai,Fuli Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对大语言模型智能体在陌生环境中因"过早开发"而失败的问题，提出将自主探索能力作为构建自适应智能体的关键。研究引入可验证指标"探索检查点覆盖率"(ECC)来量化智能体的探索广度，并开发了结合任务执行与探索的交替训练策略，以及"先探索后行动"(Explore-then-Act)范式，证明了系统化探索学习对构建可泛化、适应真实世界的智能体至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM智能体在陌生环境中表现不佳，根源在于"过早开发"（premature exploitation）——在获取足够环境特定信息前就依赖先验知识行动。这反映出智能体缺乏系统化的自主探索能力，限制了其在开放世界中的适应性和泛化性。

Method: 1) 提出可验证指标"探索检查点覆盖率"(Exploration Checkpoint Coverage)，衡量智能体发现关键状态、对象和可供性的广度；2) 开发交替训练策略，交替执行任务执行rollout和探索rollout，分别由各自的可验证奖励优化；3) 提出"先探索后行动"范式(Explore-then-Act)，将信息收集与任务执行解耦：先利用交互预算获取环境知识，再用于任务解决。

Result: 系统评估表明，采用标准任务导向强化学习训练的智能体表现出狭隘且重复的行为模式，阻碍下游性能。通过学习系统化探索，智能体能够更广泛地发现环境中的关键元素，从而提升泛化能力和真实世界适应性。

Conclusion: 学习系统化探索是构建可泛化、真实世界可用智能体的必要条件。"先探索后行动"的范式通过将探索阶段与执行阶段解耦，有效解决了过早开发问题，为构建自适应智能体提供了新思路。

Abstract: Large language model based agents often fail in unfamiliar environments due to premature exploitation: a tendency to act on prior knowledge before acquiring sufficient environment-specific information. We identify autonomous exploration as a critical yet underexplored capability for building adaptive agents. To formalize and quantify this capability, we introduce Exploration Checkpoint Coverage, a verifiable metric that measures how broadly an agent discovers key states, objects, and affordances. Our systematic evaluation reveals that agents trained with standard task-oriented reinforcement learning consistently exhibit narrow and repetitive behaviors that impede downstream performance. To address this limitation, we develop a training strategy that interleaves task-execution rollouts and exploration rollouts, with each type of rollout optimized by its corresponding verifiable reward. Building on this training strategy, we propose the Explore-then-Act paradigm, which decouples information-gathering from task execution: agents first utilize an interaction budget to acquire grounded environmental knowledge, then leverage it for task resolution. Our results demonstrate that learning to systematically explore is imperative for building generalizable and real-world-ready agents.

</details>


### [181] [An Algebraic Exposition of the Theory of Dyadic Morality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16153)
*Kush R. Varshney*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将二元道德理论（TDM）进行形式化，使用结构因果建模识别三种心理操作符，并展示在AI政策设计中的具体应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为二元道德理论（TDM）提供代数形式的正式框架，TDM是一种基于简单二元模板（有意主体导致脆弱客体受到伤害）的道德判断心理学模型，现有理论缺乏形式化基础。

Method: 使用结构因果建模（SCM）符号形式化TDM，识别三个心理操作符（类型转换操作符、完成操作符、 valence依赖推理机制），通过节点崩溃和顺序处理解决扩展性问题，展示道德认知如何压缩多节点场景。

Result: 完成TDM的数学形式化；揭示道德认知通过节点崩溃和顺序处理压缩多节点场景的机制；开发出可应用于AI政策设计的具体工具（冲突义务检测、用户能动性保护、因果干预沟通设计）。

Conclusion: 提出三种具体应用（冲突义务检测、帮助性策略设计、故障后沟通干预）；推荐情境化心智感知测量方法；使神经符号AI系统能够以数学严谨且忠实于人类道德认知的方式计算道德问题。

Abstract: This paper provides an algebraic exposition of the theory of dyadic morality (TDM), a psychological model of moral judgment grounded in a simple two-node template: an intentional agent causing harm to a vulnerable patient. We formalize TDM using structural causal modeling (SCM) notation and identify three psychological operators (typecasting operator, completion operator, and valence-dependent inference mechanism) that extend standard SCM to capture how people compute moral judgments under constraints. We address scalability challenges arising from TDM's dyadic limitation, showing how moral cognition compresses multi-node scenarios through node collapse and sequential processing. Drawing on this algebraic framework, we demonstrate concrete applications to AI policy design: detecting conflicting obligations, structuring helpfulness policies to preserve user agency, and designing post-failure communication as causal interventions. Finally, we recommend scoped, contextual measurement of mind perception over universal averaging to operationalize the theory empirically. This algebraic formalization enables neurosymbolic AI systems to compute morality in a way that is both mathematically rigorous and faithful to human moral cognition.

</details>


### [182] [Fully Open Meditron: An Auditable Pipeline for Clinical LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16215)
*Xavier Theimer-Lienhard,Mushtaha El-Amin,Fay Elhassan,Sahaj Vaidya,Victor Cartier-Negadi,David Sasu,Lars Klein,Mary-Anne Hartley*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出首个医学领域的全开放 LLM-CDSS 训练流程 Meditron，通过开放完整训练栈（数据、管道、评估）替代传统仅开放权重的模式，显著提升模型可审计性与可复现性，并刷新全开放医学模型的最佳性能记录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于LLM的临床决策支持系统（CDSS）大多不透明：多数所谓"开放"模型仅开放权重，却未公开数据来源、处理流程和生成管道；医学领域尚无真正的全开放（Fully Open, FO）模型。

Method: 构建了首个全流程开放的医学大模型训练管道，包括：1) 临床医生审核的训练语料库（整合8个公开医学QA数据集并添加3类合成扩展）；2) 可复现的数据构建与训练框架（含系统级去污染、金标准重采样、4人医师小组端到端验证）；3) 对齐应用的评估协议（基于专家撰写临床 vignettes 的 LLM-as-judge 评估，经204名人工评分者校准）。该方案应用于5个全开放基座模型。

Result: 所有 MeditronFO 变体均优于其基座模型：Apertus-70B-MeditronFO 在医学基准测试上提升+6.6分（47.2%→53.8%），刷新 FO 领域最佳成绩；Gemma-3-27B-MeditronFO 在 LLM-as-judge 比较中以58.6%的偏好率胜出 MedGemma，且在 HealthBench 上表现更优（58% vs 55.9%）。

Conclusion: Fully开放流程可以在不牺牲可审计性和可重现性的前提下实现最先进的领域特定性能。

Abstract: Clinical decision support systems (CDSS) require scrutable, auditable pipelines that enable rigorous, reproducible validation. Yet current LLM-based CDSS remain largely opaque. Most "open" models are open-weight only, releasing parameters while withholding the data provenance, curation procedures, and generation pipelines that determine model behavior. Fully Open (FO) models, which expose the complete training stack end-to-end, do not currently exist in medicine. We introduce Fully Open Meditron, the first fully open pipeline for building LLM-CDSS, comprising a clinician-audited training corpus, a reproducible data construction and training framework, and a use-aligned evaluation protocol. The corpus unifies eight public medical QA datasets into a normalized conversational format and expands coverage with three clinician-vetted synthetic extensions: exam-style QA, guideline-grounded QA derived from 46,469 clinical practice guidelines, and clinical vignettes. The pipeline enforces system-wide decontamination, gold-label resampling of teacher generations, and end-to-end validation by a four-physician panel. We evaluate using an LLM-as-a-judge protocol over expert-written clinical vignettes, calibrated against 204 human raters. We apply the recipe to five FO base models (Apertus-70B/8B-Instruct, OLMo-2-32B-SFT, EuroLLM-22B/9B-Instruct). All MeditronFO variants are preferred over their bases. Apertus-70B-MeditronFO improves +6.6 points over its base (47.2% to 53.8%) on aggregate medical benchmarks, establishing a new FO SoTA. Gemma-3-27B-MeditronFO is preferred over MedGemma in 58.6% of LLM-as-a-judge comparisons and outperforms it on HealthBench (58% vs 55.9%). These results show that fully open pipelines can achieve state-of-the-art domain-specific performance without sacrificing auditability or reproducibility.

</details>


### [183] [Prospective multi-pathogen disease forecasting using autonomous LLM-guided tree search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16238)
*Sarah Martinson,Michael P. Brenner,Martyna Plomecka,Brian P. Williams,Nicholas G. Reich,Zahra Shamsi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于大语言模型（LLM）引导的树搜索自主系统，可自动生成、评估和优化传染病预测代码。在2025-2026年美国呼吸道疾病季节的前瞻性实时评估中，该系统自主发现了流感、COVID-19和RSV的多样化模型，其集成预测表现匹配或优于CDC人工策划的金标准集成模型，并成功处理了RSV的"冷启动"场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统传染病预测依赖专家团队人工策划模型，这种定制化开发模式限制了向细粒度地理分辨率或新发病原体的可扩展性，成为预测工作的瓶颈。

Method: 该系统采用LLM引导的树搜索方法，迭代生成、评估和优化可执行的预测软件。通过前瞻性实时评估，使用对数尺度距离度量防止奖励黑客（reward hacking），并引入自动化评判者回路确保对复杂科学理论的结构保真度。

Result: 系统成功自主发现了流感、COVID-19和RSV的方法多样化模型；集成这些机器生成模型后，在样本外预测中一致匹配或优于CDC金标准集成模型；在RSV数据稀缺"冷启动"场景中表现良好；对数尺度度量优化和评判者回路的有效性通过回顾性消融实验得到验证。

Conclusion: 该框架能将流行病学理论自主转化为准确、透明代码，克服了建模劳动力瓶颈，使在前所未有的规模上快速部署专家级疾病预测成为可能。

Abstract: Probabilistic forecasting of infectious diseases is crucial for public health but relies on labor-intensive manual model curation by expert modeling teams. This bespoke development bottlenecks scalability to granular geographic resolutions or emerging pathogens. Here, we present an autonomous system using Large Language Model (LLM)-guided tree search to iteratively generate, evaluate, and optimize executable forecasting software. In a fully prospective, real-time evaluation during the 2025-2026 US respiratory season, the system autonomously discovered methodologically diverse models for influenza, COVID-19, and respiratory syncytial virus (RSV). Aggregating these machine-generated models yielded an ensemble that consistently matched or outperformed the gold-standard, human-curated Centers for Disease Control and Prevention (CDC) hub ensembles out-of-sample. The system successfully navigated data-scarce "cold start" scenarios for RSV. Moreover, controlled retrospective ablations revealed that optimizing log-scale distance metrics prevents reward hacking, while an automated judge-in-the-loop ensures structural fidelity to complex scientific theories. By autonomously translating epidemiological theory into accurate, transparent code, this framework overcomes the modeling labor bottleneck, enabling rapid deployment of expert-level disease forecasting at unprecedented scales.

</details>


<div id='cs.CY'></div>

# cs.CY [[Back]](#toc)

### [184] [Validated Hypotheses as a Lens for Human-Likeness Evaluation in AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15473)
*Xuan Liu,HaoYang Shang,Zizhang Liu,Yuanjun Feng,Guankai Zhai,Yunze Xiao,Yiwen Tu,Haojian Jin*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该论文提出使用经过验证的行为假设作为评估 LLM 智能体人类相似性的透镜，通过 HumanStudy-Bench 平台将 12 项已发表的人类研究转化为模拟环境进行评估，发现 agent design 对对齐的影响大于模型规模，且影响呈非单调性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要客观、可分解、可扩展的方法来评估 LLM 智能体的人类行为模拟能力。现有方法缺乏可验证的标准和系统化的评估框架。

Method: 建立 HumanStudy-Bench 开放平台，将已发表的人类主体研究转化为可重用的模拟环境。使用两个评估指标：概率对齐分数(PAS)衡量推断一致性，效果一致性分数(ECS)衡量效应量一致性。对 10 个模型和 4 种 agent 设计进行评估。

Result: 智能体响应在完全复现和完全失败之间两极分化；agent design对对齐的影响比模型规模更大，但效果是非单调的。

Conclusion: HumanStudy-Bench 提供了一种客观、可分解、可扩展的方法来评估 LLM 智能体的人类对齐程度。研究发现 agent design 对对齐的影响比模型规模更重要，但这种影响是非单调的，表明当前 LLM agents 在模拟人类行为方面仍存在显著差距。

Abstract: We propose using validated behavioral hypotheses as a lens for evaluating human-likeness in LLM-based agents. Our key idea is simple: If an agent is human-like, a population of such agents should reach the same inferential conclusion as the human population when run through the same experiment. Decades of social science have produced many such validated findings, each anchored to concrete experimental protocols and robustly established through independent replication. This yields an evaluation that is objective, decomposable, and scalable. We operationalize this lens through HumanStudy-Bench, an open platform that turns published human-subject studies into reusable simulation environments and administers the evaluation to configurable agents. It scores agent-human alignment on two metrics: the Probability Alignment Score (PAS) for inferential agreement and the Effect Consistency Score (ECS) for effect-size agreement. We curated an initial suite of 12 studies whose hypotheses are robustly established through independent replication, and evaluated 10 models under 4 agent designs. Results show that agent responses polarize between full replication and complete failure; agent design influences alignment more than model scale, but its effect is non-monotonic.

</details>


### [185] [Who, Why, and How: Disentangling the Effects of Moderation Source, Context, and Language on Post-Removal Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16204)
*Siyi Zhou,Lindsay Young,Marlon Twyman,Emilio Ferrara*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该研究基于HAII-TIME理论，利用Reddit大规模数据（近1200万条 moderation 事件）考察 moderation 的来源（bot/human/modteam）、违规情境和语言风格如何共同影响用户后续行为。研究发现：bot moderation 比 human/modteam 产生更高合规率和更低自我审查；modteam moderation 导致最强自我审查效应；违规严重性是关键调节变量——在严重违规情境下，利他性框架和情感化表达更有效，而常规情境有效的策略（详细解释、社区级呼吁、直接称呼）可能适得其反。480个语言交互中33个通过FDR校正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将 content moderation 视为统一干预手段，忽视了 moderator 来源、违规情境和语言风格的交互效应，用户行为如何被 moderation 的多维度特征塑造尚不清晰。

Method: 大规模观察性数据集，包含2021-2025年 Reddit 上 11,795,036 条 moderation 事件、9,285,410 名用户和 61,261 个子板块。采用概率行为分类、ANOVA、OLS回归和 PCA 提取语言特征进行分析。

Result: Bot moderation 比 human/modteam 产生更高合规率和更低自我审查；modteam moderation 产生最强自我审查效应；违规严重性显著调节语言策略效果——常规情境下有效的策略在严重违规时可能适得其反；480个语言交互中33个通过FDR校正。

Conclusion: 将违规显著度（violation salience）引入 HAII-TIME 理论作为 cue-based processing 的调节变量，为情境适应性 moderation 设计提供实证依据。

Abstract: Content moderation is a central mechanism through which platforms attempt to balance user engagement with community governance. Yet existing research has largely treated moderation as a uniform intervention, overlooking how moderator source, violation context, and linguistic style jointly shape user behavior. Drawing on the Human--AI Interaction Theory of Interactive Media Effects (HAII-TIME), this study examines how these three dimensions produce divergent post-moderation behavioral trajectories in a large-scale observational dataset of 11,795,036 moderation events across 9,285,410 users and 61,261 subreddits on Reddit (2021--2025). Using probabilistic behavioral classification, ANOVA, and OLS regression with PCA-derived linguistic features, we find that bot moderation consistently produces higher compliance and lower self-censorship than human or modteam moderation, challenging the assumption that human agency cues are inherently advantageous. Modteam moderation produces the strongest self-censorship effects, suggesting that institutional depersonalization is a meaningful driver of behavioral withdrawal. Violation severity emerges as a critical contingency: linguistic strategies effective in routine contexts -- elaborated explanation, community-scale appeals, direct personal address -- can backfire for serious violations, whereas prosocially framed and emotionally emphatic messages become most effective when stakes are highest. Of 480 linguistic interactions tested, 33 survive FDR correction. These findings extend HAII-TIME by introducing violation salience as a moderator of cue-based processing, and offer empirical grounding for context-adaptive moderation design.

</details>


### [186] [Inside Baseball: The Automated Ball-Strike System as an Object Lesson in Technological Rule Enforcement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16237)
*Andrea Wen-Yi Wang,Waki Kamino,David Mimno,Karen Levy,Malte F. Jung*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文通过MLB的ABS系统七年实践案例，揭示即使明确定义的规则（如strike zone）在自动化过程中也面临复杂挑战。研究发现规则的技术实现与规则文本之间存在距离，这种距离源于strike zone的"ground truth"历史上存在争议（规则实践始终是规则定义与裁判执法的混合体），且技术系统嵌入在多方利益相关者的生态系统中。研究挑战了以规则与技术实现之间距离为中心的传统评估范式，呼吁采用深度社会科学方法评估技术在实践中的体验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLB的ABS系统经过七年实验才实现自动化判罚，看似简单的"准确判断投球与好球带距离"任务却花了七年，挑战了"明确定义的规则易于自动化和评估"的假设

Method: 使用科学技术研究（STS）的分析框架追踪ABS的设计决策，揭示规则到技术实现之间存在距离的两个原因：（1）strike zone的"ground truth"历史上存在争议——规则在实践中始终是规则书定义与裁判执法决策的混合体；（2）ABS的使用嵌入在现有生态系统中，技术执法系统的实现需要平衡多方利益相关者的价值

Result: 揭示"明确定义的规则"与"技术实现"之间存在距离；该距离源于strike zone的历史争议性（规则实践 = 规则书定义 + 裁判执法决策的混合体）以及ABS嵌入多方利益相关者的生态系统

Conclusion: 挑战了以规则与技术实现之间"距离"为中心的传统评估范式，呼吁采用深度社会科学方法来评估技术在实践中的体验，贡献于FAccT（公平、问责与透明度）领域关于sociotechnical系统实施与评估的持续讨论

Abstract: Clearly-defined rules are often assumed to be straightforward to automate and evaluate. We challenge this assumption through an in-depth study of Major League Baseball's (MLB) seven-year experimentation with the Automated Ball-Strike System (ABS). ABS is envisioned to call balls and strikes accurately: a seemingly straightforward use of technology to objectively determine the distance between a pitch and the strike zone. Although the strike zone is an area clearly defined in the rulebook, it took MLB seven years to figure out how to automate calling balls and strikes with ABS, showing how even seemingly straightforward rules require a complex translation process to operationalize via technological systems. In this paper, we trace the design decisions that led to the current implementation of ABS. Our case study reveals that "distance" exists even between a clear rule and its technological implementation. Using analytic frameworks from Science and Technology Studies (STS), we show that such distance exists because (1) historically, the "ground truth" of the strike zone is contested: the rule in practice has always reflected a hybrid between the rulebook definition and umpires' enforcement decisions; and (2) the use of ABS is embedded in an existing eco-system, where the implementation of a technological enforcement system needs to balance multiple stakeholder values. This perspective challenges conventional evaluation paradigms that center on the distance between a formalized rule and its technological implementation, and instead calls for evaluating how such systems are experienced in practice. Addressing this question requires in-depth social science approaches, contributing to ongoing conversations in FAccT about the implementation and evaluation of sociotechnical systems.

</details>


### [187] [AI-Mediated Communication Can Steer Collective Opinion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16245)
*Stratis Tsirtsis,Kai Rawal,Chris Russell,Brent Mittelstadt,Sandra Wachter*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: This paper investigates how generative AI biases — when embedded as mediators in human-to-human online communication — propagate through social networks and influence collective opinion. Using empirical audits, theoretical modeling, and platform-level investigations, the authors demonstrate that LLMs introduce directional biases that can amplify through networks, and call for regulatory attention (e.g., EU frameworks) to govern AI's role as an intermediary in public discourse.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: While prior research established that AI can express biased opinions and shape individual opinions in direct human-AI interaction, the influence of AI mediators on collective opinion formation during human-to-human communication remains underexplored — despite the growing integration of LLMs into online platforms that mediate social discourse.

Method: Three-pronged approach: (1) Empirical auditing of LLMs (multiple popular families) for directional biases when instructed to edit human-written texts on contested topics (gun control, atheism, abortion); (2) Mathematical modeling of opinion dynamics with AI-mediated communication on social networks, analytically characterized at equilibrium; (3) Simulation on real social network data and platform feature auditing ("Explain this post" on X/Grok).

Result: LLMs across popular families introduce consistent directional biases when editing texts on contested topics (e.g., nudging toward gun control, against atheism). These biases can amplify through social networks and shift collective opinion equilibria. Platform auditing revealed Grok's "Explain this post" feature exhibits pro-life bias on abortion content, traceable to specific design choices.

Conclusion: AI biases in human-to-human communication can amplify through social networks and shift collective opinion. Platform-level interventions (auditing tools like X's "Explain this post") can partially detect these biases, but systemic regulatory frameworks (e.g., EU legislation) are needed to address this emerging societal risk.

Abstract: Generative artificial intelligence (AI) is increasingly integrated into the online platforms where humans exchange opinions; large language models (LLMs) now polish users' posts on LinkedIn and provide context for content shared on X. While prior work has shown that AI can express biased opinions and shape individuals' opinions during human-AI interactions, less attention has been paid to its influence on collective opinion formation when mediating human-to-human communication. We address this gap via a combination of empirical and theoretical analyses. We show empirically that LLMs from multiple popular families introduce directional biases when instructed to edit human-written texts on contested topics, for example, nudging texts in favor of gun control and against atheism. Building on this observation, we introduce a mathematical model of opinion dynamics in which an AI system sits between users on a social network, transforming the opinions they express and perceive. By analytically characterizing the equilibrium of this model and performing simulations on real social network data, we show that biases introduced by AI in human-to-human communication can be amplified through the network and shift collective opinion in their direction. In light of these findings, we investigate whether such biases are controllable by online platforms. We audit the "Explain this post" feature on X and find evidence of pro-life bias in Grok's outputs on abortion-related content, which we trace back to specific design choices. We conclude with a discussion of the broader implications of our findings in relation to ongoing legislative efforts in the European Union.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [188] [A Few GPUs, A Whole Lotta Scale: Faithful LLM Training Emulation with PrismLLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15617)
*Shaoke Xi,ChonLam Lao,Boyi Jia,Jiaqi Gao,Zhipeng Zhang,Jiamin Cao,Brian Sutioso,Erci Xu,Minlan Yu,Kui Ren,Yong Li,Zhengping Qian,Ennan Zhai,Jingren Zhou*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: PrismLLM通过混合仿真技术，用不到1%的物理GPU实现对8192 GPU集群规模的高保真模拟迭代时间和内存行为，解决了大规模LLM训练中开发调试与生产资源访问困难之间的矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模LLM训练框架的开发、调试和性能优化需要频繁访问生产规模集群，但绝大多数GPU已被生产工作负载占用，导致独占访问愈发困难。现有方案（如复杂性能模型的仿真、按比例缩减的实验）难以捕捉规模依赖行为。

Method: PrismLLM采用基于切片的执行图构建方法，捕获目标规模下的计算、通信和依赖关系，然后执行混合仿真：选定的rank执行原始程序，其余rank作为虚拟参与者回放。

Result: 迭代时间平均误差仅0.58%，峰值GPU内存误差小于0.01%，可使用少于原部署1%的物理GPU模拟最多8192个GPU的集群。

Conclusion: PrismLLM successfully decouples large-scale execution from physical cluster constraints, enabling faithful emulation of up to 8192 GPUs with fewer than 1% of original physical resources while maintaining high accuracy.

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) training today runs on clusters spanning thousands of GPUs. While this scale enables rapid model advances, developing, debugging, and performance-tuning the training framework inevitably becomes complex and costly. This is because engineers often need to reproduce production behaviors to diagnose failures or evaluate optimizations, thereby demanding frequent and even exclusive access to production-scale clusters -- which becomes increasingly hard given that the majority of GPUs are already committed to production workloads. Simulation relies on complex performance models that are difficult to maintain, and downscaled experiments often fail to capture scale-dependent behaviors.
  We present PrismLLM to decouple large-scale execution from the need to access large clusters, enabling engineers to run and observe ranks of interest under faithful large-scale behavior using only a few GPUs. PrismLLM constructs a high-fidelity execution graph via a slicing-based approach that captures computation, communication, and dependencies of the target scale. Then, PrismLLM performs hybrid emulation where selected ranks execute the original program while the remaining ranks are replayed as virtual participants.
  Experiments on large-scale LLM training workloads show that PrismLLM accurately reproduces performance and memory behavior, achieving only 0.58\% average error in iteration time and less than 0.01\% error in peak GPU memory usage. PrismLLM can emulate clusters of up to 8192 GPUs using fewer than 1\% of the physical GPUs required by the original deployment.

</details>


### [189] [ParamSpMM: Adaptive and Efficient Sparse Matrix-Matrix Multiplication on GPUs for GNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15695)
*Lixing Zhang,Guanhua Ye,Hongzheng Li,Shigang Li,Yingxia Shao*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对GNN中SpMM算子难以适应多样化输入特征的问题，提出了ParamSpMM参数化方法。该方法通过新数据结构PCSR灵活整合优化技术，并配合ML-decider根据输入特征自适应选择最优配置，实现平均1.92x加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNN应用快速增长，SpMM是其关键算子，但现有SpMM设计难以适应多样化的输入特征，限制了GNN的训练效率。

Method: 提出了参数化方法：1) 设计了新的数据结构PCSR (Parameterized Compressed Sparse Row) 以灵活整合现有优化技术；2) 开发了ML-based SpMM-decider，根据精心设计的输入特征预测最优配置，实现自适应优化。

Result: ParamSpMM相比Nvidia cuSPARSE平均加速1.92x，显著提升了GNN训练效率。

Conclusion: ParamSpMM在GNN训练中实现了显著的性能提升，平均加速1.92倍，超越了Nvidia cuSPARSE。

Abstract: Fueled by the ability to mine real-world graph data, GNN applications have experienced phenomenal growth. Sparse Matrix-Matrix Multiplication (SpMM) is a critical operator in GNNs. However, existing SpMM designs for GNNs struggle to adapt to diverse input characteristics. In this paper, we first conduct a comprehensive analysis of existing SpMM optimizations, revealing their limitations through statistical and empirical evidence. Based on this analysis, we introduce ParamSpMM, a parametric approach for highly adaptive and efficient SpMM computation in GNNs. It incorporates a new data structure, the Parameterized Compressed Sparse Row (PCSR), to flexibly integrate existing optimization techniques. ParamSpMM enables the configuration of these optimization techniques according to various input characteristics. Furthermore, we complement ParamSpMM with an ML-based SpMM-decider that predicts optimal configurations based on carefully crafted input features. Our evaluations demonstrate that ParamSpMM outperforms Nvidia cuSPARSE with an average speedup of 1.92x, significantly enhancing GNN training efficiency.

</details>


### [190] [Scale: Deep Reinforcement Learning for Container Scheduling in Serverless Edge Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15704)
*Chen Chen,Zihan Jia,Andrea Sabbioni,Reza Farahani,Lei Jiao*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出Scale框架——一种面向无服务器边缘计算的SLO感知容器调度与资源分配框架，采用策略驱动的深度强化学习算法，综合考虑SLO约束、延迟和数据局部性，在保持接近最优解的同时实现毫秒级调度决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无服务器计算(Serverless)虽为边缘计算带来前景，但事件驱动模型在高度动态、异构、分布式的边缘系统中面临请求放置和资源管理的重大挑战，需要减少资源过度配置和不必要的数据移动。

Method: 采用基于策略的深度强化学习(DRL)算法，将SLO约束、端到端延迟和数据局部性联合纳入调度决策过程，实现稳定性与性能的平衡。

Result: Scale在华为云大规模真实数据集上验证，效果达到最优整数线性规划(ILP)求解器的1.11~1.15倍，同时决策时间减少达99%，实现了接近最优解与毫秒级响应的兼顾。

Conclusion: Scale通过结合DRL和传统优化的混合方法，在华为云大规模真实数据集上验证了其有效性。该框架在保持接近最优解的同时，将决策时间缩短至毫秒级（降低99%），具备显著的实用价值。

Abstract: Serverless computing has emerged as a promising computing paradigm for edge computing. However, adopting the event driven model in highly dynamic, heterogeneous, and distributed edge systems poses significant challenges in request placement and resource management. Efficiently allocating requests to containers is therefore critical to reduce resource over provisioning and unnecessary data movement. This paper proposes Scale, a Service Level Objective aware container scheduling and resource allocation framework designed for serverless edge computing. Scale employs a policy based deep reinforcement learning algorithm to balance system stability and performance under dynamic workloads. The design jointly incorporates SLO constraints, end to end latency, and data locality into the scheduling decision process. Extensive simulations using large scale real world datasets from Huawei Cloud demonstrate that Scale achieves solutions within a factor of 1.11 to 1.15 of a state of the art Integer Linear Programming solver, while reducing decision making time by up to 99%.

</details>


### [191] [ADAPT: A Self-Calibrating Proactive Autoscaler for Container Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15788)
*Himanshu Singh Baghel*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ADAPT是一种自适应冷启动时长预测的主动容器伸缩系统，通过EWMA在线估计和MPC动态规划视野调整，在多场景测试中实现了优于反应式方案和Prophet模型的SLA保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动伸缩依赖于准确预估配置延迟（冷启动时长），但该延迟在实际环境中存在显著差异，既可能在不同环境中不同，也可能在连续扩容事件中波动。

Method: ADAPT采用在线EWMA估计器实时追踪冷启动时长，并将动态规划视野（FH-OPT）输入模型预测控制器（MPC）以优化滚动窗口内的副本数，形成闭环主动伸缩系统。

Result: MPC+LSTM在6种工作负载类型、5个随机种子测试下，所有工作负载均实现低于5%的SLA违规；相比之下，反应式HPA违规率为7-19%，MPC+Prophet在双峰流量下高达28.7%。

Conclusion: ADAPT通过实时测量提供延迟，实现了闭环主动伸缩设计，MPC+LSTM在所有工作负载上实现低于5%的SLA违规，显著优于反应式HPA（7-19%）和MPC+Prophet（高达28.7%）。

Abstract: Proactive autoscaling for containerized workloads depends on knowing the provisioning delay, i.e., the time between a scaling decision and the moment new capacity is ready to serve traffic. In practice, this cold-start duration can vary substantially across environments and even across consecutive scale-out events. We present ADAPT (Adaptive Duration Approximation for Predictive Timing), an online EWMA estimator that tracks coldstart duration at runtime. ADAPT feeds a dynamic planning horizon, FH-OPT, into a Model Predictive Controller (MPC) that optimizes replica counts over a rolling window. Together, these components form a closed-loop proactive autoscaling design that adapts its lookahead based on measured provisioning delay. Evaluated across three policies (MPC+LSTM, MPC+Prophet, HPA) and six workload archetypes with five random seeds, MPC+LSTM achieves below 5% SLA violation on all workloads, compared with 7-19% for reactive HPA and up to 28.7% for MPC+Prophet on bimodal traffic.

</details>


### [192] [High-Performance Star-M SVD for Big Data Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16058)
*Md Taufique Hussain,Grey Ballard,Aditya Devarakonda,Srinivas Eswar,Naman Pesricha,Vishwas Rao*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种共享内存并行的高性能软件实现方案，用于在star-M张量框架下实现张量SVD分解，以实现大规模科学数据集的最优压缩。相比传统矩阵方法和现有生产力导向语言实现，该方案在保持最优性保证的同时，显著提升了计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大数据时代需要高效压缩大规模数据集并执行复杂数学运算，传统矩阵方法在压缩能力和精度方面存在局限性，现有star-M张量SVD的软件实现主要局限于生产力导向的编程语言，缺乏高性能并行化实现，限制了其在实际科学数据集上的应用。

Method: 在star-M张量框架下，采用矩阵模拟（matrix-mimetic）的方式对张量进行分解，利用star-M SVD实现张量的奇异值分解；开发共享内存并行架构以提升计算效率，实现高性能解决方案。

Result: 该软件实现能够对大规模科学数据集进行最优压缩，在特定类型数据上表现出色，同时保持了star-M SVD的理论最优性保证，相比传统矩阵方法具有更小的精度损失。

Conclusion: 该并行高性能软件的开发为大规模科学数据集的压缩与分析提供了有效工具，有望推动数据分析和科学发现的新突破。

Abstract: In the era of big data, effectively compressing large datasets while performing complex mathematical operations is crucial. Tensor-based decomposition methods have shown superior compression capabilities with minimal loss of accuracy compared to traditional matrix methods. Under the star-M tensor framework, tensors can be decomposed in a matrix-mimetic way, including using the star-M SVD. This tensor SVD has optimality guarantees and has shown exceptional performance on specific types of data, but software implementations have been mostly limited to productivity-oriented languages. In this work, we present our development of a shared-memory parallel, high-performance solution designed to efficiently implement the underlying algorithms. This software will enable optimal compression of extensive scientific datasets, paving the way for enhanced data analysis and insights.

</details>


### [193] [An efficient multi-GPU implementation for the Discontinuous Galerkin ocean model SLIM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16082)
*Miguel De Le Court,Vincent Legat,Ange P. Ishimwe,Colin Scherpereel,Emmanuel Hanert,Jonathan Lambrechts*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本研究开发了针对GPU架构优化的3D DG-FE海洋模型，实现了大规模并行计算，突破了传统方法在沿海模拟中的分辨率限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非结构化网格海洋模型虽能灵活处理复杂海岸几何并实现局部网格加密，但其计算成本过高，尤其是DG-FE方法因自由度数量大而受限；GPU高性能计算的兴起为解决该瓶颈提供了新途径。

Method: 采用完整3D DG-FE方法实现，优化了内存布局、内核级并行化，并采用无矩阵求解器处理关键垂直过程；代码支持NVIDIA和AMD GPU架构的单卡及多卡优化。

Result: 单块NVIDIA A100 GPU性能相当于约1500个CPU核心；4块A100节点相比128核CPU节点加速约50倍；弱扩展性可延伸至1024块GPU；大堡礁实际应用实现了比现有最精确模型高5倍的空间分辨率，且物理时间步与数值时间步之比维持100。

Conclusion: GPU加速的DG-FE方法能够显著提升非结构化网格海洋模型的计算能力，使此前不可行的超高分辨率沿海模拟成为可能，为海洋数值模拟领域带来突破性进展。

Abstract: Unstructured-mesh ocean models are increasingly used for coastal applications due to their ability to represent complex geometries and apply local grid refinement where needed. However, their broader use has been hindered by their high computational cost, particularly for models based on the Discontinuous Galerkin finite element (DG-FE) method, which involves significantly more degrees of freedom than traditional finite volume or continuous finite element approaches. The rapid emergence of GPU-based high-performance computing architectures now offers a pathway to address this limitation, as DG-FE formulations are inherently well suited to massively parallel, element-wise computations. Here, we present a full 3D DG-FE ocean model implementation optimized for both single- and multi-GPU systems, with support for both NVIDIA and AMD architectures. We detail the computational strategies employed to achieve high performance, including memory layout optimization, kernel-level parallelization, and matrix-free solvers for key vertical processes. Benchmark results demonstrate that a single HPC-grade GPU (e.g. NVIDIA A100) delivers performance equivalent to approximately 1500 CPU cores, while replacing a 128-core CPU node with a 4xA100 GPU node yields a speedup of around 50x. Weak-scaling efficiency is maintained up to 1024 GPUs. We further demonstrate the model's capabilities on a real-world application in the Great Barrier Reef, achieving a spatial resolution five times finer than the most accurate existing model while maintaining a physical-to-numerical time ratio of 100. These results highlight how GPU-accelerated DG-FE methods can dramatically advance the capabilities of unstructured-mesh ocean modeling, enabling ultra-high-resolution coastal simulations that were previously infeasible.

</details>


### [194] [Runtime-Orchestrated Second-Order Optimization for Scalable LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16184)
*Yishun Lu,Junhao Zhang,Zeyu Yang,Wes Armour*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Second-order methods offer an attractive path toward more sample-efficient LLM training, but their practical use is often blocked by the systems cost of maintaining and updating large matrix-based optimizer states. We introduce \textbf{Asteria}, a runtime system designed to remove this bottleneck by separating second-order optimization logic from the critical GPU training path. Rather than keeping all preconditioner state on the accelerator, Asteria dynamically distributes optimizer state across GPU memory, CPU memory, and optional NVMe storage according to architectural constraints and runtime pressure. It further uses training hooks to prepare shadow states in advance, allowing expensive inverse-root computations to proceed asynchronously on the host while GPU computation continues. For distributed training, Asteria employs a bounded-staleness protocol that limits synchronization frequency while preserving optimizer effectiveness through topology-aware coordination.
  We evaluate Asteria on both memory-constrained and distributed training settings. On a DGX Spark platform with a single GB10 GPU and 128GB unified memory, Asteria supports second-order training for a 1B-parameter language model. On multi-node GH200 systems, it lowers visible optimizer overhead, reduces recurring latency spikes, accelerates convergence in wall-clock time, and maintains the optimization advantages of SOAP and KL-Shampoo in a 7B-parameter language model. Our results suggest that second-order LLM training can be made practical not by simplifying the optimizer alone, but by rethinking how optimizer state, background computation, and distributed synchronization are managed at the runtime level.

</details>


### [195] [Designing Datacenter Power Delivery Hierarchies for the AI Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16255)
*Grant Wilkins,Fiodar Kazhamiaka,Alok Gautam Kumbhare,Chaojie Zhang,Ricardo Bianchini*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Demand for AI accelerators is rapidly increasing rack power density, with projections approaching 1MW per deployment by 2027. This poses a major challenge for datacenter power delivery designers. As power densities increase, a datacenter designed for a different target density may strand power, i.e., may be unable to use all the power that its delivery hierarchy has provisioned. Designs must remain efficient over long datacenter lifetimes and multiple hardware generations. Power utilization is particularly important as grid power capacity is a scarce resource in the AI era.
  Designing an efficient power delivery hierarchy for the long run is difficult because rack placement feasibility, workload impact, and cost depend jointly on electrical topology, deployment granularity, placement policy, power oversubscription, and workload mix. Moreover, each of these factors evolve over time, have inter-dependencies across multiple resource dimensions, and generally do not lend themselves to closed-form analysis.
  To address this challenge, we develop a framework for evaluating datacenter power delivery designs using throughput, power, and cost metrics over realistic arrival, oversubscription, and decommissioning sequences. The framework combines projection models for GPU, compute, and storage deployments with operational factors grounded in production data from Microsoft Azure. Our results show that multi-resource stranding materially changes deployable capacity, effective capital expenditure, and delivered performance, and quantify how rising density from rack- and pod-scale AI systems shapes these outcomes. For AI datacenter design, the relevant planning objective is not installed megawatts, but deployable capacity over time.

</details>
